        slotStatuses         = {},\
        StatusLbl            = nil,\
        BossStatusLbl        = nil,\
        SlotLabels           = {},\
    \
        -- Event carry session state. This is separate from Runtime.carryEscapeActive\
        -- so the Rift farm can never be blocked by the main Auto Farm state.\
        carryHandling        = false,\
        carryCategory        = nil,\
        carryBeforeEggCount  = nil,\
        carrySession         = 0,\
        lastCarryResult      = nil,\
    \
        -- Rift reward lifecycle state.\
        pendingRewardSince   = nil,\
        lastRiftState        = nil,\
    \
        -- Fuse-start safety.\
        -- SAFEZONE startup is owned ONLY by the OFF -> ON session transition.\
        -- The scanner is NEVER allowed to trigger this movement.\
        fuseStartSafezoneDone     = false, -- opening pass consumed for current session\
        fuseStartSafezoneComplete = false, -- opening walk finished; scanner may run\
        fuseSessionId             = 0,\
    \
        -- Treadmill safety ONLY while Fuse scanner is idle/not walking.\
        -- This must never interfere with the proven outbound egg WalkToTarget.\
        lastFuseTreadmillReleaseAt = 0,\
    \
        -- Lightweight treadmill release watcher for the Fuse Event.\
        fuseTreadmillWatcherRunning = false,\
    }\
    \
    -- Boss target state is intentionally character-scoped.\
    -- A respawn invalidates every previous beam/target so old debris can never\
    -- become the active target for the new character.\
    local BossRuntime = {\
        character            = LP.Character,\
        generation           = 0,\
        respawnGraceUntil    = 0,\
        fallbackBlockedUntil = 0,\
        lastTarget           = nil,\
        lastTargetKind       = nil,\
    \
        -- Boss-session state.\
        arena                 = nil,\
        bossModel             = nil,\
        destroyedCrystals     = {}, -- [CrystalTower] = true; NEVER target again this boss\
        shopVisited           = false,\
        exitingToLobby        = false,\
        waitForNextSpawn      = false,\
        sawClosedAfterExit    = false,\
        lastAttackAt          = 0,\
        lastWearBatAt         = 0,\
    \
        -- Stable Phase-2 route state.\
        handCheckpoint        = nil,\
        handCheckpointTower   = nil,\
    \
        -- Post-defeat state.\
        shopDwellSince        = nil,\
        exitPortalTarget      = nil,\
    \
        -- Exact TutorialBeam state.\
        lastArrowSeenAt       = 0,\
        lastArrowPosition     = nil,\
        lastArrowKind         = nil,\
    \
        -- Robust bat cache. The actual field bat does not have to contain\
        -- the literal word \"bat\" in its Tool.Name.\
        cachedBatTool         = nil,\
    \
        -- Post-defeat arrow -> shop -> return-lobby state.\
        shopApproachDone      = false,\
    \
        -- V2.3.5.2 source-confirmed boss state.\
        state                 = \"Idle\",\
        currentCrystal        = nil,\
        batCategory           = nil,\
        batProbeIndex         = 1,\
        batProbeBlockedUntil  = 0,\
        attackSequence        = 0,\
        handBeamMissingSince  = nil,\
        shopArrivedAt         = nil,\
        entryZoneName         = nil,\
    \
        -- Boss-only gate lifecycle:\
        -- keep the completed gate from immediately stealing movement back from Fuse.\
        activeEntranceGate    = nil,\
        completedEntranceGate = nil,\
    \
        -- Phase 1 basic attack loop.\
        -- Runs independently from crystal replication/target movement.\
        phase1AttackRunning   = false,\
        phase1AttackGeneration = 0,\
    }\
    \
    local function resetBossTargetState(char, isRespawn)\
        BossRuntime.character = char\
        BossRuntime.generation = BossRuntime.generation + 1\
        BossRuntime.lastTarget = nil\
        BossRuntime.lastTargetKind = nil\
        BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
        BossRuntime.cachedBatTool = nil\
        BossRuntime.handCheckpoint = nil\
        BossRuntime.handCheckpointTower = nil\
        BossRuntime.handBeamMissingSince = nil\
    \
        -- Invalidate any Phase-1 attack coroutine belonging to the old character.\
        BossRuntime.phase1AttackGeneration =\
            (BossRuntime.phase1AttackGeneration or 0) + 1\
        BossRuntime.phase1AttackRunning = false\
    \
        local now = tick()\
        if isRespawn then\
            BossRuntime.respawnGraceUntil = now + 1.25\
            BossRuntime.fallbackBlockedUntil = now + 2.50\
        else\
            BossRuntime.respawnGraceUntil = now\
            BossRuntime.fallbackBlockedUntil = now\
        end\
    \
        task.spawn(function()\
            if not char then return end\
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"Humanoid\") or char:WaitForChild(\"Humanoid\", 5)\
            if not hum then return end\
    \
            local diedConn\
            diedConn = hum.Died:Connect(function()\
                -- Do not retain a crystal/hand from the dead character.\
                BossRuntime.lastTarget = nil\
                BossRuntime.lastTargetKind = nil\
                BossRuntime.respawnGraceUntil = tick() + 3\
                BossRuntime.fallbackBlockedUntil = tick() + 3\
            end)\
            table.insert(ScriptConnections, diedConn)\
        end)\
    end\
    \
    resetBossTargetState(LP.Character, false)\
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)\
        resetBossTargetState(char, true)\
    end)\
    \
    local function UpdateEventStatus(txt)\
        if EventRuntime.StatusLbl then\
            EventRuntime.StatusLbl.Text = \"Fuse Event: \" .. tostring(txt)\
        end\
    end\
    \
    local function UpdateBossStatus(txt)\
        if EventRuntime.BossStatusLbl then\
            EventRuntime.BossStatusLbl.Text = \"Boss Event: \" .. tostring(txt)\
        end\
    end\
    \
    -- ============================================================\
    -- [RIFT FUSE START SAFETY]\
    -- SAFEZONE_POS is already a saved Vector3 coordinate.\
    -- There is intentionally NO Workspace Safezone Part lookup here.\
    -- ============================================================\
    local function startFuseTreadmillSafetyWatcher()\
        -- One watchdog for the whole Auto Fuse ON session.\
        -- It does NOT own movement and never sends the player to Safezone.\
        -- It only releases an actually-stalled treadmill capture, then the current\
        -- WalkToTarget / Fuse / Boss sub-engine continues its own route normally.\
        if EventRuntime.fuseTreadmillWatcherRunning then\
            return\
        end\
    \
        EventRuntime.fuseTreadmillWatcherRunning = true\
    \
        task.spawn(function()\
            local lastPos = nil\
            local lastProgressAt = tick()\
            local treadmillNearSince = nil\
    \
            while EventCfg.autoFuseEvent do\
                local root = getRoot()\
                local hum = getHumanoid()\
    \
                if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then\
                    lastPos = nil\
                    treadmillNearSince = nil\
                    lastProgressAt = tick()\
                    task.wait(0.15)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                -- Track REAL character progress independently from whichever Fuse/Boss\
                -- engine currently owns Humanoid:MoveTo().\
                if lastPos then\
                    local moved = (root.Position - lastPos).Magnitude\
                    if moved >= 0.75 then\
                        lastProgressAt = tick()\
                    end\
                else\
                    lastProgressAt = tick()\
                end\
                lastPos = root.Position\
    \
                local onTreadmill = false\
                local treadmillPart = nil\
    \
                pcall(function()\
                    local yes, tb = isPlayerOnTreadmill()\
                    onTreadmill = yes == true\
                    treadmillPart = tb\
                end)\
    \
                if onTreadmill then\
                    treadmillNearSince = treadmillNearSince or tick()\
    \
                    local nearTime = tick() - treadmillNearSince\
                    local stalledFor = tick() - lastProgressAt\
                    local distToTreadmill = math.huge\
    \
                    if treadmillPart and treadmillPart:IsA(\"BasePart\") then\
                        distToTreadmill = (root.Position - treadmillPart.Position).Magnitude\
                    end\
    \
                    -- Important anti-false-positive rule:\
                    -- isPlayerOnTreadmill() is proximity-based (<=12 studs), so merely\
                    -- walking past a treadmill must NOT trigger release.\
                    --\
                    -- Release only when:\
                    --   * Auto Fuse is ON,\
                    --   * player has remained in the treadmill zone,\
                    --   * character has made almost no position progress for ~0.55s,\
                    --   * and release cooldown has expired.\
                    local trulyStuck =\
                        nearTime >= 0.40\
                        and stalledFor >= 0.55\
                        and distToTreadmill <= 12\
    \
                    if trulyStuck\
                        and tick() - (EventRuntime.lastFuseTreadmillReleaseAt or 0) >= 0.85\
                    then\
                        EventRuntime.lastFuseTreadmillReleaseAt = tick()\
    \
                        UpdateEventStatus(\"Treadmill STUCK detected -> releasing...\")\
                        releaseTreadmill()\
    \
                        -- Give AskDoff / presentation controller / jump impulse time\
                        -- to detach before another check. The active movement engine\
                        -- will naturally re-issue its own MoveTo target.\
                        task.wait(0.18)\
    \
                        local freshRoot = getRoot()\
                        lastPos = freshRoot and freshRoot.Position or nil\
                        lastProgressAt = tick()\
                        treadmillNearSince = nil\
                    end\
                else\
                    treadmillNearSince = nil\
    \
                    -- While away from treadmill, being stationary is perfectly valid\
                    -- (scanner wait, Rift reveal, Boss countdown, etc.).\
                    lastProgressAt = tick()\
                end\
    \
                task.wait(0.12)\
            end\
    \
            EventRuntime.fuseTreadmillWatcherRunning = false\
        end)\
    end\
    \
    local function runFuseStartSafezoneOnce(sessionId)\
        if not EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then return false end\
    \
        -- This function is called ONLY by the session starter, never by the scanner.\
        if EventRuntime.fuseStartSafezoneDone then\
            return EventRuntime.fuseStartSafezoneComplete == true\
        end\
    \
        EventRuntime.fuseStartSafezoneDone = true\
        EventRuntime.fuseStartSafezoneComplete = false\
    \
        local mySession = sessionId or EventRuntime.fuseSessionId\
    \
        UpdateEventStatus(\"Starting Fuse Event -> walking to Safezone ONCE...\")\
    \
        -- One opening treadmill release is fine; after this, scanner movement never\
        -- owns Safezone and outbound egg walking keeps its own target.\
        local onTreadmill = false\
        pcall(function()\
            onTreadmill = isPlayerOnTreadmill() == true\
        end)\
        if onTreadmill then\
            releaseTreadmill()\
            task.wait(0.12)\
        end\
    \
        ensureCharacter(5)\
    \
        pcall(function()\
            WalkToTarget(\
                SAFEZONE_POS,\
                7,\
                false,\
                function()\
                    return EventCfg.autoFuseEvent\
                        and EventRuntime.fuseSessionId == mySession\
                end\
            )\
        end)\
    \
        -- Ignore stale completion from an old ON session.\
        if EventRuntime.fuseSessionId ~= mySession or not EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then\
            return false\
        end\
    \
        EventRuntime.fuseStartSafezoneComplete = true\
        UpdateEventStatus(\"Initial Safezone pass complete -> scanner unlocked\")\
        return true\
    end\
    \
    local function beginFuseEventSession()\
        if not EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then return end\
    \
        EventRuntime.fuseSessionId = EventRuntime.fuseSessionId + 1\
        local mySession = EventRuntime.fuseSessionId\
    \
        EventRuntime.fuseStartSafezoneDone = false\
        EventRuntime.fuseStartSafezoneComplete = false\
    \
        -- Auto Fuse ON = treadmill protection remains active regardless of which\
        -- current sub-engine is running (scanner, egg route, carry, boss handoff, etc.).\
        startFuseTreadmillSafetyWatcher()\
    \
        task.spawn(function()\
            runFuseStartSafezoneOnce(mySession)\
    \
            -- If the coordinator was not already alive (for example Auto Boss OFF),\
            -- start it only after the one-time opening route has been consumed.\
            if EventCfg.autoFuseEvent\
                and EventRuntime.fuseSessionId == mySession\
                and not EventRuntime.running\
            then\
                task.spawn(doEventMasterLoop)\
            end\
        end)\
    end\
    \
    -- ============================================================\
    -- [BOSS FIELD BAT — SOURCE-CONFIRMED]\
    -- AskWearFieldBat REQUIRES one area-category string.\
    -- Successful server call parents a Tool with IsBat=true directly to Character.\
    -- ============================================================\
    local BOSS_BAT_CATEGORY_CANDIDATES = {\
        \"Prehistoric\",      -- runtime-confirmed working category/tool on test account\
        \"Titan Temple\",\
        \"Cherry Blossom\",\
        \"Cosmic\",\
        \"Abyss Ocean\",\
        \"Volcano\",\
        \"Snow\",\
        \"Jungle\",\
        \"Desert\",\
        \"Lake\",\
        \"Forest\",\
    }\
    \
    local function getEquippedBossBat()\
        local char = LP.Character\
        if not char then return nil end\
    \
        for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do\
            if tool:IsA(\"Tool\") and tool:GetAttribute(\"IsBat\") == true then\
                BossRuntime.cachedBatTool = tool\
    \
                local gearName = tostring(tool:GetAttribute(\"GearName\") or \"\")\
                local category = gearName:match(\"^(.-)%s+Bat$\")\
                if category and category ~= \"\" then\
                    BossRuntime.batCategory = category\
                end\
    \
                return tool\
            end\
        end\
    \
        BossRuntime.cachedBatTool = nil\
        return nil\
    end\
    \
    local function getAnyOwnedBossBat()\
        local equipped = getEquippedBossBat()\
        if equipped then return equipped end\
    \
        local bp = LP:FindFirstChild(\"Backpack\")\
        if bp then\
            for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do\
                if tool:IsA(\"Tool\") and tool:GetAttribute(\"IsBat\") == true then\
                    return tool\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        return nil\
    end\
    \
    local function buildBatCategoryProbeList()\
        local result = {}\
        local seen = {}\
    \
        local function push(v)\
            v = tostring(v or \"\")\
            if v ~= \"\" and not seen[v] then\
                seen[v] = true\
                table.insert(result, v)\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- Previously proven category first.\
        push(BossRuntime.batCategory)\
    \
        -- The random entrance area is a valid Codex-style area category candidate.\
        push(BossRuntime.entryZoneName)\
    \
        -- Runtime-confirmed working test category, then the remaining area categories.\
        for _, category in ipairs(BOSS_BAT_CATEGORY_CANDIDATES) do\
            push(category)\
        end\
    \
        return result\
    end\
    \
    local function ensureBossBatEquipped()\
        local char = LP.Character\
        local hum = getHumanoid()\
        if not char or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return nil end\
    \
        -- Official successful state: IsBat Tool is parented directly to Character.\
        local equipped = getEquippedBossBat()\
        if equipped then return equipped end\
    \
        -- Recovery only: if a valid bat somehow ended up in Backpack, re-equip it.\
        local owned = getAnyOwnedBossBat()\
        if owned and owned.Parent ~= char then\
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(owned) end)\
            task.wait(0.04)\
            equipped = getEquippedBossBat()\
            if equipped then return equipped end\
        end\
    \
        if not RMT.BossWearFieldBat then return nil end\
        if tick() < (BossRuntime.batProbeBlockedUntil or 0) then return nil end\
    \
        local candidates = buildBatCategoryProbeList()\
        if #candidates == 0 then return nil end\
    \
        local idx = math.clamp(BossRuntime.batProbeIndex or 1, 1, #candidates)\
        local category = candidates[idx]\
    \
        BossRuntime.lastWearBatAt = tick()\
    \
        local okCall, success, errMsg = pcall(function()\
            return RMT.BossWearFieldBat:InvokeServer(category)\
        end)\
    \
        if okCall and success == true then\
            BossRuntime.batCategory = category\
            BossRuntime.batProbeIndex = 1\
            BossRuntime.batProbeBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.30\
    \
            local deadline = tick() + 1.25\
            while EventCfg.autoBoss and tick() < deadline do\
                equipped = getEquippedBossBat()\
                if equipped then return equipped end\
                task.wait(0.05)\
            end\
        else\
            BossRuntime.batProbeIndex = idx + 1\
            if BossRuntime.batProbeIndex > #candidates then\
                BossRuntime.batProbeIndex = 1\
                BossRuntime.batProbeBlockedUntil = tick() + 1.25\
            else\
                BossRuntime.batProbeBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.18\
            end\
        end\
    \
        return getEquippedBossBat()\
    end\
    \
    local function isPlayerInBossArena()\
        if LP:GetAttribute(\"InBossArena\") == true then return true end\
        local arena = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"BossArena\")\
        if arena then\
            local floor = arena:FindFirstChild(\"Floor\")\
            local root = getRoot()\
            if root and floor and (root.Position - floor.Position).Magnitude <= 350 then\
                return true\
            end\
        end\
        return false\
    end\
    \
    -- BossSnapshot.Open only says the boss EVENT WINDOW is active.\
    -- It does NOT mean the entrance gate exists at the main Rift/Fuse crystal.\
    --\
    -- The real entrance is a separately spawned workspace.BossArenaTeleport that\
    -- appears around one of the normal egg-spawn areas. Auto Boss must not take\
    -- movement ownership until this real world object exists.\
    local function getBossTeleportPart(gate)\
        if not gate then return nil end\
        if gate:IsA(\"BasePart\") then return gate end\
    \
        local p = gate.PrimaryPart\
            or gate:FindFirstChild(\"Hitbox\", true)\
            or gate:FindFirstChild(\"Zone\", true)\
            or gate:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(\"BasePart\", true)\
    \
        return p and p:IsA(\"BasePart\") and p or nil\
    end\
    \
    local function findSpawnedBossEntranceGate()\
        if isPlayerInBossArena() then return nil, nil end\
    \
        -- IMPORTANT: exact BossArenaTeleport only.\
        -- Never substitute \"The Rift\", RiftTradeIn objects, or SAFEZONE_POS.\
        local gate = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"BossArenaTeleport\")\
    \
        -- The entrance from a boss that was JUST completed can remain in Workspace\
        -- briefly after returning to Lobby. Ignore ONLY that exact Instance.\
        local completedGate = BossRuntime.completedEntranceGate\
        if completedGate then\
            if not completedGate.Parent then\
                BossRuntime.completedEntranceGate = nil\
                completedGate = nil\
            elseif gate == completedGate then\
                return nil, nil\
            elseif gate and gate ~= completedGate then\
                -- Different Instance = genuinely new random boss gate.\
                BossRuntime.completedEntranceGate = nil\
                completedGate = nil\
            end\
        end\
    \
        if not gate then\
            return nil, nil\
        end\
    \
        local part = getBossTeleportPart(gate)\
        if not part then\
            return nil, nil\
        end\
    \
        return gate, part\
    end\
    \
    local function getNearestBossGateZoneName(pos)\
        if not pos then return \"Unknown Area\" end\
    \
        local bestName = nil\
        local bestDist = math.huge\
        for zoneName, zonePos in pairs(ZONES) do\
            local d = (Vector3.new(pos.X, 0, pos.Z) - Vector3.new(zonePos.X, 0, zonePos.Z)).Magnitude\
            if d < bestDist then\
                bestDist = d\
                bestName = zoneName\
            end\
        end\
    \
        return bestName or \"Unknown Area\"\
    end\
    \
    local function checkBossGateOpen()\
        if isPlayerInBossArena() then return true end\
        local gate, part = findSpawnedBossEntranceGate()\
        return gate ~= nil and part ~= nil\
    end\
    \
    local function askRiftState()\
        local state = nil\
        if RMT.RiftAskState then\
            local ok, res = pcall(function()\
                return RMT.RiftAskState:InvokeServer()\
            end)\
            if ok and type(res) == \"table\" then\
                state = res\
            end\
        end\
        EventRuntime.lastRiftState = state\
        return state\
    end\
    \
    local function fetchRiftRequirements()\
        local reqs = {}\
        local state = askRiftState()\
    \
        if state and type(state.Requirements) == \"table\" then\
            reqs = state.Requirements\
        end\
    \
        -- Save fallback is read-only. IMPORTANT: do not call AskFinishReveal here.\
        -- The official client only finishes the reveal after the Rift sequence window.\
        if #reqs == 0 then\
            pcall(function()\
                local Save = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save).Get()\
                if Save and Save.Rift and type(Save.Rift.Requirements) == \"table\" then\
                    reqs = Save.Rift.Requirements\
                end\
            end)\
        end\
    \
        EventRuntime.requirements = reqs\
        return reqs, state\
    end\
    \
    local function resetRiftCycleRuntime()\
        EventRuntime.requirements = {}\
        EventRuntime.petStatuses = {}\
        EventRuntime.slotStatuses = {}\
        EventRuntime.pendingRewardSince = nil\
    \
        for idx, lbl in pairs(EventRuntime.SlotLabels) do\
            if lbl then\
                pcall(function()\
                    lbl.Text = string.format(\"[ ] Slot %d (Scanning...)\", tonumber(idx) or 0)\
                    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(237, 232, 255)\
                end)\
            end\
        end\
    end\
    \
    -- Official client flow:\
    -- AskTradeIn -> AskState -> RiftSequence (~5-6s) -> AskFinishReveal -> AskState.\
    -- IMPORTANT: a Rift cycle is NOT considered finished until the reward egg is\
    -- actually visible in Save.EggInventory. PendingReward=false alone is not enough.\
    local function snapshotOwnedEggUids()\
        local set = {}\
        pcall(function()\
            local Save = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save).Get()\
            local eggInv = Save and Save.EggInventory or {}\
            for uid, _ in pairs(eggInv) do\
                set[tostring(uid)] = true\
            end\
        end)\
        return set\
    end\
    \
    local function findNewOwnedEggUid(beforeSet)\
        local newUid = nil\
        pcall(function()\
            local Save = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save).Get()\
            local eggInv = Save and Save.EggInventory or {}\
            for uid, _ in pairs(eggInv) do\
                uid = tostring(uid)\
                if not beforeSet or not beforeSet[uid] then\
                    newUid = uid\
                    break\
                end\
            end\
        end)\
        return newUid\
    end\
    \
    local function waitForNewOwnedEgg(beforeSet, timeout)\
        timeout = timeout or 2.5\
        local deadline = tick() + timeout\
        while EventCfg.autoFuseEvent and tick() < deadline do\
            local uid = findNewOwnedEggUid(beforeSet)\
            if uid then return true, uid end\
            task.wait(0.08)\
        end\
        local uid = findNewOwnedEggUid(beforeSet)\
        return uid ~= nil, uid\
    end\
    \
    local function claimPendingRiftReward(waitForOfficialRevealWindow, beforeRewardEggs)\
        if not RMT.RiftAskFinishReveal then\
            return false, askRiftState(), nil\
        end\
    \
        -- For a normal post-trade claim this snapshot is passed from BEFORE AskTradeIn.\
        -- For a pending reward recovered after reload, snapshot right before claiming.\
        beforeRewardEggs = beforeRewardEggs or snapshotOwnedEggUids()\
    \
        local state = askRiftState()\
    \
        -- Do NOT abort only because PendingReward is momentarily false. The official\
        -- client still reaches AskFinishReveal from the RiftSequence callback, and\
        -- state replication can race the local event loop.\
        local notBefore = tick()\
        if waitForOfficialRevealWindow then\
            local base = EventRuntime.pendingRewardSince or tick()\
            -- Reverse-engineering showed ~5-6 seconds. Use a slightly safer boundary.\
            notBefore = base + 6.10\
        end\
    \
        while EventCfg.autoFuseEvent and tick() < notBefore do\
            local remain = math.max(0, notBefore - tick())\
            UpdateEventStatus(string.format('Reward reveal animation... %.1fs', remain))\
            task.wait(math.min(0.20, remain))\
        end\
    \
        if not EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then\
            return false, state, nil\
        end\
    \
        -- The manual/official claim action is exactly AskFinishReveal() with NO args.\
        -- Keep pressing that server-side claim until the reward egg really appears.\
        local deadline = tick() + 10.0\
        local attempt = 0\
        while EventCfg.autoFuseEvent and tick() < deadline do\
            attempt = attempt + 1\
            UpdateEventStatus(string.format('Claiming Rift Egg automatically... [%d]', attempt))\
    \
            pcall(function()\
                RMT.RiftAskFinishReveal:InvokeServer()\
            end)\
    \
            -- Reward replication is the authoritative completion signal for this engine.\
            local gotEgg, rewardUid = waitForNewOwnedEgg(beforeRewardEggs, 0.90)\
            state = askRiftState()\
    \
            if gotEgg then\
                EventRuntime.pendingRewardSince = nil\
                return true, state, rewardUid\
            end\
    \
            -- If server says PendingReward cleared, give Save replication an extra window,\
            -- but DO NOT reset the Rift engine until the actual reward egg is present.\
            if state and state.PendingReward == false then\
                gotEgg, rewardUid = waitForNewOwnedEgg(beforeRewardEggs, 1.50)\
                if gotEgg then\
                    EventRuntime.pendingRewardSince = nil\
                    return true, state, rewardUid\
                end\
            end\
    \
            task.wait(0.25)\
        end\
    \
        return false, state, nil\
    end\
    \
    local function refreshPetStatuses()\
        local reqs = fetchRiftRequirements()\
        local Save = nil\
        pcall(function() Save = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save).Get() end)\
    \
        local inv = Save and Save.Inventory or {}\
        local eggInv = Save and Save.EggInventory or {}\
    \
        -- IMPORTANT: status is indexed by requirement SLOT, not by pet name.\
        -- This prevents one pet/egg from satisfying two equal requirements.\
        local slotStatuses = {}\
        local readyCount = 0\
        local usedPetUids = {}\
        local usedEggUids = {}\
    \
        for idx, petName in ipairs(reqs) do\
            local st = {\
                slotIndex = idx,\
                category = petName,\
                ready = false,\
                status = \"Need to Farm Egg\",\
                uid = nil,\
                eggUid = nil,\
            }\
    \
            -- 1) Prefer an already-hatched spare pet.\
            for uid, petData in pairs(inv) do\
                if not usedPetUids[uid]\
                    and not petData.InFuse\
                    and petData.Category == petName\
                then\
                    st.ready = true\
                    st.status = \"Ready\"\
                    st.uid = uid\
                    usedPetUids[uid] = true\
                    readyCount = readyCount + 1\
                    break\
                end\
            end\
    \
            -- 2) If no spare pet exists, reserve one unique egg for this slot.\
            if not st.ready then\
                for eggUid, eggData in pairs(eggInv) do\
                    if not usedEggUids[eggUid] and eggData.AssetCategory == petName then\
                        usedEggUids[eggUid] = true\
                        st.eggUid = eggUid\
                        if eggData.Placement then\
                            st.status = \"Hatching in Plot\"\
                        else\
                            st.status = \"Egg in Backpack\"\
                        end\
                        break\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            slotStatuses[idx] = st\
    \
            if EventRuntime.SlotLabels[idx] then\
                local slot = EventRuntime.SlotLabels[idx]\
                if st.ready then\
                    slot.Text = string.format(\"[✓] %s (Ready)\", petName)\
                    slot.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120)\
                else\
                    slot.Text = string.format(\"[ ] %s (%s)\", petName, st.status)\
                    slot.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(237, 232, 255)\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        EventRuntime.slotStatuses = slotStatuses\
        EventRuntime.petStatuses = slotStatuses\
        return slotStatuses, readyCount, #reqs\
    end\
    \
    -- Otomatis klik tombol \"Add\" di UI The Rift dan pilih pet-nya\
    local function _resolveGuiButton(inst, stopAt)\
        if not inst then return nil end\
        if inst:IsA(\"GuiButton\") then return inst end\
    \
        for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do\
            if d:IsA(\"GuiButton\") then return d end\
        end\
    \
        local cur = inst.Parent\
        while cur and cur ~= stopAt do\
            if cur:IsA(\"GuiButton\") then return cur end\
            cur = cur.Parent\
        end\
        return nil\
    end\
    \
    local function _fireActivated(btn)\
        if not btn or not btn:IsA(\"GuiButton\") then return false end\
        local ok = pcall(function()\
            if type(firesignal) == \"function\" then\
                firesignal(btn.Activated)\
            else\
                error(\"firesignal unavailable\")\
            end\
        end)\
        return ok\
    end\
    \
    -- Automatically fills the exact Rift slot with the exact pet UID.\
    -- The decompiled picker names entries \"Pet_<Uid>\", so we search the whole\
    -- RiftTradeIn ScreenGui instead of assuming a non-existent inventory frame.\
    local function autoAddPetToRiftSlot(slotIndex, petCategory, petUid)\
        local pg = LP:FindFirstChild(\"PlayerGui\")\
        local rti = pg and pg:FindFirstChild(\"RiftTradeIn\")\
        local main = rti and rti:FindFirstChild(\"RiftTradeInMain\")\
        if not main then return false end\
    \
        local inputs = main:FindFirstChild(\"SacrificeInputs\")\
        local inputFrame = inputs and inputs:FindFirstChild(\"Input\" .. tostring(slotIndex))\
        if not inputFrame then return false end\
    \
        local full = inputFrame:FindFirstChild(\"Full\")\
        if full and full.Visible then return true end\
    \
        local empty = inputFrame:FindFirstChild(\"Empty\")\
        local addBtn = empty and empty:FindFirstChild(\"Add\")\
        if not addBtn then return false end\
    \
        _fireActivated(addBtn)\
        task.wait(0.18)\
    \
        local pickedButton = nil\
    \
        -- Best path: exact UID generated by the game's picker.\
        if petUid then\
            local exact = rti:FindFirstChild(\"Pet_\" .. tostring(petUid), true)\
            pickedButton = _resolveGuiButton(exact, rti)\
        end\
    \
        -- Fallback: find a picker entry whose text exactly matches the required pet.\
        if not pickedButton then\
            local wanted = tostring(petCategory or \"\"):lower():gsub(\"^%s+\", \"\"):gsub(\"%s+$\", \"\")\
            for _, obj in ipairs(rti:GetDescendants()) do\
                if (obj:IsA(\"TextLabel\") or obj:IsA(\"TextButton\")) and obj.Visible then\
                    local txt = tostring(obj.Text or \"\"):lower():gsub(\"^%s+\", \"\"):gsub(\"%s+$\", \"\")\
                    if txt == wanted then\
                        local btn = _resolveGuiButton(obj, rti)\
                        if btn and btn ~= addBtn then\
                            pickedButton = btn\
                            break\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        if not pickedButton then\
            return false\
        end\
    \
        _fireActivated(pickedButton)\
    \
        local deadline = tick() + 1.25\
        while tick() < deadline do\
            if full and full.Visible then return true end\
            task.wait(0.05)\
        end\
    \
        return full and full.Visible or false\
    end\
    \
    -- Farm satu telur spesifik dengan carry escape & anti-boss persis farm utama\
    local function countOwnedEggCategory(category)\
        local Save = nil\
        pcall(function() Save = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save).Get() end)\
        local eggInv = Save and Save.EggInventory or {}\
        local count = 0\
        for _, eggData in pairs(eggInv) do\
            if eggData.AssetCategory == category then\
                count = count + 1\
            end\
        end\
        return count\
    end\
    \
    local function waitForOwnedEggCategoryIncrease(category, beforeCount, timeout)\
        timeout = timeout or 3.0\
        local deadline = tick() + timeout\
        while EventCfg.autoFuseEvent and tick() < deadline do\
            if countOwnedEggCategory(category) > (beforeCount or 0) then\
                return true\
            end\
            task.wait(0.08)\
        end\
        return countOwnedEggCategory(category) > (beforeCount or 0)\
    end\
    \
    -- Event-safe version of the SAME carry pipeline used by the main farm:\
    -- carry confirmed -> Anti Boss spam (if enabled) -> walk Safezone -> unequip ->\
    -- wait until EggInventory actually receives the egg.\
    local function eventCarryEscapeToSafezone(category, beforeEggCount)\
        Runtime.steals = Runtime.steals + 1\
        ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
        ScanCache.eggs = {}\
    \
        if Cfg.antiBoss then\
            UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\"Carry confirmed → Anti Boss → Safezone [%d]\", Runtime.steals))\
            triggerAntiBossSpamTeleport()\
            waitForAntiBossTeleport()\
        else\
            UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\"Carry confirmed → Safezone [%d]\", Runtime.steals))\
        end\
    \
        -- Carry/deposit ALWAYS has priority. Do not cancel this walk just because\
        -- the boss gate opened while the player was already carrying an egg.\
        local function carryAlive()\
            return EventCfg.autoFuseEvent\
        end\
    \
        local reached = WalkToTarget(SAFEZONE_POS, 5, false, carryAlive)\
    \
        -- If the character died during the escape, bind the fresh character and retry.\
        if not reached and EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then\
            ensureCharacter(8)\
            if getRoot() and getHumanoid() and getHumanoid().Health > 0 then\
                reached = WalkToTarget(SAFEZONE_POS, 5, false, carryAlive)\
            end\
        end\
    \
        stopAntiBossSpamTeleport()\
        forceUnequipTools()\
        task.wait(0.05)\
        forceUnequipTools()\
    \
        if category and beforeEggCount ~= nil then\
            if waitForOwnedEggCategoryIncrease(category, beforeEggCount, 3.0) then\
                return true\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- Replication fallback: if carry is gone and we reached Safezone, consider\
        -- the deposit phase completed; the next state refresh will verify inventory.\
        local root = getRoot()\
        local inSafezone = root and (root.Position - SAFEZONE_POS).Magnitude <= 12\
        return reached and inSafezone and not isCarryingEgg()\
    end\
    \
    \
    local function handleEventCarryToSafezone(category, beforeEggCount)\
        -- If another watcher already owns this carry, wait for it instead of\
        -- launching a second anti-boss / WalkToTarget sequence.\
        if EventRuntime.carryHandling then\
            local deadline = tick() + 12\
            while EventCfg.autoFuseEvent and EventRuntime.carryHandling and tick() < deadline do\
                task.wait(0.05)\
            end\
            return EventRuntime.lastCarryResult == true\
        end\
    \
        EventRuntime.carryHandling = true\
        EventRuntime.lastCarryResult = false\
    \
        local ok, result = pcall(function()\
            return eventCarryEscapeToSafezone(category, beforeEggCount)\
        end)\
    \
        EventRuntime.lastCarryResult = ok and result == true\
        EventRuntime.carryHandling = false\
    \
        if EventRuntime.lastCarryResult then\
            EventRuntime.carryCategory = nil\
            EventRuntime.carryBeforeEggCount = nil\
        end\
    \
        return EventRuntime.lastCarryResult\
    end\
    \
    -- Event-only smooth route wrapper.\
    -- The old global treadmill watcher used proximity alone and repeatedly called\
    -- releaseTreadmill(), which applies Jump + upward velocity. That made normal\
    -- movement near a plot/treadmill look like stop-go movement.\
    --\
    -- This watchdog ONLY releases the treadmill when BOTH are true:\
    --   1) the character is near a treadmill, AND\
    --   2) the character has actually stalled for a sustained interval.\
    --\
    -- It NEVER changes the destination to Safezone.\
    -- Farm one specific event egg. Optional preselectedEgg avoids scanning twice.\
    -- IMPORTANT: pickup/carry is NOT a separate Rift implementation anymore.\
    -- It calls the SAME attemptPickup() used by the main Auto Farm engine.\
    local function farmSpecificEgg(targetPetCategory, preselectedEgg)\
        local beforeEggCount = countOwnedEggCategory(targetPetCategory)\
    \
        -- If carry already replicated before this step, run the exact proven main-farm escape now.\
        if isCarryingEgg() then\
            UpdateEventStatus(\"Carry already detected -> using MAIN FARM escape engine...\")\
            instantCarryEscape()\
            waitForOwnedEggCategoryIncrease(targetPetCategory, beforeEggCount, 4.0)\
            return countOwnedEggCategory(targetPetCategory) > beforeEggCount or not isCarryingEgg()\
        end\
    \
        local rootPos = getRoot() and getRoot().Position or Vector3.zero\
        local foundEgg = preselectedEgg\
    \
        if not foundEgg then\
            UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\"Scanning field for %s egg...\", targetPetCategory))\
            local minDistance = math.huge\
    \
            pcall(function()\
                local EggState = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)\
                local snap = EggState.ReadFieldEggs()\
                if snap and snap.Records then\
                    for _, rec in ipairs(snap.Records) do\
                        if rec.AssetCategory == targetPetCategory and rec.Uid then\
                            local model = S.Workspace.AreaEggSlotsClient:FindFirstChild(rec.Uid)\
                                or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(rec.Uid)\
                                or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(rec.Uid, true)\
    \
                            local pos = rec.BottomCFrame and rec.BottomCFrame.Position\
                            if model then\
                                local part = model:FindFirstChild(\"Hitbox\")\
                                    or model:FindFirstChild(\"CustomBoundingBox\")\
                                    or model.PrimaryPart\
                                    or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(\"BasePart\")\
                                if part then pos = part.Position end\
                            end\
    \
                            if pos then\
                                local dist = (pos - rootPos).Magnitude\
                                if dist < minDistance then\
                                    minDistance = dist\
                                    foundEgg = {\
                                        uid = rec.Uid,\
                                        model = model,\
                                        pos = pos,\
                                        slotKey = tostring(rec.NestId or rec.Uid),\
                                        category = targetPetCategory,\
                                    }\
                                end\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end)\
    \
            if not foundEgg then\
                for _, child in ipairs(S.Workspace:GetChildren()) do\
                    if isDroppedEggModel(child) then\
                        local cat = tostring(child:GetAttribute(\"AssetCategory\") or child.Name)\
                        if cat:lower():find(targetPetCategory:lower(), 1, true) then\
                            local part = child:FindFirstChild(\"Hitbox\")\
                                or child:FindFirstChild(\"CustomBoundingBox\")\
                                or child.PrimaryPart\
                                or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(\"BasePart\")\
                            if part then\
                                foundEgg = {\
                                    uid = child.Name,\
                                    model = child,\
                                    pos = part.Position,\
                                    slotKey = tostring(child:GetAttribute(\"FirstAreaSlotKey\") or child.Name),\
                                    category = targetPetCategory,\
                                }\
                                break\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        if not foundEgg then\
            UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\"Egg for %s not found. Rescanning...\", targetPetCategory))\
            task.wait(1.0)\
            return false\
        end\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- FUSE EVENT: SAFEZONE STAGE BEFORE WALKING TO LOCKED EGG\
        -- ========================================================\
        -- foundEgg is already selected above. Do NOT rescan or replace it.\
        -- We only insert one Safezone leg before the existing direct egg route.\
        UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\
            \"Required %s detected -> Safezone first...\",\
            targetPetCategory\
        ))\
    \
        local stagedSafezone = WalkToTarget(\
            SAFEZONE_POS,\
            6,\
            false,\
            function()\
                return EventCfg.autoFuseEvent\
                    and not (EventCfg.autoBoss and checkBossGateOpen())\
            end\
        )\
    \
        if not stagedSafezone then\
            return false\
        end\
    \
        local prompt = getEggPrompt(foundEgg.pos)\
        local walkTarget = (prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent:IsA(\"BasePart\"))\
            and prompt.Parent.Position\
            or foundEgg.pos\
    \
        UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\"Walking to required %s egg...\", targetPetCategory))\
        forceUnequipTools()\
    \
        -- PROVEN V2.3.2.1 MOVEMENT:\
        -- Direct WalkToTarget only. No extra Fuse route wrapper/watchdog owns movement.\
        -- Scanner/requirement logic may continue refreshing data, but it cannot issue\
        -- another movement command while this route is running.\
        local reached = WalkToTarget(walkTarget, 4, true, function()\
            return EventCfg.autoFuseEvent\
                and not (EventCfg.autoBoss and checkBossGateOpen())\
        end)\
    \
        if isCarryingEgg() then\
            UpdateEventStatus(\"Carry detected during approach -> MAIN FARM Anti Boss -> Safezone...\")\
            instantCarryEscape()\
            waitForOwnedEggCategoryIncrease(targetPetCategory, beforeEggCount, 4.0)\
            return countOwnedEggCategory(targetPetCategory) > beforeEggCount or not isCarryingEgg()\
        end\
    \
        if not reached then\
            return false\
        end\
    \
        UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\"Stealing required %s with MAIN FARM pickup engine...\", targetPetCategory))\
    \
        -- EXACT SAME pickup engine as Tab Auto. The only difference is the\
        -- continuation flag: Rift Auto Fuse instead of Cfg.autoFarm.\
        local pickedUp = attemptPickup(foundEgg, function()\
            return EventCfg.autoFuseEvent\
                and not (EventCfg.autoBoss and checkBossGateOpen())\
        end)\
    \
        if not pickedUp then\
            UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\"Pickup %s failed -> rescanning...\", targetPetCategory))\
            return false\
        end\
    \
        -- attemptPickup() already performs instantCarryEscape():\
        -- carry confirmed -> Anti Boss -> WalkToTarget(SAFEZONE_POS) -> unequip.\
        UpdateEventStatus(\"Safezone reached -> waiting for required egg in backpack...\")\
        waitForOwnedEggCategoryIncrease(targetPetCategory, beforeEggCount, 4.0)\
    \
        return countOwnedEggCategory(targetPetCategory) > beforeEggCount or not isCarryingEgg()\
    end\
    \
    -- Hatch only eggs that belong to current Rift requirements.\
    -- Event farming may continue collecting other missing eggs while these incubate.\
    local function autoHatchRequiredEventEggs()\
        if not EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then return false end\
    \
        local requiredCounts = {}\
        for _, category in ipairs(EventRuntime.requirements or {}) do\
            requiredCounts[category] = (requiredCounts[category] or 0) + 1\
        end\
        if not next(requiredCounts) then return false end\
    \
        local rs = S.ReplicatedStorage\
        local Save = nil\
        local EggState = nil\
        local PlacedEggRenderer = nil\
        pcall(function() Save = require(rs.Shared.Save).Get() end)\
        pcall(function() EggState = require(rs.Client.EggState) end)\
        pcall(function() PlacedEggRenderer = require(rs.Shared.Eggs.PlacedEggRenderer) end)\
    \
        local eggInv = Save and Save.EggInventory or {}\
        local hatched = false\
    \
        local function guiSaysReady(uid)\
            local pg = LP:FindFirstChild(\"PlayerGui\")\
            local growing = pg and pg:FindFirstChild(\"GrowingEggs\")\
            local frame = growing and growing:FindFirstChild(\"Frame\")\
            local sf = frame and frame:FindFirstChild(\"ScrollingFrame\")\
            local card = sf and sf:FindFirstChild(tostring(uid))\
            local spacer = card and card:FindFirstChild(\"Spacer\")\
            local openBtn = spacer and spacer:FindFirstChild(\"Open\")\
            return openBtn and openBtn.Visible == true\
        end\
    \
        for uid, eggData in pairs(eggInv) do\
            if not EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then break end\
            local category = eggData.AssetCategory\
            if requiredCounts[category] and eggData.Placement then\
                local ready = false\
                if EggState and EggState.IsReadyToHatch then\
                    pcall(function() ready = EggState.IsReadyToHatch(uid) == true end)\
                end\
                if not ready then ready = guiSaysReady(uid) == true end\
    \
                if ready then\
                    UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\"Hatching required %s egg...\", tostring(category)))\
                    if PlacedEggRenderer then\
                        pcall(function() PlacedEggRenderer.ActivateLocalEgg(uid) end)\
                    end\
    \
                    local hatchAccepted = false\
                    if RMT.Hatch then\
                        pcall(function()\
                            local r = RMT.Hatch:InvokeServer(uid)\
                            hatchAccepted = (r == nil or r == true or (type(r) == \"table\" and (r.success == true or r.Success == true)))\
                        end)\
                    end\
    \
                    task.wait(0.08)\
                    if RMT.FinishHatch then\
                        pcall(function() RMT.FinishHatch:InvokeServer(uid) end)\
                    end\
    \
                    hatched = hatchAccepted or true\
                    task.wait(0.18)\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        return hatched\
    end\
    \
    -- Sub-engine: Auto Fuse Event\
    local function stepAutoFuseEvent()\
        if not EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then return end\
    \
        -- IMPORTANT:\
        -- The Auto Fuse treadmill watchdog is active for the entire ON session, but\
        -- it requires a REAL movement stall before releasing. The lightweight check\
        -- below remains only as an immediate idle/scanner fast-path.\
    \
        -- Scanner NEVER owns the startup Safezone route.\
        -- It only waits until the OFF -> ON preflight has finished.\
        -- Once unlocked, repeated scans can never send the character back to Safezone.\
        if not EventRuntime.fuseStartSafezoneComplete then\
            UpdateEventStatus(\"Waiting initial Safezone pass...\")\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- Fuse treadmill safety:\
        -- only release while the scanner owns NO movement.\
        -- Runtime.isAutoWalking=true during the proven egg route, so this can never\
        -- create the old stop-go movement bug.\
        if not Runtime.isAutoWalking\
            and not Runtime.carryEscapeActive\
            and not isCarryingEgg()\
            and tick() - (EventRuntime.lastFuseTreadmillReleaseAt or 0) >= 1.0\
        then\
            local onTreadmill = false\
            pcall(function()\
                onTreadmill = isPlayerOnTreadmill() == true\
            end)\
    \
            if onTreadmill then\
                EventRuntime.lastFuseTreadmillReleaseAt = tick()\
                UpdateEventStatus(\"Scanner: treadmill active -> releasing...\")\
                releaseTreadmill()\
                task.wait(0.12)\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- HIGHEST PRIORITY: carried event egg always completes the same escape\
        -- lifecycle as the main Auto Farm before any boss/fuse work continues.\
        if isCarryingEgg() then\
            UpdateEventStatus(\"Carry detected -> MAIN FARM Anti Boss -> Safezone...\")\
            instantCarryEscape()\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- 1) Read authoritative Rift state first.\
        local currentState = askRiftState()\
    \
        -- If a previous sacrifice left a reward pending, finish that lifecycle FIRST.\
        if currentState and currentState.PendingReward == true then\
            UpdateEventStatus(\"Pending Rift reward detected -> auto claiming egg...\")\
            local beforeRewardEggs = snapshotOwnedEggUids()\
            local claimed, newState, rewardUid = claimPendingRiftReward(EventRuntime.pendingRewardSince ~= nil, beforeRewardEggs)\
            if claimed then\
                UpdateEventStatus(\"Rift Egg received -> resetting scanner...\")\
                Notify(\"The Rift: reward egg claimed automatically!\", 3)\
                resetRiftCycleRuntime()\
                if newState and type(newState.Requirements) == \"table\" then\
                    EventRuntime.requirements = newState.Requirements\
                    EventRuntime.lastRiftState = newState\
                end\
                task.wait(0.25)\
                UpdateEventStatus(\"Scanning new Rift requirements...\")\
                refreshPetStatuses()\
            else\
                UpdateEventStatus(\"Reward not received yet -> retrying auto claim...\")\
                task.wait(0.6)\
            end\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- 2) Free-only banner reroll.\
        if EventCfg.autoRerollBanner and EventCfg.desiredBanner ~= \"Any\" then\
            pcall(function()\
                if RMT.RiftAskRefresh then\
                    local state = currentState or askRiftState()\
                    if state and (state.FreeRefreshesRemaining or 0) > 0 then\
                        local curBanner = tostring(state.BannerDisplayName or \"\")\
                        if curBanner ~= \"\" and curBanner:lower() ~= EventCfg.desiredBanner:lower() then\
                            UpdateEventStatus(\"Rerolling banner to \" .. EventCfg.desiredBanner .. \"...\")\
                            RMT.RiftAskRefresh:InvokeServer()\
                            task.wait(0.7)\
                            currentState = askRiftState()\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end)\
        end\
    \
        -- 3) Requirements -> backpack/pet scan.\
        local statuses, readyCount, totalCount = refreshPetStatuses()\
        if totalCount == 0 then\
            UpdateEventStatus(\"Fetching Rift requirements...\")\
            task.wait(1)\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- 4) Existing pets are added immediately to their exact slot / UID.\
        for slotIdx, st in ipairs(statuses) do\
            if st.ready and st.uid then\
                UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\
                    \"Requirement %d/%d: adding existing %s pet...\",\
                    slotIdx, totalCount, tostring(st.category)\
                ))\
                autoAddPetToRiftSlot(slotIdx, st.category, st.uid)\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- 5) Place every required egg already in backpack before farming more.\
        for slotIdx = 1, totalCount do\
            if not EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then return end\
    \
            statuses, readyCount, totalCount = refreshPetStatuses()\
            local st = statuses[slotIdx]\
            if st and st.status == \"Egg in Backpack\" then\
                UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\
                    \"Requirement %d/%d: placing %s egg...\",\
                    slotIdx, totalCount, tostring(st.category)\
                ))\
                if doPlantSpecificEgg(st.category) then\
                    task.wait(0.18)\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- 6) Hatch only required eggs that are ready; do not wait for others.\
        if autoHatchRequiredEventEggs() then\
            task.wait(0.35)\
        end\
    \
        statuses, readyCount, totalCount = refreshPetStatuses()\
    \
        -- 7) Hatch may have created a new pet. Add exact UID now.\
        for slotIdx, st in ipairs(statuses) do\
            if st.ready and st.uid then\
                UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\"Adding %s to Rift slot %d...\", tostring(st.category), slotIdx))\
                autoAddPetToRiftSlot(slotIdx, st.category, st.uid)\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- 8) All three pets exist -> official trade/reveal lifecycle.\
        if readyCount >= totalCount and totalCount == 3 then\
            local uids = {}\
            for slotIdx = 1, totalCount do\
                local st = statuses[slotIdx]\
                if st and st.uid then table.insert(uids, st.uid) end\
            end\
    \
            if #uids == 3 and RMT.RiftAskTradeIn then\
                UpdateEventStatus(\"All 3 pets ready -> sacrificing...\")\
    \
                -- Snapshot BEFORE sacrifice. The Fuse Event may only reset after a NEW\
                -- Rift reward egg is confirmed in EggInventory.\
                local beforeRewardEggs = snapshotOwnedEggUids()\
    \
                local okCall, tradeResult = pcall(function()\
                    return RMT.RiftAskTradeIn:InvokeServer(uids)\
                end)\
    \
                -- Reverse-engineered official client accepts ONLY return == true.\
                if not okCall or tradeResult ~= true then\
                    UpdateEventStatus(\"Rift refused trade -> refreshing slots...\")\
                    task.wait(0.5)\
                    refreshPetStatuses()\
                    return\
                end\
    \
                EventRuntime.pendingRewardSince = tick()\
                UpdateEventStatus(\"Sacrifice accepted -> syncing reward state...\")\
    \
                -- Official client performs AskState immediately after successful trade.\
                local stateAfterTrade = askRiftState()\
                local stateDeadline = tick() + 2.0\
                while EventCfg.autoFuseEvent\
                    and (not stateAfterTrade or stateAfterTrade.PendingReward ~= true)\
                    and tick() < stateDeadline\
                do\
                    task.wait(0.15)\
                    stateAfterTrade = askRiftState()\
                end\
    \
                -- Respect the official reveal window, then repeatedly press the exact\
                -- server-side claim action until the Rift Egg appears in EggInventory.\
                local claimed, nextState, rewardUid = claimPendingRiftReward(true, beforeRewardEggs)\
                if not claimed then\
                    UpdateEventStatus(\"Sacrifice accepted; Rift Egg not received yet -> retrying claim...\")\
                    task.wait(0.8)\
                    return\
                end\
    \
                -- IMPORTANT: reset ONLY AFTER the actual reward egg exists locally.\
                UpdateEventStatus(\"Rift Egg received -> starting next cycle...\")\
                Notify(\"The Rift: reward egg claimed automatically!\", 3)\
                resetRiftCycleRuntime()\
    \
                if nextState and type(nextState.Requirements) == \"table\" then\
                    EventRuntime.requirements = nextState.Requirements\
                    EventRuntime.lastRiftState = nextState\
                end\
    \
                task.wait(0.25)\
                UpdateEventStatus(\"Scanning new Rift requirements...\")\
                refreshPetStatuses()\
                return\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- 9) Build the exact missing SLOT list after placing/hatching/adding.\
        local missingCategories = {}\
        for slotIdx, st in ipairs(statuses) do\
            if st and st.status == \"Need to Farm Egg\" then\
                table.insert(missingCategories, st.category)\
            end\
        end\
    \
        if #missingCategories > 0 then\
            UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\
                \"Need %d more egg(s): %s\",\
                #missingCategories,\
                table.concat(missingCategories, \", \")\
            ))\
    \
            -- Scan once for the nearest egg matching ANY missing requirement.\
            local rootPos = getRoot() and getRoot().Position or Vector3.zero\
            local bestEgg = nil\
            local bestDist = math.huge\
    \
            pcall(function()\
                local EggState = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)\
                local snap = EggState.ReadFieldEggs()\
                if snap and snap.Records then\
                    for _, rec in ipairs(snap.Records) do\
                        local neededCat = nil\
                        for _, cat in ipairs(missingCategories) do\
                            if rec.AssetCategory == cat then\
                                neededCat = cat\
                                break\
                            end\
                        end\
    \
                        if neededCat and rec.Uid then\
                            local model = S.Workspace.AreaEggSlotsClient:FindFirstChild(rec.Uid)\
                                or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(rec.Uid)\
                                or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(rec.Uid, true)\
    \
                            local pos = rec.BottomCFrame and rec.BottomCFrame.Position\
                            if model then\
                                local part = model:FindFirstChild(\"Hitbox\")\
                                    or model:FindFirstChild(\"CustomBoundingBox\")\
                                    or model.PrimaryPart\
                                    or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(\"BasePart\")\
                                if part then pos = part.Position end\
                            end\
    \
                            if pos then\
                                local d = (pos - rootPos).Magnitude\
                                if d < bestDist then\
                                    bestDist = d\
                                    bestEgg = {\
                                        uid = rec.Uid,\
                                        model = model,\
                                        pos = pos,\
                                        slotKey = tostring(rec.NestId or rec.Uid),\
                                        category = neededCat,\
                                    }\
                                end\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end)\
    \
            if not bestEgg then\
                for _, child in ipairs(S.Workspace:GetChildren()) do\
                    if isDroppedEggModel(child) then\
                        local catText = tostring(child:GetAttribute(\"AssetCategory\") or child.Name)\
                        local neededCat = nil\
                        for _, missCat in ipairs(missingCategories) do\
                            if catText:lower():find(missCat:lower(), 1, true) then\
                                neededCat = missCat\
                                break\
                            end\
                        end\
    \
                        if neededCat then\
                            local part = child:FindFirstChild(\"Hitbox\")\
                                or child:FindFirstChild(\"CustomBoundingBox\")\
                                or child.PrimaryPart\
                                or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(\"BasePart\")\
    \
                            if part then\
                                local d = (part.Position - rootPos).Magnitude\
                                if d < bestDist then\
                                    bestDist = d\
                                    bestEgg = {\
                                        uid = child.Name,\
                                        model = child,\
                                        pos = part.Position,\
                                        slotKey = tostring(child:GetAttribute(\"FirstAreaSlotKey\") or child.Name),\
                                        category = neededCat,\
                                    }\
                                end\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            if bestEgg then\
                UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\
                    \"Found required %s egg → Safezone first...\",\
                    tostring(bestEgg.category)\
                ))\
    \
                -- POST-PLACE / NEXT-REQUIREMENT SAFEZONE STAGE\
                -- bestEgg is already selected above and is NOT changed/rescanned.\
                -- We only route to the neutral Safezone before continuing to\
                -- farmSpecificEgg() with this exact same target.\
                local stagedSafezone = WalkToTarget(\
                    SAFEZONE_POS,\
                    6,\
                    false,\
                    function()\
                        return EventCfg.autoFuseEvent\
                            and not (EventCfg.autoBoss and checkBossGateOpen())\
                    end\
                )\
    \
                if not stagedSafezone then\
                    return\
                end\
    \
                UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\
                    \"Safezone reached → farming required %s egg...\",\
                    tostring(bestEgg.category)\
                ))\
    \
                local success = farmSpecificEgg(bestEgg.category, bestEgg)\
                if success then\
                    -- The carry helper already waited for EggInventory replication.\
                    UpdateEventStatus(string.format(\
                        \"%s secured → placing immediately...\",\
                        tostring(bestEgg.category)\
                    ))\
    \
                    if doPlantSpecificEgg(bestEgg.category) then\
                        task.wait(0.2)\
                        autoHatchRequiredEventEggs()\
                        refreshPetStatuses()\
                    else\
                        UpdateEventStatus(\"Egg secured; waiting for backpack/plot replication...\")\
                    end\
                else\
                    UpdateEventStatus(\"Required egg pickup/deposit failed → rescanning...\")\
                    task.wait(0.5)\
                end\
                return\
            end\
    \
            UpdateEventStatus(\"Required eggs not visible yet — rescanning...\")\
            task.wait(1.0)\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- 10) Nothing left to farm: keep hatching + adding as incubation completes.\
        UpdateEventStatus(\"All required eggs secured — waiting for hatch...\")\
        autoHatchRequiredEventEggs()\
        task.wait(0.75)\
    end\
    \
    -- Deteksi dan hindari laser/meteor (melompat saat hazard mendekat)\
    local function handleArenaHazards()\
        local hazards = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"BossHazards\")\
        if not hazards then return end\
    \
        local root = getRoot()\
        local hum = getHumanoid()\
        if not root or not hum then return end\
    \
        local myPos = root.Position\
    \
        for _, hz in ipairs(hazards:GetChildren()) do\
            if hz:IsA(\"BasePart\") then\
                local name = hz.Name\
                local dist = (myPos - hz.Position).Magnitude\
    \
                -- Laser beam (RotatingX) → Lompat saat jarak <= 6 stud\
                if (name == \"XBar\" or name == \"XIndicator\" or name:find(\"Bar\") or name:find(\"Laser\")) and dist <= 6 then\
                    pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end)\
                end\
    \
                -- Meteor ring (ExpandingRing) → Lompat saat dekat\
                if (name == \"RingSegment\" or name:find(\"Ring\") or name:find(\"Meteor\")) and dist <= 7 then\
                    pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end)\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    end\
    \
    -- ============================================================\
    -- [BOSS ENGINE V2 — ARROW FOLLOWING + SMART EXIT]\
    -- Ikuti tanda panah merah (TutorialBeam.Attachment1) untuk menentukan target.\
    -- Jika tidak ada panah: cari crystal aktif. Jika tidak ada crystal: serang tangan boss.\
    -- Setelah boss kalah: kunjungi BossShopStand dulu, lalu keluar via portal.\
    -- ============================================================\
    \
    -- Sub-engine: Auto Boss\
    local function stepAutoBoss()\
        if not EventCfg.autoBoss then return end\
    \
        local function setBossState(state, status)\
            BossRuntime.state = state\
            if status then UpdateBossStatus(status) end\
        end\
    \
        local function readSnapshot()\
            local snap = nil\
            pcall(function()\
                if RMT.BossAskSnapshot then\
                    local r = RMT.BossAskSnapshot:InvokeServer()\
                    if type(r) == \"table\" then snap = r end\
                end\
            end)\
            return snap\
        end\
    \
        local function flatDistance(a, b)\
            local dx = a.X - b.X\
            local dz = a.Z - b.Z\
            return math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)\
        end\
    \
        local function stopMoving()\
            local r = getRoot()\
            local h = getHumanoid()\
            _stopHumanoidAtCurrentPosition(h, r)\
        end\
    \
        local function resetFreshEncounter(arena)\
            BossRuntime.arena = arena\
            BossRuntime.bossModel = nil\
            BossRuntime.destroyedCrystals = {}\
            BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
            BossRuntime.shopVisited = false\
            BossRuntime.shopApproachDone = false\
            BossRuntime.shopArrivedAt = nil\
            BossRuntime.exitingToLobby = false\
            BossRuntime.exitPortalTarget = nil\
            BossRuntime.lastTarget = nil\
            BossRuntime.lastTargetKind = nil\
            BossRuntime.handCheckpoint = nil\
            BossRuntime.handCheckpointTower = nil\
            BossRuntime.handBeamMissingSince = nil\
            BossRuntime.cachedBatTool = nil\
            BossRuntime.attackSequence = 0\
            BossRuntime.lastAttackAt = 0\
        end\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- OUTSIDE ARENA — ONLY REAL RANDOM ENTRANCE GATE COUNTS\
        -- ========================================================\
        if not isPlayerInBossArena() then\
            -- If we were inside this encounter on the previous tick but are now\
            -- outside, the Return-to-Lobby portal has succeeded (manual OR auto).\
            -- Do not leave the UI stuck on the last arena status.\
            if BossRuntime.arena ~= nil then\
                -- InBossArena already became false: the Leave Portal succeeded.\
                -- Boss must release coordinator ownership immediately so Fuse can resume.\
                BossRuntime.completedEntranceGate =\
                    BossRuntime.activeEntranceGate\
                    or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"BossArenaTeleport\")\
                BossRuntime.activeEntranceGate = nil\
    \
                BossRuntime.waitForNextSpawn = false\
                BossRuntime.sawClosedAfterExit = false\
                BossRuntime.arena = nil\
                BossRuntime.bossModel = nil\
                BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
                BossRuntime.handCheckpoint = nil\
                BossRuntime.handCheckpointTower = nil\
                BossRuntime.handBeamMissingSince = nil\
                BossRuntime.destroyedCrystals = {}\
                BossRuntime.cachedBatTool = nil\
    \
                setBossState(\"IDLE\", \"Idle\")\
                return\
            end\
    \
            if BossRuntime.waitForNextSpawn then\
                local snap = readSnapshot()\
    \
                if snap and snap.Open == false then\
                    BossRuntime.sawClosedAfterExit = true\
                    setBossState(\"WAIT_NEXT\", \"Boss complete -> waiting next spawn...\")\
                    return\
                end\
    \
                if snap and snap.Open == true and not BossRuntime.sawClosedAfterExit then\
                    setBossState(\"WAIT_CLOSE\", \"Lobby -> waiting current boss window close...\")\
                    return\
                end\
    \
                if snap and snap.Open == true and BossRuntime.sawClosedAfterExit then\
                    BossRuntime.waitForNextSpawn = false\
                    BossRuntime.sawClosedAfterExit = false\
                    BossRuntime.arena = nil\
                    BossRuntime.bossModel = nil\
                    BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
                    BossRuntime.destroyedCrystals = {}\
                    BossRuntime.shopVisited = false\
                    BossRuntime.shopApproachDone = false\
                    BossRuntime.cachedBatTool = nil\
                    setBossState(\"WAIT_GATE\", \"New boss window -> scanning random gate...\")\
                elseif not snap then\
                    setBossState(\"WAIT_SERVER\", \"Waiting boss server state...\")\
                    return\
                else\
                    return\
                end\
            end\
    \
            local gate, gatePart = findSpawnedBossEntranceGate()\
            if not gate or not gatePart then\
                setBossState(\"WAIT_GATE\", \"Waiting random Boss Arena gate...\")\
                return\
            end\
    \
            local zoneName = getNearestBossGateZoneName(gatePart.Position)\
            BossRuntime.entryZoneName = zoneName\
            BossRuntime.activeEntranceGate = gate\
    \
            setBossState(\
                \"ENTER_GATE\",\
                string.format(\"Gate found in %s -> entering...\", tostring(zoneName))\
            )\
    \
            local reached = WalkToTarget(\
                gatePart.Position,\
                3.5,\
                false,\
                function()\
                    return EventCfg.autoBoss\
                        and not isPlayerInBossArena()\
                        and gate.Parent ~= nil\
                        and gatePart.Parent ~= nil\
                end\
            )\
    \
            if reached and not isPlayerInBossArena() then\
                pcall(function()\
                    if RMT.BossAskEnter then\
                        RMT.BossAskEnter:InvokeServer()\
                    end\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            task.wait(0.25)\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- INSIDE ARENA\
        -- ========================================================\
        local arena = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"BossArena\")\
        local root = getRoot()\
        local hum = getHumanoid()\
        local char = LP.Character\
    \
        if not arena or not root or not hum or not char then return end\
    \
        if BossRuntime.arena ~= arena then\
            resetFreshEncounter(arena)\
        end\
    \
        if BossRuntime.character ~= char then\
            resetBossTargetState(char, true)\
        end\
    \
        if tick() < (BossRuntime.respawnGraceUntil or 0) then\
            setBossState(\"RESPAWN\", \"Respawned -> waiting character settle...\")\
            stopMoving()\
            return\
        end\
    \
        handleArenaHazards()\
    \
        local snap = readSnapshot()\
        local boss = arena:FindFirstChild(\"Boss\")\
        local shopStand = arena:FindFirstChild(\"BossShopStand\")\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- BOSS DEFEATED -> DIRECT RETURN TO LOBBY\
        -- User explicitly requested to skip Boss Shop entirely.\
        -- BossShopStand existence and BossHealth <= 0 are both defeat signals.\
        -- This block runs BEFORE requiring the Boss model because the Boss model\
        -- may disappear immediately after defeat.\
        -- ========================================================\
        local snapshotBossHp = snap and tonumber(snap.BossHealth) or nil\
        local bossDefeated = shopStand ~= nil\
            or (snapshotBossHp ~= nil and snapshotBossHp <= 0)\
    \
        if bossDefeated then\
            BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
            BossRuntime.handCheckpoint = nil\
            BossRuntime.handCheckpointTower = nil\
            BossRuntime.handBeamMissingSince = nil\
    \
            local leavePortal = arena:FindFirstChild(\"BossArenaLeaveTeleport\")\
            local leaveHitbox = leavePortal and leavePortal:FindFirstChild(\"Hitbox\")\
    \
            if not leaveHitbox or not leaveHitbox:IsA(\"BasePart\") then\
                setBossState(\"WAIT_EXIT\", \"Boss defeated -> waiting Return to Lobby portal...\")\
                stopMoving()\
                return\
            end\
    \
            setBossState(\"EXIT\", \"Boss defeated -> DIRECT Return to Lobby...\")\
    \
            -- Hitbox depth is only about 2 studs, so target the exact center.\
            WalkToTarget(\
                leaveHitbox.Position,\
                0.5,\
                false,\
                function()\
                    return EventCfg.autoBoss\
                        and isPlayerInBossArena()\
                        and leaveHitbox.Parent ~= nil\
                end\
            )\
    \
            -- Continue walking through center until the server clears InBossArena.\
            local exitDeadline = tick() + 4.0\
            while EventCfg.autoBoss\
                and isPlayerInBossArena()\
                and leaveHitbox.Parent\
                and tick() < exitDeadline\
            do\
                local h = getHumanoid()\
                if h then\
                    pcall(function()\
                        h:MoveTo(leaveHitbox.Position)\
                    end)\
                end\
                task.wait(0.06)\
            end\
    \
            if not isPlayerInBossArena() then\
                BossRuntime.completedEntranceGate =\
                    BossRuntime.activeEntranceGate\
                    or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"BossArenaTeleport\")\
                BossRuntime.activeEntranceGate = nil\
    \
                BossRuntime.waitForNextSpawn = false\
                BossRuntime.sawClosedAfterExit = false\
                BossRuntime.arena = nil\
                BossRuntime.bossModel = nil\
                BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
                BossRuntime.destroyedCrystals = {}\
                BossRuntime.cachedBatTool = nil\
    \
                -- Boss is COMPLETE, but its public state returns to IDLE immediately.\
                -- The next coordinator tick can therefore continue Auto Fuse Event.\
                setBossState(\"IDLE\", \"Idle\")\
            else\
                setBossState(\"EXIT\", \"Return portal touch retry...\")\
            end\
            return\
        end\
    \
        if boss and BossRuntime.bossModel ~= boss then\
            BossRuntime.bossModel = boss\
            BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
