            BossRuntime.destroyedCrystals = {}\
            BossRuntime.handCheckpoint = nil\
            BossRuntime.handBeamMissingSince = nil\
            BossRuntime.shopVisited = false\
            BossRuntime.shopApproachDone = false\
            BossRuntime.shopArrivedAt = nil\
        end\
    \
        -- Equip bat immediately after entering. AskWearFieldBat(category) is the\
        -- source-confirmed official equip path; success parents IsBat=true Tool to Character.\
        local bat = ensureBossBatEquipped()\
    \
        if not bat then\
            setBossState(\
                \"EQUIP_BAT\",\
                boss and \"Equipping Field Bat (IsBat=true)...\"\
                    or \"Arena entered -> equipping Field Bat...\"\
            )\
            stopMoving()\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- SOURCE-CONFIRMED BAT SWING\
        -- Cooldown = 0.6s. Server requires Tool Parent == Character.\
        -- FireServer args = targetPlayer? (nil in boss arena), traceId string.\
        -- ========================================================\
        local function swingBossBat(targetPos)\
            local currentBat = getEquippedBossBat() or ensureBossBatEquipped()\
            local currentRoot = getRoot()\
            if not currentBat or currentBat.Parent ~= LP.Character or not currentRoot then\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            if tick() - (BossRuntime.lastAttackAt or 0) < 0.62 then\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            if targetPos then\
                pcall(function()\
                    currentRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(\
                        currentRoot.Position,\
                        Vector3.new(targetPos.X, currentRoot.Position.Y, targetPos.Z)\
                    )\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            BossRuntime.attackSequence = (BossRuntime.attackSequence or 0) + 1\
            local traceId = string.format(\
                \"%d:%d:%d\",\
                LP.UserId,\
                BossRuntime.attackSequence,\
                math.floor(S.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() * 1000)\
            )\
    \
            -- Direct server swing is authoritative for Auto Boss and avoids the\
            -- ToolGameplayGuard client rejection discovered in BatController.Client.\
            if RMT.BatSwingTrigger then\
                pcall(function()\
                    RMT.BatSwingTrigger:FireServer(nil, traceId)\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            -- Also request the normal Tool animation/local activation. If the local\
            -- gameplay guard accepts it, this only mirrors normal player behavior.\
            pcall(function()\
                currentBat:Activate()\
            end)\
    \
            BossRuntime.lastAttackAt = tick()\
            return true\
        end\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- PHASE 1 — SOURCE-CONFIRMED CRYSTAL ENGINE\
        -- ========================================================\
        -- Official client source:\
        --   workspace.BossArena.CrystalTowers.<Model>.Hitbox\
        --   type(Hitbox:GetAttribute(\"Health\")) == \"number\" and Health > 0\
        --\
        -- IMPORTANT:\
        -- Visual CrystalTowers/Hitbox can replicate BEFORE the Health attribute.\
        -- Health == nil at arena entry means \"NOT READY\", NOT \"all destroyed\".\
        local crystalFolder = arena:FindFirstChild(\"CrystalTowers\")\
    \
        -- Source report identifies phase attributes on Boss.Model.\
        -- Keep compatibility with revisions where the returned Boss object itself\
        -- is already the actual model carrying the attributes.\
        local bossStateModel = nil\
        if boss then\
            local nestedModel = boss:FindFirstChild(\"Model\")\
            bossStateModel =\
                (nestedModel and nestedModel:IsA(\"Model\") and nestedModel)\
                or boss\
        end\
    \
        local function getPhaseTwoAt()\
            if not bossStateModel then return nil end\
            return bossStateModel:GetAttribute(\"PhaseTwoAt\")\
        end\
    \
        local function bossIsSpawning()\
            if not bossStateModel then return false end\
            return bossStateModel:GetAttribute(\"Spawning\") == true\
        end\
    \
        local function readOfficialCrystalState()\
            local result = {\
                folderReady = crystalFolder ~= nil,\
                modelCount = 0,\
                hitboxCount = 0,\
                numericHealthCount = 0,\
                alive = {},\
            }\
    \
            if not crystalFolder then\
                return result\
            end\
    \
            for _, crystalModel in ipairs(crystalFolder:GetChildren()) do\
                result.modelCount = result.modelCount + 1\
    \
                -- EXACT official path: direct child Hitbox.\
                local hitbox = crystalModel:FindFirstChild(\"Hitbox\")\
    \
                if hitbox and hitbox:IsA(\"BasePart\") then\
                    result.hitboxCount = result.hitboxCount + 1\
    \
                    local health = hitbox:GetAttribute(\"Health\")\
    \
                    -- CRITICAL:\
                    -- nil/non-number means replication is not authoritative yet.\
                    if type(health) == \"number\" then\
                        result.numericHealthCount = result.numericHealthCount + 1\
    \
                        if health > 0 then\
                            table.insert(result.alive, {\
                                hitbox = hitbox,\
                                health = health,\
                                model = crystalModel,\
                            })\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return result\
        end\
    \
        local function chooseNearestOfficialCrystal(state)\
            local r = getRoot()\
            if not r or not state then return nil end\
    \
            local best = nil\
            local bestDist = math.huge\
    \
            for _, entry in ipairs(state.alive or {}) do\
                local hitbox = entry.hitbox\
    \
                if hitbox and hitbox.Parent then\
                    local health = hitbox:GetAttribute(\"Health\")\
    \
                    if type(health) == \"number\" and health > 0 then\
                        local dist = (hitbox.Position - r.Position).Magnitude\
    \
                        if dist < bestDist then\
                            bestDist = dist\
                            best = hitbox\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return best\
        end\
    \
        -- --------------------------------------------------------\
        -- COUNTDOWN\
        -- --------------------------------------------------------\
        local bossSpawnsAt = snap and tonumber(snap.BossSpawnsAt) or nil\
        local serverNow = S.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()\
        local spawning =\
            bossIsSpawning()\
            or (bossSpawnsAt ~= nil and bossSpawnsAt > serverNow + 0.05)\
    \
        if spawning then\
            local remain = bossSpawnsAt\
                and math.max(0, bossSpawnsAt - serverNow)\
                or nil\
    \
            setBossState(\
                \"COUNTDOWN\",\
                remain\
                    and string.format(\"Bat equipped -> countdown %.1fs...\", remain)\
                    or \"Bat equipped -> waiting boss countdown...\"\
            )\
    \
            stopMoving()\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- --------------------------------------------------------\
        -- AUTHORITATIVE PHASE GATE\
        -- --------------------------------------------------------\
        local phaseTwoAt = getPhaseTwoAt()\
    \
        if phaseTwoAt == nil then\
            -- ====================================================\
            -- PHASE 1: ALWAYS-ON BASIC ATTACK LOOP\
            -- ====================================================\
            -- Start attacking as soon as Phase 1 exists.\
            -- DO NOT wait for CrystalTowers / Health / marker / target.\
            --\
            -- This intentionally reuses swingBossBat(), the exact same proven\
            -- attack primitive used by the working Phase-2 hand engine.\
            if not BossRuntime.phase1AttackRunning then\
                BossRuntime.phase1AttackRunning = true\
                BossRuntime.phase1AttackGeneration =\
                    (BossRuntime.phase1AttackGeneration or 0) + 1\
    \
                local myAttackGeneration = BossRuntime.phase1AttackGeneration\
                local myBossGeneration = BossRuntime.generation\
    \
                task.spawn(function()\
                    while EventCfg.autoBoss\
                        and isPlayerInBossArena()\
                        and BossRuntime.generation == myBossGeneration\
                        and BossRuntime.phase1AttackGeneration == myAttackGeneration\
                    do\
                        local arenaNow = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"BossArena\")\
                        local bossNow = arenaNow and arenaNow:FindFirstChild(\"Boss\")\
    \
                        local phaseModel = nil\
                        if bossNow then\
                            local nested = bossNow:FindFirstChild(\"Model\")\
                            phaseModel =\
                                (nested and nested:IsA(\"Model\") and nested)\
                                or bossNow\
                        end\
    \
                        -- Phase 2 is authoritative stop condition.\
                        if phaseModel\
                            and phaseModel:GetAttribute(\"PhaseTwoAt\") ~= nil\
                        then\
                            break\
                        end\
    \
                        -- If a crystal target is already known, face it while swinging.\
                        -- Otherwise swing with nil target exactly like normal boss-arena\
                        -- basic attack; server proximity decides whether anything is hit.\
                        local attackPos = nil\
                        local currentCrystal = BossRuntime.currentCrystal\
    \
                        if currentCrystal and currentCrystal.Parent then\
                            local hp = currentCrystal:GetAttribute(\"Health\")\
                            if type(hp) == \"number\" and hp > 0 then\
                                attackPos = currentCrystal.Position\
                            end\
                        end\
    \
                        -- Call continuously. swingBossBat() itself keeps the proven\
                        -- official ~0.62 second accepted swing cadence.\
                        swingBossBat(attackPos)\
    \
                        task.wait(0.05)\
                    end\
    \
                    if BossRuntime.phase1AttackGeneration == myAttackGeneration then\
                        BossRuntime.phase1AttackRunning = false\
                    end\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            local crystalState = readOfficialCrystalState()\
    \
            -- Crystal folder/model/Health attributes can arrive in separate\
            -- replication frames. NONE of these cases means crystals are dead.\
            if not crystalState.folderReady then\
                setBossState(\
                    \"WAIT_CRYSTAL_REPLICATION\",\
                    \"Phase 1 -> waiting CrystalTowers replication...\"\
                )\
                stopMoving()\
                return\
            end\
    \
            if crystalState.modelCount == 0 then\
                setBossState(\
                    \"WAIT_CRYSTAL_REPLICATION\",\
                    \"Phase 1 -> waiting crystal models replication...\"\
                )\
                stopMoving()\
                return\
            end\
    \
            if crystalState.hitboxCount == 0 then\
                setBossState(\
                    \"WAIT_CRYSTAL_REPLICATION\",\
                    \"Phase 1 -> waiting crystal Hitbox replication...\"\
                )\
                stopMoving()\
                return\
            end\
    \
            if crystalState.numericHealthCount == 0 then\
                -- THIS is the original bug:\
                -- visual crystals exist, but Health attributes have not replicated.\
                setBossState(\
                    \"WAIT_CRYSTAL_HEALTH\",\
                    string.format(\
                        \"Phase 1 -> waiting crystal Health replication... [%d Hitbox]\",\
                        crystalState.hitboxCount\
                    )\
                )\
                stopMoving()\
                return\
            end\
    \
            -- ----------------------------------------------------\
            -- OFFICIAL NEAREST-LIVE CRYSTAL\
            -- ----------------------------------------------------\
            local target = chooseNearestOfficialCrystal(crystalState)\
    \
            if target then\
                BossRuntime.currentCrystal = target\
    \
                local health = target:GetAttribute(\"Health\")\
                local dist = (target.Position - root.Position).Magnitude\
    \
                setBossState(\
                    \"CRYSTAL\",\
                    string.format(\
                        \"Crystal target -> HP %s | Alive %d\",\
                        tostring(health),\
                        #crystalState.alive\
                    )\
                )\
    \
                -- Official bat server range is 17 studs.\
                -- Keep the proven tighter 9-stud approach for reliable validation.\
                if dist > 9 then\
                    ArenaMoveTo(target.Position, 8)\
                    return\
                end\
    \
                stopMoving()\
    \
                pcall(function()\
                    root = getRoot()\
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(\
                        root.Position,\
                        Vector3.new(\
                            target.Position.X,\
                            root.Position.Y,\
                            target.Position.Z\
                        )\
                    )\
                end)\
    \
                -- Absolute pre-swing liveness re-check.\
                local healthBeforeSwing = target:GetAttribute(\"Health\")\
                if type(healthBeforeSwing) ~= \"number\"\
                    or healthBeforeSwing <= 0\
                then\
                    BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
                    return\
                end\
    \
                setBossState(\
                    \"CRYSTAL_ATTACK\",\
                    string.format(\
                        \"Crystal in range -> Phase 1 attack loop active | HP %s\",\
                        tostring(healthBeforeSwing)\
                    )\
                )\
    \
                -- Non-blocking nudge. The always-on Phase-1 coroutine above already\
                -- calls this same proven attack primitive continuously.\
                swingBossBat(target.Position)\
    \
                local healthAfterSwing = target:GetAttribute(\"Health\")\
                if type(healthAfterSwing) == \"number\"\
                    and healthAfterSwing <= 0\
                then\
                    BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
                end\
    \
                return\
            end\
    \
            -- We have authoritative numeric Health data and currently every\
            -- crystal is <= 0, BUT the SERVER has not set PhaseTwoAt yet.\
            --\
            -- DO NOT enter hand phase until PhaseTwoAt appears.\
            BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
            setBossState(\
                \"WAIT_PHASE2_CONFIRM\",\
                \"All crystal HP <= 0 -> waiting server PhaseTwoAt...\"\
            )\
            stopMoving()\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- Server has now authoritatively confirmed Phase 2.\
        BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
    \
        -- Kill the Phase-1 attack loop before handing control to the already-proven\
        -- hand engine below.\
        BossRuntime.phase1AttackGeneration =\
            (BossRuntime.phase1AttackGeneration or 0) + 1\
        BossRuntime.phase1AttackRunning = false\
    \
        -- From here on crystal combat is permanently disabled for this encounter.\
        BossRuntime.currentCrystal = nil\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- POST-DEFEAT SHOP FLOW REMOVED.\
        -- Boss defeat is handled earlier and exits directly through\
        -- BossArenaLeaveTeleport.Hitbox.\
        -- ========================================================\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- PHASE 2 — WAIT UNTIL THE ONE HITTABLE HAND HAS Health CHILD.\
        -- ========================================================\
        local hand = boss:FindFirstChild(\"UpperHand1.R\", true)\
        if not hand or not hand:IsA(\"Bone\") then\
            setBossState(\"WAIT_HAND\", \"Phase 2 -> waiting UpperHand1.R...\")\
            stopMoving()\
            return\
        end\
    \
        if hand:FindFirstChild(\"Health\") == nil then\
            setBossState(\"WAIT_HAND\", \"Phase 2 -> waiting hand hittable state...\")\
            stopMoving()\
            return\
        end\
    \
        local function handWorldPosition()\
            local p = nil\
            pcall(function() p = hand.WorldPosition end)\
            return p\
        end\
    \
        -- Source-confirmed own-character beam only. Never scan Workspace globally.\
        local function getOwnHandBeamPosition()\
            local r = getRoot()\
            if not r then return nil end\
    \
            local origin = r:FindFirstChild(\"BossArmBeamOrigin\")\
            local beam = origin and origin:FindFirstChild(\"TutorialBeam\")\
            if not beam or not beam:IsA(\"Beam\") or not beam.Enabled then return nil end\
    \
            local target = beam.Attachment1\
            if not target or target ~= hand then return nil end\
    \
            local p = nil\
            pcall(function() p = target.WorldPosition end)\
            return p\
        end\
    \
        local beamPos = getOwnHandBeamPosition()\
        local targetHandPos = nil\
    \
        if beamPos then\
            BossRuntime.handBeamMissingSince = nil\
            targetHandPos = beamPos\
            setBossState(\"HAND_BEAM\", \"HAND line active -> following exact target...\")\
        else\
            BossRuntime.handBeamMissingSince = BossRuntime.handBeamMissingSince or tick()\
    \
            -- Official arm beam can be recreated between Heartbeats. Give it 0.5 sec.\
            if tick() - BossRuntime.handBeamMissingSince < 0.50 then\
                setBossState(\"WAIT_HAND_BEAM\", \"Hand hittable -> waiting fresh line...\")\
                stopMoving()\
                return\
            end\
    \
            targetHandPos = handWorldPosition()\
            setBossState(\"HAND_FALLBACK\", \"No hand line -> tracking live HandBone...\")\
        end\
    \
        if not targetHandPos then\
            stopMoving()\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- DEAD CRYSTAL CHECKPOINT ROUTING TO MOVING HAND\
        -- Combat target remains the hand. Dead crystals are movement checkpoints only.\
        -- ========================================================\
        local function chooseDeadCrystalCheckpoint(handPos)\
            local r = getRoot()\
            if not r then return nil end\
    \
            local direct = flatDistance(r.Position, handPos)\
            if direct <= 24 then return nil end\
    \
            local best = nil\
            local bestScore = math.huge\
    \
            for hb, dead in pairs(BossRuntime.destroyedCrystals) do\
                if dead and hb and hb.Parent then\
                    local fromMe = flatDistance(r.Position, hb.Position)\
                    local toHand = flatDistance(hb.Position, handPos)\
    \
                    if fromMe > 5\
                        and fromMe <= 60\
                        and toHand <= direct - 7\
                    then\
                        local score = toHand + fromMe * 0.25\
                        if score < bestScore then\
                            bestScore = score\
                            best = hb\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return best\
        end\
    \
        local function safeHandApproachPosition(handPos)\
            local floor = arena:FindFirstChild(\"Floor\")\
            if not floor or not floor:IsA(\"BasePart\") then return handPos end\
    \
            -- Clamp in floor LOCAL X/Z rectangle, not radial center clamping.\
            local localP = floor.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(handPos)\
            local margin = 4\
            local maxX = math.max(1, floor.Size.X * 0.5 - margin)\
            local maxZ = math.max(1, floor.Size.Z * 0.5 - margin)\
    \
            local clamped = Vector3.new(\
                math.clamp(localP.X, -maxX, maxX),\
                floor.Size.Y * 0.5 + 3,\
                math.clamp(localP.Z, -maxZ, maxZ)\
            )\
    \
            return floor.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(clamped)\
        end\
    \
        -- Re-read line every step; if it appears during fallback, next master tick\
        -- immediately switches back to HAND_BEAM priority.\
        root = getRoot()\
        local handDist = root and (root.Position - targetHandPos).Magnitude or math.huge\
    \
        if handDist > 13 then\
            local checkpoint = chooseDeadCrystalCheckpoint(targetHandPos)\
    \
            if checkpoint then\
                setBossState(\"HAND_ROUTE\", \"Hand target -> destroyed crystal checkpoint...\")\
                ArenaMoveTo(checkpoint.Position, 5)\
                return\
            end\
    \
            local safePos = safeHandApproachPosition(targetHandPos)\
            setBossState(\
                beamPos and \"HAND_BEAM\" or \"HAND_FALLBACK\",\
                beamPos and \"HAND line -> safe final approach...\"\
                    or \"Tracking hand -> safe final approach...\"\
            )\
            hum:MoveTo(safePos)\
            return\
        end\
    \
        -- Very close to the actual moving hand: stop walking, face it, basic attack\
        -- continuously at the official 0.6-second cooldown until boss is defeated.\
        stopMoving()\
        targetHandPos = beamPos or handWorldPosition() or targetHandPos\
    \
        pcall(function()\
            root = getRoot()\
            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(\
                root.Position,\
                Vector3.new(targetHandPos.X, root.Position.Y, targetHandPos.Z)\
            )\
        end)\
    \
        setBossState(\"HAND_ATTACK\", \"Near Boss Hand -> BASIC ATTACK...\")\
        swingBossBat(targetHandPos)\
    end\
    \
    -- Master Coordinator Loop untuk Event (The Rift)\
    doEventMasterLoop = function()\
        if EventRuntime.running then return end\
        EventRuntime.running = true\
    \
        while EventCfg.autoFuseEvent or EventCfg.autoBoss do\
            -- A carried egg ALWAYS wins over boss switching. Deposit first.\
            if EventCfg.autoFuseEvent and isCarryingEgg() then\
                EventRuntime.activeSubEngine = \"FuseCarry\"\
                stepAutoFuseEvent()\
            elseif EventCfg.autoBoss and (\
                BossRuntime.arena ~= nil\
                or checkBossGateOpen()\
                or isPlayerInBossArena()\
            ) then\
                EventRuntime.activeSubEngine = \"Boss\"\
                stepAutoBoss()\
            elseif EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then\
                EventRuntime.activeSubEngine = \"Fuse\"\
                stepAutoFuseEvent()\
            else\
                EventRuntime.activeSubEngine = \"Idle\"\
            end\
    \
            task.wait(0.15)\
        end\
    \
        EventRuntime.running = false\
        EventRuntime.activeSubEngine = \"Idle\"\
        -- DO NOT reset Fuse Safezone startup here.\
        -- Only an explicit Auto Fuse OFF -> ON transition may arm it again.\
        UpdateEventStatus(\"Idle\")\
        UpdateBossStatus(\"Idle\")\
    end\
    \
    -- ============================================================\
    -- [EVENT UTILITY ENGINE — AUTO CONSUMABLE + AUTO BOSS SHOP]\
    -- LOCAL-LIMIT SAFE: implementation is isolated inside one closure.\
    -- ============================================================\
    local EventUtility = (function()\
    \
        local EventUtilityRuntime = {\
            consumableRunning = false,\
            shopRunning = false,\
            consumableStatus = \"Idle\",\
            shopStatus = \"Idle\",\
            consumableLabel = nil,\
            shopLabel = nil,\
        }\
    \
        local AUTO_CONSUMABLE_RARITIES = {\
            \"All\",\"Basic\",\"BrainrotGod\",\"Celestial\",\"Common\",\"Cosmic\",\"Divine\",\
            \"Epic\",\"Eternal\",\"Exclusive\",\"Exotic\",\"Legendary\",\"Limited\",\"Mythic\",\
            \"Mythical\",\"Rainbow\",\"Rare\",\"Secret\",\"SuperRare\",\"Superior\",\"Titan\",\
            \"Uncommon\",\"Prismatic\",\"Transcendent\",\"Admin\",\
        }\
    \
        local BOSS_SHOP_UI_ITEMS = {\
            \"Cash Booster\",\
            \"Speed Boost\",\
            \"Treadmill Booster\",\
            \"Mutation Consumable\",\
        }\
    \
        local BOSS_SHOP_ID_BY_UI = {\
            [\"Cash Booster\"]        = \"CashBooster\",\
            [\"Speed Boost\"]         = \"SpeedBoost\",\
            [\"Treadmill Booster\"]   = \"TreadmillBooster\",\
            [\"Mutation Consumable\"] = \"MutationConsumable\",\
        }\
    \
        local EventUtilityModules = {\
            loaded = false,\
            Save = nil,\
            EggState = nil,\
            Assets = nil,\
            RarityData = nil,\
            BossMastery = nil,\
            Remotes = nil,\
        }\
    \
        local function loadEventUtilityModules()\
            if EventUtilityModules.loaded then\
                return EventUtilityModules\
            end\
    \
            local rs = S.ReplicatedStorage\
    \
            pcall(function()\
                EventUtilityModules.Save = require(rs.Shared.Save)\
            end)\
            pcall(function()\
                EventUtilityModules.EggState = require(rs.Client.EggState)\
            end)\
            pcall(function()\
                EventUtilityModules.Assets = require(rs.Data.Assets)\
            end)\
            pcall(function()\
                EventUtilityModules.RarityData = require(rs.Data.Rarity)\
            end)\
            pcall(function()\
                EventUtilityModules.BossMastery = require(rs.Data.BossMastery)\
            end)\
            pcall(function()\
                EventUtilityModules.Remotes = require(rs.Shared.Remotes)\
            end)\
    \
            EventUtilityModules.loaded =\
                EventUtilityModules.Save ~= nil\
                and EventUtilityModules.EggState ~= nil\
                and EventUtilityModules.Assets ~= nil\
                and EventUtilityModules.BossMastery ~= nil\
                and EventUtilityModules.Remotes ~= nil\
    \
            return EventUtilityModules\
        end\
    \
        local function getEventSave()\
            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
            if not m.Save or not m.Save.Get then return nil end\
    \
            local save = nil\
            pcall(function()\
                save = m.Save.Get()\
            end)\
            return save\
        end\
    \
        local function getMutationConsumableCount()\
            local save = getEventSave()\
            local bm = save and save.BossMastery\
            return math.max(0, tonumber(bm and bm.MutationConsumables) or 0)\
        end\
    \
        local function getBossTokenCount()\
            local save = getEventSave()\
            return math.max(0, tonumber(save and save.BossTokens) or 0)\
        end\
    \
        local function normalizeConsumableRarity(name)\
            name = tostring(name or \"\")\
            if name == \"Prismatic\" then return \"Rainbow\" end\
            if name == \"Transcendent\" then return \"Superior\" end\
            if name == \"Admin\" then return \"Exclusive\" end\
            return name\
        end\
    \
        local function getPlacedEggRarityName(record)\
            if type(record) ~= \"table\" then return nil end\
    \
            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
            local assets = m.Assets\
            local directory = assets and (assets.Directory or assets)\
            local cfg = directory and directory[record.AssetCategory]\
            local rarity = cfg and cfg.Rarity\
            if not rarity then return nil end\
    \
            if type(rarity) == \"table\" and rarity._id then\
                return tostring(rarity._id)\
            end\
    \
            local rarityData = m.RarityData\
            local rarities = rarityData and (rarityData.Rarities or rarityData)\
    \
            if type(rarities) == \"table\" then\
                for rarityName, rarityCfg in pairs(rarities) do\
                    if rarityCfg == rarity then\
                        return tostring(rarityName)\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return nil\
        end\
    \
        local function isConsumableRaritySelected(rarityName)\
            local selected = EventCfg.consumableRarities\
    \
            if selected[\"All\"] then return true end\
            if not next(selected) then return false end\
    \
            local normalized = normalizeConsumableRarity(rarityName)\
    \
            for chosen, enabled in pairs(selected) do\
                if enabled\
                    and chosen ~= \"All\"\
                    and normalizeConsumableRarity(chosen) == normalized\
                then\
                    return true\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return false\
        end\
    \
        local function hasBossMutation(record)\
            if type(record) ~= \"table\" then return false end\
    \
            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
            local mutationId =\
                (m.BossMastery and m.BossMastery.MutationId)\
                or \"Boss\"\
    \
            return table.find(record.Mutations or {}, mutationId) ~= nil\
        end\
    \
        local function isPlacedEggConsumableEligible(record)\
            if type(record) ~= \"table\" then return false end\
            if record.Placement == nil then return false end\
            if record.IsHatching == true then return false end\
            if record.MutationPresenting == true then return false end\
            if hasBossMutation(record) then return false end\
            return true\
        end\
    \
        local function readMyPlacedEggs()\
            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
            if not m.EggState or not m.EggState.ReadOwnerEggs then\
                return {}\
            end\
    \
            local eggs = nil\
            pcall(function()\
                eggs = m.EggState.ReadOwnerEggs(LP.UserId)\
            end)\
    \
            return type(eggs) == \"table\" and eggs or {}\
        end\
    \
        local function setConsumableUtilityStatus(txt)\
            EventUtilityRuntime.consumableStatus = tostring(txt or \"Idle\")\
        end\
    \
        local function setBossShopUtilityStatus(txt)\
            EventUtilityRuntime.shopStatus = tostring(txt or \"Idle\")\
        end\
    \
        local function refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
            local consumables = getMutationConsumableCount()\
            local tokens = getBossTokenCount()\
    \
            if EventUtilityRuntime.consumableLabel then\
                EventUtilityRuntime.consumableLabel.Text =\
                    string.format(\
                        \"Mutation Consumables: %d  |  %s\",\
                        consumables,\
                        EventUtilityRuntime.consumableStatus\
                    )\
            end\
    \
            if EventUtilityRuntime.shopLabel then\
                EventUtilityRuntime.shopLabel.Text =\
                    string.format(\
                        \"Boss Tokens: %s  |  %s\",\
                        tostring(tokens),\
                        EventUtilityRuntime.shopStatus\
                    )\
            end\
        end\
    \
        local function getMutationConsumableTool()\
            local char = LP.Character\
            local backpack = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"Backpack\")\
    \
            local function isMutationConsumableTool(obj)\
                if not obj or not obj:IsA(\"Tool\") then\
                    return false\
                end\
    \
                -- Source-confirmed identifier used by the official client.\
                if obj:GetAttribute(\"ItemType\") == \"MutationConsumable\" then\
                    return true\
                end\
    \
                -- Fallback only if ItemType replication is late.\
                local lowerName = obj.Name:lower()\
                return lowerName:find(\"mutation\", 1, true) ~= nil\
                    and lowerName:find(\"consumable\", 1, true) ~= nil\
            end\
    \
            if char then\
                for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do\
                    if isMutationConsumableTool(obj) then\
                        return obj, true\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            if backpack then\
                for _, obj in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do\
                    if isMutationConsumableTool(obj) then\
                        return obj, false\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return nil, false\
        end\
    \
        local function ensureMutationConsumableEquipped()\
            local char = LP.Character\
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"Humanoid\")\
    \
            if not char or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then\
                return false, \"Character unavailable\"\
            end\
    \
            local tool, alreadyEquipped = getMutationConsumableTool()\
    \
            if not tool then\
                return false, \"Mutation Consumable tool not found\"\
            end\
    \
            if alreadyEquipped and tool.Parent == char then\
                return true, tool\
            end\
    \
            local ok = pcall(function()\
                hum:EquipTool(tool)\
            end)\
    \
            if not ok then\
                return false, \"Failed to equip Mutation Consumable\"\
            end\
    \
            task.wait(0.10)\
    \
            if tool.Parent == char then\
                return true, tool\
            end\
    \
            return false, \"Mutation Consumable did not equip\"\
        end\
    \
        local function invokeMutationConsumable(uid)\
            -- Re-equip before every attempt because other farm movement can\
            -- unequip tools between mutation requests.\
            local equipped, equipInfo = ensureMutationConsumableEquipped()\
    \
            if not equipped then\
                return false, \"equip_failed\", {\
                    Success = false,\
                    Message = tostring(equipInfo),\
                }\
            end\
    \
            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
            local remote =\
                m.Remotes\
                and m.Remotes.BossMastery\
                and m.Remotes.BossMastery.AskUseMutationConsumable\
    \
            if not remote then\
                return false, \"remote_missing\", nil\
            end\
    \
            local ok, result = pcall(function()\
                return remote:InvokeServer(uid)\
            end)\
    \
            if not ok or type(result) ~= \"table\" then\
                return false, \"error\", result\
            end\
    \
            if result.Success ~= true then\
                return false, \"rejected\", result\
            end\
    \
            if result.Mutated == true then\
                return true, \"mutated\", result\
            end\
    \
            return false, \"fizzled\", result\
        end\
    \
        local function chooseNextConsumableEgg()\
            local eggs = readMyPlacedEggs()\
    \
            local bestUid = nil\
            local bestRecord = nil\
            local bestRarityNumber = -math.huge\
    \
            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
            local assets = m.Assets\
            local directory = assets and (assets.Directory or assets)\
    \
            for uid, record in pairs(eggs) do\
                if isPlacedEggConsumableEligible(record) then\
                    local rarityName = getPlacedEggRarityName(record)\
    \
                    if rarityName and isConsumableRaritySelected(rarityName) then\
                        local cfg = directory and directory[record.AssetCategory]\
                        local rarityNumber =\
                            cfg\
                            and cfg.Rarity\
                            and tonumber(cfg.Rarity.RarityNumber)\
                            or 0\
    \
                        if not bestRecord\
                            or rarityNumber > bestRarityNumber\
                            or (\
                                rarityNumber == bestRarityNumber\
                                and tostring(uid) < tostring(bestUid)\
                            )\
                        then\
                            bestUid = tostring(uid)\
                            bestRecord = record\
                            bestRarityNumber = rarityNumber\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return bestUid, bestRecord\
        end\
    \
        local function runAutoConsumableEngine()\
            if EventUtilityRuntime.consumableRunning then return end\
            EventUtilityRuntime.consumableRunning = true\
    \
            while EventCfg.autoConsumable do\
                local count = getMutationConsumableCount()\
    \
                if count <= 0 then\
                    setConsumableUtilityStatus(\"No consumables\")\
                    refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                    task.wait(1.0)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                if not next(EventCfg.consumableRarities) then\
                    setConsumableUtilityStatus(\"Select rarity\")\
                    refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                    task.wait(1.0)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                local uid, record = chooseNextConsumableEgg()\
    \
                if not uid or not record then\
                    setConsumableUtilityStatus(\"No eligible selected-rarity egg\")\
                    refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                    task.wait(1.0)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                local rarityName = getPlacedEggRarityName(record) or \"Unknown\"\
                setConsumableUtilityStatus(\
                    string.format(\"Target %s -> remote apply from current position...\", rarityName)\
                )\
                refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
    \
                -- Retry the SAME egg after a fizzled 10% roll.\
                while EventCfg.autoConsumable do\
                    local freshEggs = readMyPlacedEggs()\
                    local fresh = freshEggs[uid]\
    \
                    if not fresh or not isPlacedEggConsumableEligible(fresh) then\
                        break\
                    end\
    \
                    if getMutationConsumableCount() <= 0 then\
                        break\
                    end\
    \
                    local mutated, status, result =\
                        invokeMutationConsumable(uid)\
    \
                    if mutated then\
                        setConsumableUtilityStatus(\
                            string.format(\"%s mutated successfully\", rarityName)\
                        )\
                        refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                        task.wait(0.55)\
                        break\
                    end\
    \
                    if status == \"fizzled\" then\
                        setConsumableUtilityStatus(\
                            string.format(\"%s fizzled -> retrying\", rarityName)\
                        )\
                        refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                        task.wait(0.55)\
    \
                    elseif status == \"equip_failed\" then\
                        local message =\
                            type(result) == \"table\"\
                            and tostring(result.Message or \"Equip failed\")\
                            or \"Equip failed\"\
    \
                        setConsumableUtilityStatus(message)\
                        refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                        task.wait(0.75)\
    \
                    elseif status == \"rejected\" then\
                        local message =\
                            type(result) == \"table\"\
                            and tostring(result.Message or \"Rejected\")\
                            or \"Rejected\"\
    \
                        setConsumableUtilityStatus(message)\
                        refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                        task.wait(1.0)\
                        break\
    \
                    else\
                        setConsumableUtilityStatus(\"Mutation request error\")\
                        refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                        task.wait(1.0)\
                        break\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                task.wait(0.15)\
            end\
    \
            EventUtilityRuntime.consumableRunning = false\
            setConsumableUtilityStatus(\"Idle\")\
            refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
        end\
    \
        local function getCurrentBossShopProducts()\
            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
            if not m.BossMastery or not m.BossMastery.GetShopProducts then\
                return {}, {}\
            end\
    \
            local products = {}\
            pcall(function()\
                products = m.BossMastery.GetShopProducts() or {}\
            end)\
    \
            local byId = {}\
            for _, product in ipairs(products) do\
                if type(product) == \"table\" and product.Id then\
                    byId[tostring(product.Id)] = product\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return products, byId\
        end\
    \
        local function getOfficialBossShopPrice(product)\
            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
            if not product then return math.huge end\
    \
            local price = nil\
            if m.BossMastery and m.BossMastery.GetShopPrice then\
                pcall(function()\
                    price = m.BossMastery.GetShopPrice(product)\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            return tonumber(price)\
                or tonumber(product.Price)\
                or math.huge\
        end\
    \
        local function buyBossShopProduct(product)\
            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
            local remote =\
                m.Remotes\
                and m.Remotes.BossMastery\
                and m.Remotes.BossMastery.AskBuyShopItem\
    \
            if not remote or not product or not product.Id then\
                return false, \"Purchase remote unavailable\"\
            end\
    \
            local ok, success, message = pcall(function()\
                return remote:InvokeServer(product.Id)\
            end)\
    \
            if not ok then\
                return false, \"Purchase request error\"\
            end\
    \
            if success == true then\
                return true, tostring(message or \"Purchased\")\
            end\
    \
            return false, tostring(message or \"Purchase rejected\")\
        end\
    \
        local function selectedBossShopIdsInPriority()\
            local ids = {}\
    \
            for _, uiName in ipairs(BOSS_SHOP_UI_ITEMS) do\
                if EventCfg.bossShopItems[uiName] then\
                    local id = BOSS_SHOP_ID_BY_UI[uiName]\
                    if id then table.insert(ids, id) end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return ids\
        end\
    \
        local function runAutoBuyBossShopEngine()\
            if EventUtilityRuntime.shopRunning then return end\
            EventUtilityRuntime.shopRunning = true\
    \
            while EventCfg.autoBuyBossShop do\
                local selectedIds = selectedBossShopIdsInPriority()\
    \
                if #selectedIds == 0 then\
                    setBossShopUtilityStatus(\"Select shop item\")\
                    refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                    task.wait(1.0)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                local _, productsById = getCurrentBossShopProducts()\
                local boughtThisCycle = false\
    \
                for _, itemId in ipairs(selectedIds) do\
                    if not EventCfg.autoBuyBossShop then break end\
    \
                    local product = productsById[itemId]\
                    if product then\
                        local price = getOfficialBossShopPrice(product)\
                        local tokens = getBossTokenCount()\
    \
                        if price ~= math.huge and tokens >= price then\
                            local displayName = itemId\
                            local m = loadEventUtilityModules()\
    \
                            if m.BossMastery\
                                and m.BossMastery.GetShopDisplayName\
                            then\
                                pcall(function()\
                                    displayName =\
                                        m.BossMastery.GetShopDisplayName(product)\
                                        or displayName\
                                end)\
                            end\
    \
                            setBossShopUtilityStatus(\
                                string.format(\
                                    \"Buying %s (%d)\",\
                                    tostring(displayName),\
                                    price\
                                )\
                            )\
                            refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
    \
                            local success, message =\
                                buyBossShopProduct(product)\
    \
                            if success then\
                                boughtThisCycle = true\
                                setBossShopUtilityStatus(\
                                    string.format(\
                                        \"Purchased %s\",\
                                        tostring(displayName)\
                                    )\
                                )\
                                refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                                task.wait(0.55)\
                            else\
                                setBossShopUtilityStatus(message)\
                                refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                                task.wait(1.0)\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                if not boughtThisCycle then\
                    setBossShopUtilityStatus(\"Waiting for enough Boss Tokens\")\
                    refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
                    task.wait(1.0)\
                else\
                    task.wait(0.15)\
                end\
            end\
    \
            EventUtilityRuntime.shopRunning = false\
            setBossShopUtilityStatus(\"Idle\")\
            refreshEventUtilityLabels()\
        end\
    \
        local function startAutoConsumableEngine()\
            if EventCfg.autoConsumable\
                and not EventUtilityRuntime.consumableRunning\
            then\
                task.spawn(runAutoConsumableEngine)\
            end\
        end\
    \
        local function startAutoBuyBossShopEngine()\
            if EventCfg.autoBuyBossShop\
                and not EventUtilityRuntime.shopRunning\
            then\
                task.spawn(runAutoBuyBossShopEngine)\
            end\
        end\
    \
        task.spawn(function()\
            while true do\
                pcall(refreshEventUtilityLabels)\
                task.wait(0.5)\
            end\
        end)\
    \
    \
        return {\
            Runtime = EventUtilityRuntime,\
            Rarities = AUTO_CONSUMABLE_RARITIES,\
            ShopItems = BOSS_SHOP_UI_ITEMS,\
    \
            StartConsumable = startAutoConsumableEngine,\
            StartShop = startAutoBuyBossShopEngine,\
    \
            SetConsumableStatus = setConsumableUtilityStatus,\
            SetShopStatus = setBossShopUtilityStatus,\
            Refresh = refreshEventUtilityLabels,\
    \
            GetConsumableCount = getMutationConsumableCount,\
            GetBossTokens = getBossTokenCount,\
        }\
    end)()\
    \
    -- ============================================================\
    -- [MISC ENGINE]\
    -- ============================================================\
    local FpsState = {\
        enabled = false,\
        savedShadows = nil,\
        savedWater = nil,\
        savedParticles = {},\
        savedPostFx = {},\
        conn = nil,\
    }\
    \
    local function applyFpsBoost(enabled)\
        FpsState.enabled = enabled\
        local Lighting = S.Lighting\
        local POST = {BloomEffect=true, BlurEffect=true, ColorCorrectionEffect=true, DepthOfFieldEffect=true, SunRaysEffect=true}\
        local KILL = {ParticleEmitter=true, Trail=true, Beam=true, Smoke=true, Fire=true, Sparkles=true}\
    \
        if enabled then\
            pcall(function()\
                if FpsState.savedShadows == nil then FpsState.savedShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows end\
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = false\
            end)\
            pcall(function()\
                if S.Workspace.Terrain then\
                    if FpsState.savedWater == nil then FpsState.savedWater = S.Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed end\
                    S.Workspace.Terrain.Decoration = false\
                    S.Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0\
                    S.Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0\
                end\
            end)\
            for _, obj in ipairs(S.Workspace:GetDescendants()) do\
                if KILL[obj.ClassName] then\
                    FpsState.savedParticles[obj] = obj.Enabled\
                    pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)\
                elseif POST[obj.ClassName] then\
                    FpsState.savedPostFx[obj] = obj.Enabled\
                    pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)\
                end\
            end\
            for _, obj in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do\
                if POST[obj.ClassName] then\
                    FpsState.savedPostFx[obj] = obj.Enabled\
                    pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)\
                end\
            end\
            if not FpsState.conn then\
                FpsState.conn = S.Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)\
                    if not FpsState.enabled then return end\
                    if KILL[obj.ClassName] or POST[obj.ClassName] then pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end) end\
                end)\
                table.insert(ScriptConnections, FpsState.conn)\
            end\
        else\
            pcall(function() if FpsState.savedShadows ~= nil then Lighting.GlobalShadows = FpsState.savedShadows end end)\
            pcall(function()\
                if S.Workspace.Terrain and FpsState.savedWater ~= nil then\
                    S.Workspace.Terrain.Decoration = true\
                    S.Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = FpsState.savedWater\
                end\
            end)\
            for obj, was in pairs(FpsState.savedParticles) do if obj and obj.Parent then pcall(function() obj.Enabled = was end) end end\
            for obj, was in pairs(FpsState.savedPostFx) do if obj and obj.Parent then pcall(function() obj.Enabled = was end) end end\
            table.clear(FpsState.savedParticles)\
            table.clear(FpsState.savedPostFx)\
            if FpsState.conn then FpsState.conn:Disconnect(); FpsState.conn = nil end\
        end\
    end\
    \
    local HopState = { lastHopCheck = tick() }\
    \
    local function executeServerHop(method)\
        method = method or MiscCfg.hopMethod or \"Small Server\"\
        local placeId = PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId\
        local currentJobId = game.JobId\
    \
        UpdateStatus(\"Searching small server...\")\
        local targetServer = nil\
    \
        if method == \"Small Server\" then\
            local cursor = \"\"\
            local minPlayers = math.huge\
    \
            for page = 1, 5 do\
                local url = string.format(\"https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%s/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100%s\",\
                    tostring(placeId),\
                    cursor ~= \"\" and (\"&cursor=\" .. cursor) or \"\")\
    \
                local ok, res = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)\
                if not ok or type(res) ~= \"string\" or res == \"\" then break end\
    \
                local okJson, data = pcall(function() return S.HttpService:JSONDecode(res) end)\
                if not okJson or not data or not data.data then break end\
    \
                for _, s in ipairs(data.data) do\
                    local plrs = tonumber(s.playing) or 0\
                    local maxP = tonumber(s.maxPlayers) or 12\
                    if s.id ~= currentJobId and plrs > 0 and plrs < maxP then\
                        if plrs < minPlayers then\
                            minPlayers = plrs\
                            targetServer = s\
                            if plrs <= 1 then break end\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                if minPlayers <= 1 then break end\
                if data.nextPageCursor and data.nextPageCursor ~= \"\" and data.nextPageCursor ~= \"null\" then\
                    cursor = data.nextPageCursor\
                else\
                    break\
                end\
            end\
        else\
            local url = string.format(\"https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%s/servers/Public?sortOrder=Desc&limit=100\", tostring(placeId))\
            local ok, res = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)\
            if ok and type(res) == \"string\" and res ~= \"\" then\
                local okJson, data = pcall(function() return S.HttpService:JSONDecode(res) end)\
                if okJson and data and data.data then\
                    local valid = {}\
                    for _, s in ipairs(data.data) do\
                        local plrs = tonumber(s.playing) or 0\
                        local maxP = tonumber(s.maxPlayers) or 12\
                        if s.id ~= currentJobId and plrs > 0 and plrs < maxP then table.insert(valid, s) end\
                    end\
                    if #valid > 0 then targetServer = valid[math.random(1, #valid)] end\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        if targetServer and targetServer.id then\
            task.wait(0.5)\
            pcall(function() S.TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, targetServer.id, LP) end)\
            return true\
        end\
    \
        pcall(function() S.TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, LP) end)\
    end\
    \
    task.spawn(function()\
        while true do\
            task.wait(3)\
            pcall(function()\
                if ShopCfg.autoUpgradePlot then doAutoUpgradePlot() end\
                if ShopCfg.autoUpgradeTread then doAutoUpgradeTreadmill() end\
                if ShopCfg.autoFuse then doFuseOnce() end\
                if MiscCfg.autoHop then\
                    local delaySec = (MiscCfg.hopDelay or 30) * 60\
                    if tick() - HopState.lastHopCheck >= delaySec then\
                        HopState.lastHopCheck = tick()\
                        executeServerHop(MiscCfg.hopMethod)\
                    end\
                end\
            end)\
        end\
    end)\
    \
    -- [13] FARM LOOP (TAB AUTO)\
    local StandaloneAntiBossWatcherStarted = false\
    local function startStandaloneAntiBossWatcher()\
        if StandaloneAntiBossWatcherStarted then return end\
        StandaloneAntiBossWatcherStarted = true\
    \
        task.spawn(function()\
            local wasCarrying = false\
            while true do\
                task.wait(0.05)\
                local standaloneEnabled = Cfg.antiBoss and not Cfg.autoFarm and not EventCfg.autoFuseEvent\
                if not standaloneEnabled then\
                    wasCarrying = false\
                    continue\
                end\
                local carrying = false\
                pcall(function() carrying = isCarryingEgg() end)\
                if carrying and not wasCarrying then\
                    triggerAntiBossSpamTeleport()\
                end\
                wasCarrying = carrying\
            end\
        end)\
    end\
    \
    attemptPickup = function(target, shouldContinue, deferEscape)\
        -- Shared pickup engine.\
        -- deferEscape=false/nil: original behavior (Fuse Event etc.)\
        -- deferEscape=true: return as soon as carry is confirmed; caller owns the\
        -- carry -> Safezone/drop-recovery lifecycle.\
        local function pickupActive()\
            if shouldContinue then\
                local ok, result = pcall(shouldContinue)\
                return ok and result == true\
            end\
            return Cfg.autoFarm\
        end\
    \
        local function carryConfirmed()\
            if deferEscape then\
                return true\
            end\
            instantCarryEscape()\
            return true\
        end\
    \
        local walkTarget = target.pos\
        local prompt = getEggPrompt(target.pos)\
        if prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent:IsA(\"BasePart\") then\
            walkTarget = prompt.Parent.Position\
        end\
    \
        applyInstantToAll()\
        local pickStart = tick()\
        local PICK_TIMEOUT = 2.0\
    \
        while tick() - pickStart < PICK_TIMEOUT and pickupActive() do\
            local root = getRoot()\
            if root and (root.Position - walkTarget).Magnitude > 6 then\
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(walkTarget + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))\
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero\
            end\
    \
            if isCarryingEgg() then return carryConfirmed() end\
    \
            local eggStateSuccess = false\
            pcall(function()\
                local rs = S.ReplicatedStorage\
                local EggState = require(rs.Client.EggState)\
                if EggState and EggState.CarryFieldEgg then\
                    local res = EggState.CarryFieldEgg(target.uid, target.slotKey)\
                    if res == true then eggStateSuccess = true end\
                end\
            end)\
    \
            if eggStateSuccess or isCarryingEgg() then return carryConfirmed() end\
    \
            S.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()\
            if isCarryingEgg() then return carryConfirmed() end\
    \
            local remoteSuccess = false\
            if RMT.CarryEgg then\
                pcall(function()\
                    local res = RMT.CarryEgg:InvokeServer({\
                        FirstAreaSlotKey = target.slotKey,\
                        Uid = target.uid\
                    })\
                    if res == true\
                        or (type(res) == \"table\"\
                            and (res.success == true or res.Success == true))\
                    then\
                        remoteSuccess = true\
                    end\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            if remoteSuccess or isCarryingEgg() then return carryConfirmed() end\
    \
            if prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt:IsDescendantOf(S.Workspace) then\
                if Cfg.instantSteal then\
                    pcall(function() prompt.HoldDuration = 0 end)\
                end\
                pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(prompt) end)\
            else\
                prompt = getEggPrompt(walkTarget)\
            end\
    \
            local confirmStart = tick()\
            while tick() - confirmStart < 0.20 and pickupActive() do\
                if isCarryingEgg() then return carryConfirmed() end\
                S.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()\
            end\
    \
            if not target.model\
                or not target.model.Parent\
                or not target.model:IsDescendantOf(S.Workspace)\
            then\
                local finalCheck = tick()\
                while tick() - finalCheck < 0.20 and pickupActive() do\
                    if isCarryingEgg() then return carryConfirmed() end\
                    S.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()\
                end\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            S.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()\
        end\
    \
        if isCarryingEgg() then return carryConfirmed() end\
        return false\
    end\
    \
    -- ============================================================\
    -- [MAIN FARM TARGET-LOCK ENGINE]\
    -- Scanner/filter remains the normal Auto Farm scanner.\
    -- Once eggs[1] is chosen, that egg stays the objective until:\
    --   A) it is successfully deposited, or\
    --   B) it genuinely disappears for a replication grace period.\
    -- A guard knock/drop does NOT restart at Safezone.\
    -- ============================================================\
    local MainFarmLock = {\
        target = nil,\
        lostSince = nil,\
        initialSafezoneDone = false,\
    }\
    \
    local function _farmTargetPart(model)\
        if not model then return nil end\
        return model:FindFirstChild(\"Hitbox\")\
            or model:FindFirstChild(\"CustomBoundingBox\")\
            or model.PrimaryPart\
            or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(\"BasePart\")\
    end\
    \
    local function _updateLockedTargetFromModel(lock, model)\
        if not lock or not model then return false end\
        local part = _farmTargetPart(model)\
        if not part then return false end\
    \
        lock.target.model = model\
        lock.target.pos = part.Position\
        lock.target.uid = lock.target.uid or model.Name\
        lock.target.slotKey = tostring(\
            model:GetAttribute(\"FirstAreaSlotKey\")\
            or model:GetAttribute(\"SlotKey\")\
            or lock.target.slotKey\
            or model.Name\
        )\
        lock.lastKnownPos = part.Position\
        lock.lostSince = nil\
        return true\
    end\
    \
    local function refreshLockedMainFarmTarget(lock)\
        if not lock or not lock.target then return false end\
        local target = lock.target\
    \
        -- 1) Existing exact model.\
        if target.model\
            and target.model.Parent\
            and target.model:IsDescendantOf(S.Workspace)\
        then\
            if _updateLockedTargetFromModel(lock, target.model) then\
                return true\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- 2) Exact UID in the usual containers.\
        if target.uid then\
            local uid = tostring(target.uid)\
            local aesc = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"AreaEggSlotsClient\")\
            local exact = (aesc and aesc:FindFirstChild(uid))\
                or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(uid)\
                or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(uid, true)\
    \
            if exact and exact:IsA(\"Model\") and _updateLockedTargetFromModel(lock, exact) then\
                return true\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- 3) Exact UID from EggState. This catches Slot -> Carry -> Dropped state\
        -- transitions even if the Workspace model is replaced.\
        local exactRecordFound = false\
        pcall(function()\
            local EggState = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)\
            local snap = EggState.ReadFieldEggs()\
            if snap and snap.Records then\
                for _, rec in ipairs(snap.Records) do\
                    if tostring(rec.Uid) == tostring(target.uid) then\
                        local model = S.Workspace.AreaEggSlotsClient:FindFirstChild(rec.Uid)\
                            or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(rec.Uid)\
                            or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(rec.Uid, true)\
    \
                        local pos = rec.BottomCFrame and rec.BottomCFrame.Position\
                        if model then\
                            local part = _farmTargetPart(model)\
                            if part then pos = part.Position end\
                        end\
    \
                        if pos then\
                            target.model = model\
                            target.pos = pos\
                            target.slotKey = tostring(rec.NestId or rec.Uid)\
                            target.category = tostring(rec.AssetCategory or target.category or \"Egg\")\
                            lock.lastKnownPos = pos\
                            lock.lostSince = nil\
                            exactRecordFound = true\
                        end\
                        break\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
        end)\
    \
        if exactRecordFound then\
            return true\
        end\
    \
        -- 4) Guard-drop fallback: find a dropped egg of the SAME category very near\
        -- the player / previous egg location. This intentionally does not choose a\
        -- random distant egg of the same category.\
        local root = getRoot()\
        local anchor = root and root.Position or lock.lastKnownPos or target.pos\
        local wanted = tostring(target.category or \"\"):lower()\
        local bestModel = nil\
        local bestDist = math.huge\
    \
        local function consider(model)\
            if not model or not model:IsA(\"Model\") or not isDroppedEggModel(model) then return end\
    \
            local cat = tostring(model:GetAttribute(\"AssetCategory\") or model.Name):lower()\
            if wanted ~= \"\" and not cat:find(wanted, 1, true) and not wanted:find(cat, 1, true) then\
                return\
            end\
    \
            local part = _farmTargetPart(model)\
            if not part or not anchor then return end\
    \
            local d = (part.Position - anchor).Magnitude\
            if d <= 55 and d < bestDist then\
                bestDist = d\
                bestModel = model\
            end\
        end\
    \
        for _, child in ipairs(S.Workspace:GetChildren()) do\
            consider(child)\
        end\
    \
        local objects = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"__OBJECTS\")\
        if objects then\
            for _, d in ipairs(objects:GetDescendants()) do\
                if d:IsA(\"Model\") then consider(d) end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        if bestModel and _updateLockedTargetFromModel(lock, bestModel) then\
            -- A dropped replacement can have a new model name/uid. The objective is\
            -- still the same physical/category egg, so update the lock to it.\
            target.uid = bestModel.Name\
            return true\
        end\
    \
        return false\
    end\
    \
    local function mainFarmCarryEscapeLocked(lock, pickupConfirmed)\
        if not Cfg.autoFarm then\
            return false, \"stopped\"\
        end\
    \
        -- IMPORTANT:\
        -- CarryFieldEgg / AskFieldEggCarry can return success one or more frames\
        -- BEFORE isCarryingEgg() reflects the new character state.\
        --\
        -- Fuse Event already handles this correctly: a confirmed pickup immediately\
        -- runs instantCarryEscape(). Main Auto Farm must do the same.\
        local carryDetected = isCarryingEgg()\
        local carryConfirmed = pickupConfirmed == true or carryDetected\
    \
        if not carryConfirmed then\
            return false, \"lost\"\
        end\
    \
        UpdateStatus(\
            Cfg.antiBoss\
                and \"CARRY CONFIRMED -> ANTI BOSS -> SAFEZONE\"\
                or \"CARRY CONFIRMED -> SAFEZONE\"\
        )\
    \
        -- EXACT proven lifecycle used by the Fuse Event:\
        -- carry confirmation -> Anti Boss bypass (when enabled)\
        -- -> WalkToTarget(SAFEZONE_POS) -> double unequip.\
        --\
        -- Do NOT require isCarryingEgg() to already be true here; server pickup\
        -- confirmation is enough to start the escape immediately.\
        local okEscape = false\
        pcall(function()\
            okEscape = instantCarryEscape() == true\
        end)\
    \
        -- Give Safezone / EggInventory replication a short moment.\
        local settleUntil = tick() + 0.45\
        while Cfg.autoFarm and tick() < settleUntil do\
            local root = getRoot()\
            if root and (root.Position - SAFEZONE_POS).Magnitude <= 14 then\
                forceUnequipTools()\
            end\
            task.wait(0.05)\
        end\
    \
        local root = getRoot()\
        local inSafezone = root and (root.Position - SAFEZONE_POS).Magnitude <= 14\
    \
        -- Normal success:\
        -- instantCarryEscape already brought us to Safezone. Treat this target as\
        -- completed even if the local carry flag needs another frame to clear.\
        if inSafezone then\
            forceUnequipTools()\
            task.wait(0.05)\
            forceUnequipTools()\
            return true, \"deposited\"\
        end\
    \
        -- Rare recovery: character is still carrying but the first Safezone walk\
        -- was interrupted. Retry once WITHOUT tying movement to carry replication.\
        if Cfg.autoFarm and (isCarryingEgg() or pickupConfirmed) then\
            UpdateStatus(\"Carry escape retry -> Safezone\")\
    \
            WalkToTarget(\
                SAFEZONE_POS,\
                5,\
                false,\
                function()\
                    return Cfg.autoFarm\
                end\
            )\
    \
            forceUnequipTools()\
            task.wait(0.05)\
            forceUnequipTools()\
    \
            root = getRoot()\
            inSafezone = root and (root.Position - SAFEZONE_POS).Magnitude <= 14\
    \
            if inSafezone then\
                return true, \"deposited\"\
            end\
        end\
    \
        -- If a guard actually knocked the egg loose before Safezone, preserve the\
        -- target-lock behavior from V2.3.5.0 and reacquire the same dropped egg.\
        if not isCarryingEgg() and lock and refreshLockedMainFarmTarget(lock) then\
            return false, \"lost\"\
        end\
    \
        return okEscape and inSafezone, inSafezone and \"deposited\" or \"lost\"\
    end\
    \
    doFarmLoop = function()\
        if Runtime.farming then return end\
        Runtime.farming = true\
    \
        PlotRuntime.placeGeneration = PlotRuntime.placeGeneration + 1\
        PlotRuntime.placeOwner = nil\
    \
        MainFarmLock.target = nil\
        MainFarmLock.lostSince = nil\
        MainFarmLock.initialSafezoneDone = false\
    \
        UpdateStatus(\"Waiting for Character...\")\
        ensureCharacter(15)\
        UpdateStatus(\"Preparing target-lock engine...\")\
        buildAssetRarityCache()\
        task.wait(0.25)\
    \
        -- Same philosophy as the Fuse Event:\
        -- Safezone is an opening position, NOT a mandatory restart point for every scan.\
        if Cfg.autoFarm then\
            if isPlayerOnTreadmill() then\
                UpdateStatus(\"Start: releasing treadmill...\")\
                releaseTreadmill()\
                task.wait(0.10)\
            end\
    \
            -- ====================================================\
            -- MAIN FARM START SAFEZONE — ROUTE-SCOPED TREADMILL GUARD\
            -- ====================================================\
            -- IMPORTANT:\
            -- This watcher exists ONLY while the opening Safezone walk is active.\
            -- It is destroyed immediately after that route finishes, so it can never\
            -- interfere with \"Auto Treadmill (When Waiting)\" later in the farm loop.\
            local startSafezoneRouteActive = true\
            local lastStartTreadmillRelease = 0\
    \
            task.spawn(function()\
                while Cfg.autoFarm and startSafezoneRouteActive do\
                    local onTreadmill = false\
    \
                    pcall(function()\
                        onTreadmill = isPlayerOnTreadmill() == true\
                    end)\
    \
                    if onTreadmill\
                        and tick() - lastStartTreadmillRelease >= 0.35\
                    then\
                        lastStartTreadmillRelease = tick()\
    \
                        UpdateStatus(\"Start -> treadmill caught, releasing...\")\
                        releaseTreadmill()\
    \
                        -- Give AskDoff / presentation controller a moment to detach.\
                        -- WalkToTarget keeps owning the Safezone destination and will\
                        -- naturally continue issuing MoveTo after this.\
                        task.wait(0.10)\
                    else\
                        task.wait(0.06)\
                    end\
                end\
            end)\
    \
            UpdateStatus(\"Start -> Safezone once\")\
            WalkToTarget(SAFEZONE_POS, 5)\
    \
            -- Kill the temporary guard BEFORE normal scanning / waiting logic begins.\
            startSafezoneRouteActive = false\
            MainFarmLock.initialSafezoneDone = true\
        end\
    \
        while Cfg.autoFarm do\
            if isPlayerInBossArena() then\
                task.wait(0.5)\
                continue\
            end\
    \
            local curChar = LP.Character\
            local curRoot = getRoot()\
            local curHum  = getHumanoid()\
    \
            if not curChar\
                or not curRoot\
                or not curHum\
                or not curRoot.Parent\
                or curHum.Health <= 0\
            then\
                UpdateStatus(\"Respawning...\")\
                ensureCharacter(10)\
                task.wait(0.35)\
                continue\
            end\
    \
            -- ====================================================\
            -- A) ACQUIRE A NEW TARGET ONLY WHEN NO TARGET IS LOCKED\
            -- ====================================================\
            if not MainFarmLock.target then\
                -- Existing unplaced eggs can still be handled before the next field egg.\
                if PlotCfg.autoPlace then\
                    local unplaced = getUnplacedEggs()\
                    if #unplaced > 0 then\
                        UpdateStatus(\"Placing secured eggs...\")\
                        PlotRuntime.placeGeneration = PlotRuntime.placeGeneration + 1\
                        local farmPlotGeneration = PlotRuntime.placeGeneration\
    \
                        doPlotPlaceOnce(\"farm\", farmPlotGeneration)\
    \
                        if Cfg.autoFarm then\
                            UpdateStatus(\"Returning to scan position...\")\
                            WalkToTarget(SAFEZONE_POS, 5)\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                UpdateStatus(\"Scanning...\")\
                local eggs = nil\
                local scanDone = false\
    \
                scanEggsAsync(function(result)\
                    eggs = result\
                    scanDone = true\
                end)\
    \
                local waitStart = tick()\
                while Cfg.autoFarm and not scanDone and tick() - waitStart < 5 do\
                    task.wait(0.05)\
                end\
    \
                if not Cfg.autoFarm then break end\
    \
                if not eggs or #eggs == 0 then\
                    if Cfg.autoTreadmill then\
                        local penInfo = findMyPlot()\
                        local tb = penInfo\
                            and penInfo.plot\
                            and penInfo.plot:FindFirstChild(\"TreadmillBottom\")\
    \
                        if tb then\
                            UpdateStatus(\"No eggs: Treadmill waiting...\")\
                            WalkToTarget(tb.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), 4)\
    \
                            while Cfg.autoFarm and Cfg.autoTreadmill do\
                                task.wait(0.8)\
    \
                                local innerEggs = nil\
                                local innerDone = false\
                                scanEggsAsync(function(res)\
                                    innerEggs = res\
                                    innerDone = true\
                                end)\
    \
                                local w = tick()\
                                while Cfg.autoFarm and not innerDone and tick() - w < 4 do\
                                    task.wait(0.05)\
                                end\
    \
                                if innerEggs and #innerEggs > 0 then\
                                    eggs = innerEggs\
                                    break\
                                end\
    \
                                UpdateStatus(\"Treadmill: scanning...\")\
                            end\
                        end\
                    else\
                        UpdateStatus(\"No eggs found...\")\
                        task.wait(1.0)\
                    end\
    \
                    if not eggs or #eggs == 0 then\
                        continue\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                -- Egg found while using treadmill: release and go DIRECTLY to the egg.\
                -- Do not insert a Safezone trip here.\
                if isPlayerOnTreadmill() then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"Egg detected -> releasing treadmill\")\
                    releaseTreadmill()\
                    task.wait(0.12)\
                end\
    \
                local target = eggs[1]\
    \
                MainFarmLock.target = {\
                    uid          = target.uid,\
                    model        = target.model,\
                    pos          = target.pos,\
                    zone         = target.zone,\
                    rarity       = target.rarity,\
