                    rarityNum    = target.rarityNum,\
                    category     = target.category,\
                    weight       = target.weight,\
                    mutations    = target.mutations,\
                    baseMutation = target.baseMutation,\
                    slotKey      = target.slotKey,\
                    score        = target.score,\
                    dist         = target.dist,\
                    isDropped    = target.isDropped,\
                }\
                MainFarmLock.lastKnownPos = target.pos\
                MainFarmLock.lostSince = nil\
    \
                UpdateStatus(string.format(\
                    \"TARGET LOCKED: [%s] %s | %s\",\
                    tostring(target.rarity or \"?\"),\
                    tostring(target.category or \"Egg\"),\
                    tostring(target.zone or \"Unknown\")\
                ))\
            end\
    \
            local lock = MainFarmLock\
            local target = lock.target\
            if not target then\
                task.wait(0.03)\
                continue\
            end\
    \
            -- ====================================================\
            -- B) IF CARRY EXISTS, COMPLETE OR RECOVER SAME TARGET\
            -- ====================================================\
            if isCarryingEgg() then\
                local deposited, reason = mainFarmCarryEscapeLocked(lock)\
    \
                if deposited then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"Locked egg deposited -> next target\")\
                    MainFarmLock.target = nil\
                    MainFarmLock.lostSince = nil\
    \
                    if PlotCfg.autoPlace then\
                        local unplaced = getUnplacedEggs()\
                        if #unplaced > 0 then\
                            PlotRuntime.placeGeneration = PlotRuntime.placeGeneration + 1\
                            doPlotPlaceOnce(\"farm\", PlotRuntime.placeGeneration)\
                            if Cfg.autoFarm then\
                                WalkToTarget(SAFEZONE_POS, 5)\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end\
    \
                    if ShopCfg.autoSellAll then\
                        doSellCycle()\
                    end\
    \
                    task.wait(0.05)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                -- Guard knock: do NOT return to Safezone and do NOT release objective.\
                UpdateStatus(\"Egg dropped -> re-locking SAME egg...\")\
                lock.lostSince = nil\
                refreshLockedMainFarmTarget(lock)\
                task.wait(0.05)\
                continue\
            end\
    \
            -- ====================================================\
            -- C) KEEP THE SAME TARGET UPDATED THROUGH DROP/RESPAWN\
            -- ====================================================\
            local targetAlive = refreshLockedMainFarmTarget(lock)\
    \
            if not targetAlive then\
                lock.lostSince = lock.lostSince or tick()\
    \
                if tick() - lock.lostSince < 1.75 then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"Locked egg missing -> waiting drop replication...\")\
                    task.wait(0.08)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                -- Only now may the objective be released.\
                UpdateStatus(\"Locked egg truly gone -> new scan\")\
                MainFarmLock.target = nil\
                MainFarmLock.lostSince = nil\
                ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
                ScanCache.eggs = {}\
                task.wait(0.05)\
                continue\
            end\
    \
            target = lock.target\
            local prompt = getEggPrompt(target.pos)\
            local walkTarget = (prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent:IsA(\"BasePart\"))\
                and prompt.Parent.Position\
                or target.pos\
    \
            UpdateStatus(string.format(\
                \"LOCKED -> %s [%s]\",\
                tostring(target.category or \"Egg\"),\
                tostring(target.rarity or \"?\")\
            ))\
    \
            forceUnequipTools()\
    \
            local reached = WalkToTarget(\
                walkTarget,\
                4,\
                true,\
                function()\
                    -- The same target remains owner of movement until carry or loss.\
                    return Cfg.autoFarm and not isCarryingEgg()\
                end\
            )\
    \
            if not Cfg.autoFarm then break end\
    \
            -- A prompt/server interaction can grant carry on the final movement frame.\
            if isCarryingEgg() then\
                local deposited = mainFarmCarryEscapeLocked(lock)\
                if deposited then\
                    MainFarmLock.target = nil\
                    MainFarmLock.lostSince = nil\
                else\
                    UpdateStatus(\"Carry lost -> recovering locked egg...\")\
                    refreshLockedMainFarmTarget(lock)\
                end\
                task.wait(0.04)\
                continue\
            end\
    \
            -- If the model changed while walking, refresh it instead of abandoning it.\
            if not reached then\
                if refreshLockedMainFarmTarget(lock) then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"Target moved/dropped -> continuing lock...\")\
                    task.wait(0.04)\
                    continue\
                end\
            end\
    \
            UpdateStatus(\"Stealing LOCKED egg...\")\
    \
            -- Same proven shared pickup engine, but main farm owns the carry lifecycle.\
            local carried = attemptPickup(\
                target,\
                function()\
                    return Cfg.autoFarm\
                end,\
                true\
            )\
    \
            if not Cfg.autoFarm then break end\
    \
            if carried or isCarryingEgg() then\
                -- `carried` is authoritative pickup success from CarryFieldEgg /\
                -- AskFieldEggCarry and may arrive before isCarryingEgg() replication.\
                local deposited = mainFarmCarryEscapeLocked(lock, carried == true)\
    \
                if deposited then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"Locked egg secured!\")\
                    MainFarmLock.target = nil\
                    MainFarmLock.lostSince = nil\
    \
                    if PlotCfg.autoPlace then\
                        local unplaced = getUnplacedEggs()\
                        if #unplaced > 0 then\
                            PlotRuntime.placeGeneration = PlotRuntime.placeGeneration + 1\
                            doPlotPlaceOnce(\"farm\", PlotRuntime.placeGeneration)\
    \
                            if Cfg.autoFarm then\
                                WalkToTarget(SAFEZONE_POS, 5)\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end\
    \
                    if ShopCfg.autoSellAll then\
                        doSellCycle()\
                    end\
                else\
                    UpdateStatus(\"Guard dropped egg -> SAME target retained\")\
                    lock.lostSince = nil\
                    refreshLockedMainFarmTarget(lock)\
                end\
    \
                task.wait(0.04)\
                continue\
            end\
    \
            -- Pickup failed without carry: retry the same object/category first.\
            if refreshLockedMainFarmTarget(lock) then\
                UpdateStatus(\"Pickup retry -> SAME locked egg\")\
                task.wait(0.05)\
                continue\
            end\
    \
            lock.lostSince = lock.lostSince or tick()\
            task.wait(0.05)\
        end\
    \
        MainFarmLock.target = nil\
        MainFarmLock.lostSince = nil\
        MainFarmLock.initialSafezoneDone = false\
    \
        Runtime.farming = false\
        UpdateStatus(\"Idle\")\
    end\
    \
    -- ============================================================\
    -- [FAST FARM METHOD — VIDEO FLOW]\
    --\
    -- NORMAL ENGINE ABOVE IS LOCKED / UNCHANGED.\
    --\
    -- Flow:\
    --   independent target scanner\
    --   -> exact selected target snapshot\
    --   -> Fast-only character bypass\
    --   -> direct TP random Forest primer egg\
    --   -> instant carry -> instant drop\
    --   -> TPWalk exact original target\
    --   -> exact carry\
    --   -> TPWalk directly to Safezone\
    --   -> descend / deposit\
    --   -> scanner keeps running for next target\
    -- ============================================================\
    -- ============================================================\
    -- [FAST FARM — NATURAL FOREST STRIKE + IMMEDIATE TPWALK]\
    --\
    -- Per cycle:\
    --   0. Scanner lock target utama\
    --   1. Apply humanoid bypass BEFORE going to Forest\
    --   2. TP ke egg Forest → carry egg Forest\
    --   3. Fire ONLY natural ForestStrike:\
    --      {EggUid = FOREST_UID, GuardCFrame = LIVE_FOREST_GUARD_CFRAME}\
    --   4. DO NOT WAIT for ragdoll / stand-up / Handoff\
    --      Immediately TPWalk very fast toward locked main target\
    --   5. At target: spam exact CarryFieldEgg(target.uid)\
    --      until authoritative target record confirms carried\
    --   6. Carry confirmed → fly + TPWalk to SAFEZONE_POS → deposit → loop\
    -- ============================================================\
    local FastFarm = (function()\
    \
        local State = {\
            active             = false,\
            generation         = 0,\
            scannerRunning     = false,\
            workerRunning      = false,\
            latestTarget       = nil,\
            busyUid            = nil,\
            ignoreUidUntilGone = nil,\
            bypassedChar       = nil,\
        }\
    \
        local FAST_SCAN_POLL    = 0.12\
        local FAST_FLY_HEIGHT   = 35\
        local FAST_TP_OFFSET    = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- [BYPASS HUMANOID] — harus jalan sebelum TP ke tumbal\
        -- Clone humanoid tanpa animator supaya server-side collision\
        -- check bypass (karakter tidak bisa di-ragdoll oleh guard)\
        -- ============================================================\
        local function applyBypass(char)\
            if not char then return false end\
            -- Sudah bypass kalau _OldHumanoid ada\
            if char:FindFirstChild(\"_OldHumanoid\") then return true end\
    \
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"Humanoid\")\
            if not hum then return false end\
    \
            local ok = pcall(function()\
                local cam   = workspace.CurrentCamera\
                local anim  = char:FindFirstChild(\"Animate\")\
                local ws    = hum.WalkSpeed\
                local jp    = hum.JumpPower\
                local jh    = hum.JumpHeight\
                local hp    = hum.Health\
                local mhp   = hum.MaxHealth\
    \
                if anim and anim:IsA(\"LocalScript\") then\
                    anim.Disabled = true\
                end\
    \
                local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"Animator\")\
                if animator then\
                    for _, track in next, animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() do\
                        track:Stop(0)\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                hum.Archivable = true\
                local cloned   = hum:Clone()\
    \
                for _, child in next, cloned:GetChildren() do\
                    if child:IsA(\"Animator\") then child:Destroy() end\
                end\
    \
                hum.Name    = \"_OldHumanoid\"\
                cloned.Name = \"Humanoid\"\
                cloned.Parent = char\
    \
                local newAnim = Instance.new(\"Animator\")\
                newAnim.Parent = cloned\
    \
                cloned.WalkSpeed = ws\
                cloned.JumpPower = jp\
                cloned.JumpHeight = jh\
                cloned.MaxHealth = mhp\
                cloned.Health    = math.min(hp, mhp)\
    \
                if cam then cam.CameraSubject = cloned end\
                hum:Destroy()\
    \
                if anim and anim:IsA(\"LocalScript\") then\
                    task.wait()\
                    if anim.Parent then\
                        anim.Disabled = false\
                        task.defer(function()\
                            if anim.Parent then\
                                anim.Disabled = true\
                                task.wait()\
                                if anim.Parent then anim.Disabled = false end\
                            end\
                        end)\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                task.defer(function()\
                    if cloned.Parent then\
                        cloned:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)\
                    end\
                end)\
            end)\
    \
            return ok\
        end\
    \
        local function ensureBypass()\
            local char = LP.Character\
            if not char then return false end\
            if State.bypassedChar == char then\
                -- Masih char yang sama; cek bypass masih aktif\
                if char:FindFirstChild(\"_OldHumanoid\") then return true end\
            end\
            local ok = applyBypass(char)\
            if ok then State.bypassedChar = char end\
            return ok\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- REMOTE HELPERS\
        -- ============================================================\
        local _rForestStrike  = nil\
        local _rForestHandoff = nil\
    \
        local function getForestRemotes()\
            local pkg = S.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(\"Packages\")\
            local net = pkg and pkg:FindFirstChild(\"Networking\")\
            if not net then return nil, nil end\
    \
            if not (_rForestStrike and _rForestStrike.Parent) then\
                _rForestStrike = net:FindFirstChild(\"RE/GuardPatrol/ForestStrike\")\
            end\
            if not (_rForestHandoff and _rForestHandoff.Parent) then\
                _rForestHandoff = net:FindFirstChild(\"RE/GuardPatrol/ForestHandoff\")\
            end\
            return _rForestStrike, _rForestHandoff\
        end\
    \
        -- Ambil CFrame Forest guard yang LIVE.\
        -- Natural ForestGuardRuntime sends p37.GuardRoot.CFrame.\
        local function getForestGuardCF()\
            local bestCF = nil\
            local bestScore = -math.huge\
    \
            for _, obj in ipairs(S.Workspace:GetDescendants()) do\
                if obj:IsA(\"Model\") then\
                    local root = obj:FindFirstChild(\"HumanoidRootPart\")\
                        or (obj.PrimaryPart and obj.PrimaryPart.Parent and obj.PrimaryPart)\
    \
                    if root and root:IsA(\"BasePart\") then\
                        local area = tostring(obj:GetAttribute(\"AreaId\") or \"\")\
                        local name = obj.Name:lower()\
                        local score = 0\
    \
                        if area:lower() == \"forest\" then score = score + 50 end\
                        if name:find(\"guard\", 1, true) then score = score + 100 end\
                        if name:find(\"forest\", 1, true) then score = score + 20 end\
    \
                        if ZONES.Forest then\
                            local d = (root.Position - ZONES.Forest).Magnitude\
                            if d <= 160 then\
                                score = score + math.max(0, 40 - d * 0.15)\
                            else\
                                score = score - 100\
                            end\
                        end\
    \
                        if score > bestScore then\
                            bestScore = score\
                            bestCF = root.CFrame\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            if bestCF then return bestCF end\
            return CFrame.new(596.615, 69.418, -317.116)\
        end\
    \
        -- Fire ONLY the natural hit request. The natural game handles\
        -- Handoff later through its own retrieval state machine.\
        local function fireNaturalForestStrike(forestUid)\
            local rStrike = select(1, getForestRemotes())\
            if not rStrike or not forestUid then return false end\
    \
            local guardCF = getForestGuardCF()\
            local ok = pcall(function()\
                rStrike:FireServer({\
                    EggUid = tostring(forestUid),\
                    GuardCFrame = guardCF,\
                })\
            end)\
            return ok\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- CARRY DETECTION — robust: cek tool DAN server record\
        -- ============================================================\
        local function isCarryingAnyEgg()\
            -- Cek tool di character\
            local char = LP.Character\
            if char then\
                for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do\
                    if obj:IsA(\"Tool\") and obj.Name:lower():find(\"egg\") then\
                        return true\
                    end\
                end\
                -- Cek attributes\
                if char:GetAttribute(\"CarryingEgg\")\
                    or char:GetAttribute(\"IsCarrying\")\
                    or char:GetAttribute(\"HoldingEgg\")\
                then\
                    return true\
                end\
            end\
            return false\
        end\
    \
        local function isCarryingUid(uid)\
            -- Cek server record: apakah UID ini di-carry oleh player kita\
            local EggState = nil\
            pcall(function()\
                EggState = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)\
            end)\
            if not EggState then return isCarryingAnyEgg() end\
    \
            if EggState.ReadFieldEgg then\
                local ok, rec = pcall(function() return EggState.ReadFieldEgg(uid) end)\
                if ok and type(rec) == \"table\" then\
                    if rec.State == \"Carried\"\
                        and tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId) == tonumber(LP.UserId)\
                    then\
                        return true\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            if EggState.ReadFieldEggs then\
                local ok, snap = pcall(function() return EggState.ReadFieldEggs() end)\
                if ok and snap and snap.Records then\
                    for _, rec in ipairs(snap.Records) do\
                        if tostring(rec.Uid) == tostring(uid) then\
                            if rec.State == \"Carried\"\
                                and tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId) == tonumber(LP.UserId)\
                            then\
                                return true\
                            end\
                            break\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return isCarryingAnyEgg()\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- RECORD HELPERS\
        -- ============================================================\
        local function alive(gen)\
            return State.active and gen == State.generation and Cfg.autoFarm\
        end\
    \
        local function shallowCopy(t)\
            if type(t) ~= \"table\" then return nil end\
            local c = {}; for k, v in pairs(t) do c[k] = v end; return c\
        end\
    \
        local function resolveSlotKey(rec, fallback)\
            if type(rec) == \"table\" then\
                local k = rec.NestId or rec.SlotKey or rec.FirstAreaSlotKey\
                if k and tostring(k) ~= \"\" then return tostring(k) end\
            end\
            return fallback and fallback.slotKey or nil\
        end\
    \
        local function getEggState()\
            local es = nil\
            pcall(function() es = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState) end)\
            return es\
        end\
    \
        local function readExactRecord(uid)\
            if not uid then return nil end\
            local ES = getEggState()\
            if not ES then return nil end\
            if ES.ReadFieldEgg then\
                local ok, r = pcall(function() return ES.ReadFieldEgg(uid) end)\
                if ok and type(r) == \"table\" then return r end\
            end\
            if ES.ReadFieldEggs then\
                local ok, snap = pcall(function() return ES.ReadFieldEggs() end)\
                if ok and snap and snap.Records then\
                    for _, rec in ipairs(snap.Records) do\
                        if tostring(rec.Uid) == tostring(uid) then return rec end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
            return nil\
        end\
    \
        local function getPos(rec, fallback)\
            if type(rec) ~= \"table\" then return fallback end\
            for _, cf in ipairs({rec.BottomCFrame, rec.WorldCFrame, rec.CFrame}) do\
                if typeof(cf) == \"CFrame\" then return cf.Position end\
            end\
            local uid = rec.Uid and tostring(rec.Uid) or nil\
            if uid then\
                local aesc = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"AreaEggSlotsClient\")\
                local model = (aesc and aesc:FindFirstChild(uid))\
                    or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(uid)\
                if model then\
                    local part = model:FindFirstChild(\"Hitbox\")\
                        or model:FindFirstChild(\"CustomBoundingBox\")\
                        or model.PrimaryPart\
                        or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(\"BasePart\")\
                    if part then return part.Position end\
                end\
            end\
            return fallback\
        end\
    \
        local function refreshTarget(target)\
            if type(target) ~= \"table\" or not target.uid then return nil end\
            local rec = readExactRecord(target.uid)\
            if rec then\
                if rec.State == \"Claimed\" or rec.State == \"GuardCarried\" then return nil end\
                if rec.State == \"Carried\"\
                    and rec.CarrierUserId ~= nil\
                    and tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId) ~= tonumber(LP.UserId)\
                then\
                    return nil\
                end\
                target.pos      = getPos(rec, target.pos)\
                target.slotKey  = resolveSlotKey(rec, target)\
                target.zone     = tostring(rec.AreaId or target.zone or \"Unknown\")\
                target.category = tostring(rec.AssetCategory or target.category or \"Egg\")\
            end\
            return target\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- SCAN TUMBAL — egg Forest apapun selain target utama\
        -- ============================================================\
        local function findSacrificeEgg(excludeUid)\
            local ES = getEggState()\
            if not ES or not ES.ReadFieldEggs then return nil end\
    \
            local ok, snap = pcall(function() return ES.ReadFieldEggs() end)\
            local records  = ok and snap and snap.Records\
            if type(records) ~= \"table\" then return nil end\
    \
            for _, rec in ipairs(records) do\
                if type(rec) == \"table\"\
                    and rec.Uid\
                    and tostring(rec.Uid) ~= tostring(excludeUid or \"\")\
                    and (rec.State == nil or rec.State == \"Slot\" or rec.State == \"Dropped\")\
                then\
                    local area  = tostring(rec.AreaId or \"\")\
                    local pos   = getPos(rec, nil)\
                    local isF   = area:lower() == \"forest\"\
                        or (pos and ZONES.Forest and (pos - ZONES.Forest).Magnitude <= 230)\
    \
                    if isF and pos then\
                        local sk = resolveSlotKey(rec, nil)\
                        if sk then\
                            return {\
                                uid      = tostring(rec.Uid),\
                                slotKey  = sk,\
                                pos      = pos,\
                                category = tostring(rec.AssetCategory or \"Egg\"),\
                            }\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
            return nil\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- CARRY REQUEST — robust: EggState + remote + server record check\
        -- ============================================================\
        local function requestCarry(uid, slotKey, gen, timeout)\
            timeout = timeout or 2.5\
            local started = tick()\
            local ES = getEggState()\
    \
            while alive(gen) and tick() - started < timeout do\
                -- Early exit kalau sudah carry apapun atau UID ini\
                if isCarryingUid(uid) then return true end\
                if isCarryingAnyEgg() then return true end\
    \
                -- Coba EggState.CarryFieldEgg\
                local accepted = false\
                if ES and ES.CarryFieldEgg then\
                    pcall(function()\
                        local res = ES.CarryFieldEgg(uid, slotKey)\
                        if res == true\
                            or (type(res) == \"table\" and (res.success or res.Success))\
                        then\
                            accepted = true\
                        end\
                    end)\
                end\
    \
                -- Coba remote AskFieldEggCarry\
                if not accepted and RMT.CarryEgg then\
                    pcall(function()\
                        local res = RMT.CarryEgg:InvokeServer({\
                            Uid              = uid,\
                            FirstAreaSlotKey = slotKey,\
                        })\
                        if res == true\
                            or (type(res) == \"table\" and (res.success or res.Success))\
                        then\
                            accepted = true\
                        end\
                    end)\
                end\
    \
                -- Coba proximity prompt kalau ada\
                if not accepted then\
                    pcall(function()\
                        local aesc = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"AreaEggSlotsClient\")\
                        local model = (aesc and aesc:FindFirstChild(uid))\
                            or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(uid)\
                        if model then\
                            for _, desc in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do\
                                if desc:IsA(\"ProximityPrompt\") then\
                                    desc.HoldDuration = 0\
                                    fireproximityprompt(desc)\
                                    break\
                                end\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end)\
                end\
    \
                if accepted or isCarryingUid(uid) or isCarryingAnyEgg() then\
                    return true\
                end\
    \
                -- Server record final check\
                local rec = readExactRecord(uid)\
                if rec then\
                    if rec.State == \"Claimed\" or rec.State == \"GuardCarried\" then\
                        return false\
                    end\
                    if rec.State == \"Carried\"\
                        and tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId) == tonumber(LP.UserId)\
                    then\
                        return true\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                task.wait(0.05)\
            end\
    \
            return isCarryingUid(uid) or isCarryingAnyEgg()\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- EXACT MAIN TARGET CARRY\
        -- Only target.uid can complete this step.\
        -- ============================================================\
        local function requestCarryExactTarget(target, gen, timeout)\
            if type(target) ~= \"table\" or not target.uid then return false end\
            timeout = timeout or 4.0\
            local started = tick()\
    \
            while alive(gen) and tick() - started < timeout do\
                target = refreshTarget(target) or target\
                local uid = target.uid\
                local slotKey = target.slotKey\
    \
                local rec = readExactRecord(uid)\
                if rec\
                    and rec.State == \"Carried\"\
                    and tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId) == tonumber(LP.UserId)\
                then\
                    return true\
                end\
                if rec and (rec.State == \"Claimed\" or rec.State == \"GuardCarried\") then\
                    return false\
                end\
    \
                local ES = getEggState()\
                if ES and ES.CarryFieldEgg and slotKey then\
                    pcall(function() ES.CarryFieldEgg(uid, slotKey) end)\
                end\
    \
                if RMT.CarryEgg and slotKey then\
                    pcall(function()\
                        RMT.CarryEgg:InvokeServer({\
                            Uid = uid,\
                            FirstAreaSlotKey = slotKey,\
                        })\
                    end)\
                end\
    \
                pcall(function()\
                    local root = getRoot()\
                    if not root or not target.pos then return end\
                    if (root.Position - target.pos).Magnitude > 12 then return end\
                    local aesc = S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"AreaEggSlotsClient\")\
                    local model = (aesc and aesc:FindFirstChild(tostring(uid)))\
                        or S.Workspace:FindFirstChild(tostring(uid))\
                    if model then\
                        for _, desc in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do\
                            if desc:IsA(\"ProximityPrompt\") then\
                                desc.HoldDuration = 0\
                                fireproximityprompt(desc)\
                                break\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end\
                end)\
    \
                rec = readExactRecord(uid)\
                if rec\
                    and rec.State == \"Carried\"\
                    and tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId) == tonumber(LP.UserId)\
                then\
                    return true\
                end\
    \
                S.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()\
            end\
    \
            local rec = readExactRecord(target.uid)\
            return rec ~= nil\
                and rec.State == \"Carried\"\
                and tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId) == tonumber(LP.UserId)\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- MOVEMENT HELPERS\
        -- ============================================================\
            local function fastTPWalk(targetPos, stopDist, gen, speed)\
            stopDist = stopDist or 5\
            Runtime.isAutoWalking = true\
            local deadline = tick() + 30\
    \
            while alive(gen) and tick() < deadline do\
                local char, root, hum = ensureCharacter(3)\
                if not char or not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then\
                    Runtime.isAutoWalking = false; return false\
                end\
                local delta = targetPos - root.Position\
                local dist  = delta.Magnitude\
                if dist <= stopDist then\
                    pcall(function()\
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero\
                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero\
                    end)\
                    Runtime.isAutoWalking = false; return true\
                end\
                local dt = S.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()\
                if not alive(gen) then break end\
                if dist > 0.001 then\
                    local spd = tonumber(speed) or math.max(1, tonumber(Cfg.walkSpeed) or 35)\
                    local step = spd * dt * 10\
                    pcall(function()\
                        char:TranslateBy(delta.Unit * math.min(step, dist))\
                    end)\
                end\
            end\
    \
            Runtime.isAutoWalking = false; return false\
        end\
    \
        local function directTP(pos)\
            if typeof(pos) ~= \"Vector3\" then return false end\
            local char, root, hum = ensureCharacter(5)\
            if not char or not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end\
            Runtime.isAutoWalking = true\
            pcall(function()\
                hum:MoveTo(root.Position)\
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos + FAST_TP_OFFSET)\
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero\
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero\
            end)\
            Runtime.isAutoWalking = false\
            return true\
        end\
    \
        local function flyToSafezone(gen)\
            local char, root, hum = ensureCharacter(5)\
            if not char or not root or not hum then return false end\
    \
            if Cfg.antiBoss then\
                triggerAntiBossSpamTeleport()\
                waitForAntiBossTeleport()\
            end\
    \
            -- Naik dulu\
            pcall(function()\
                hum:MoveTo(root.Position)\
                root.CFrame = root.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, FAST_FLY_HEIGHT, 0)\
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero\
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero\
            end)\
    \
            -- TPWalk ke safezone di ketinggian yang sama\
            local airSz  = SAFEZONE_POS + Vector3.new(0, FAST_FLY_HEIGHT, 0)\
            local reached = fastTPWalk(airSz, 5, gen)\
            if not reached or not alive(gen) then return false end\
    \
            -- Turun ke safezone\
            root = getRoot()\
            if root then\
                pcall(function()\
                    if hum then hum:MoveTo(root.Position) end\
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(SAFEZONE_POS + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))\
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero\
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            stopAntiBossSpamTeleport()\
            forceUnequipTools(); task.wait(0.05); forceUnequipTools()\
            return true\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- SCANNER — independent, tidak owns movement\
        -- ============================================================\
        local function scanner(gen)\
            if State.scannerRunning then return end\
            State.scannerRunning = true\
    \
            while alive(gen) do\
                scanEggsAsync(function(eggs)\
                    if not alive(gen) then return end\
    \
                    local ignored    = State.ignoreUidUntilGone\
                    local ignoredGone = true\
                    local chosen     = nil\
    \
                    for _, egg in ipairs(eggs) do\
                        if egg and egg.uid then\
                            if ignored and tostring(egg.uid) == tostring(ignored) then\
                                ignoredGone = false\
                            elseif tostring(egg.uid) ~= tostring(State.busyUid or \"\") then\
                                if not chosen then chosen = shallowCopy(egg) end\
                            end\
                        end\
                    end\
    \
                    if ignored and ignoredGone then State.ignoreUidUntilGone = nil end\
                    if chosen then\
                        State.latestTarget = chosen\
                    elseif not State.busyUid then\
                        State.latestTarget = nil\
                    end\
                end)\
                task.wait(FAST_SCAN_POLL)\
            end\
    \
            State.scannerRunning = false\
        end\
    \
        local function waitForTarget(gen)\
            while alive(gen) do\
                local t = State.latestTarget\
                if t and t.uid and tostring(t.uid) ~= tostring(State.busyUid or \"\") then\
                    return shallowCopy(t)\
                end\
                UpdateStatus(\"FAST: scanning...\")\
                task.wait(0.05)\
            end\
            return nil\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- WORKER\
        -- ============================================================\
        local function worker(gen)\
            if State.workerRunning then return end\
            State.workerRunning = true\
            Runtime.farming     = true\
    \
            PlotRuntime.placeGeneration = PlotRuntime.placeGeneration + 1\
            PlotRuntime.placeOwner      = nil\
    \
            UpdateStatus(\"FAST: engine starting...\")\
            ensureCharacter(10)\
    \
            -- Bypass humanoid di awal session\
            UpdateStatus(\"FAST: applying bypass...\")\
            ensureBypass()\
    \
            while alive(gen) do\
    \
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                -- STEP 1: Lock target utama dari scanner\
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                local target = waitForTarget(gen)\
                if not target then break end\
    \
                target = refreshTarget(target)\
                if not target then\
                    State.latestTarget = nil; task.wait(0.05); continue\
                end\
    \
                State.busyUid      = tostring(target.uid)\
                State.latestTarget = nil\
    \
                UpdateStatus(string.format(\
                    \"FAST LOCK: [%s] %s | %s\",\
                    tostring(target.rarity   or \"?\"),\
                    tostring(target.category or \"Egg\"),\
                    tostring(target.zone     or \"Unknown\")\
                ))\
    \
                -- Pastikan bypass aktif sebelum mulai\
                ensureBypass()\
    \
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                -- STEP 2: Cari egg tumbal Forest\
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                local sacrifice = nil\
                local scanStart = tick()\
                while alive(gen) and not sacrifice and tick() - scanStart < 6 do\
                    sacrifice = findSacrificeEgg(target.uid)\
                    if not sacrifice then\
                        UpdateStatus(\"FAST: waiting Forest sacrifice egg...\")\
                        task.wait(0.3)\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                if not alive(gen) then break end\
                if not sacrifice then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"FAST: no Forest sacrifice → rescan main\")\
                    State.busyUid = nil; ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
                    task.wait(0.5); continue\
                end\
    \
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                -- STEP 3: TP ke tumbal → carry tumbal\
                -- Tidak ke safezone — langsung ke step remote setelah carry\
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                UpdateStatus(string.format(\
                    \"FAST: TP tumbal → %s\",\
                    tostring(sacrifice.category)\
                ))\
    \
                if not directTP(sacrifice.pos) then\
                    State.busyUid = nil; task.wait(0.05); continue\
                end\
    \
                if not alive(gen) then break end\
    \
                forceUnequipTools()\
    \
                -- Carry tumbal — pakai requestCarry yang robust\
                UpdateStatus(\"FAST: carrying sacrifice egg...\")\
                local sacCarried = requestCarry(\
                    sacrifice.uid,\
                    sacrifice.slotKey,\
                    gen,\
                    3.0\
                )\
    \
                if not alive(gen) then break end\
    \
                if not sacCarried then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"FAST: sacrifice carry failed → rescan\")\
                    State.busyUid = nil; State.latestTarget = nil\
                    ScanCache.lastScan = 0; task.wait(0.15); continue\
                end\
    \
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                -- STEP 4: Refresh locked target BEFORE strike\
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                target = refreshTarget(target)\
                if not target or not target.pos then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"FAST: target gone after Forest carry → rescan\")\
                    forceUnequipTools()\
                    State.busyUid = nil\
                    ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
                    task.wait(0.05)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                -- STEP 5: FIRE NATURAL FOREST STRIKE ONLY\
                -- Forest UID + live guard CFrame.\
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                UpdateStatus(\"FAST: ForestStrike -> immediate TPWalk\")\
                local strikeFired = fireNaturalForestStrike(sacrifice.uid)\
    \
                if not strikeFired then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"FAST: ForestStrike unavailable → retry\")\
                    State.busyUid = nil\
                    State.latestTarget = nil\
                    ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
                    task.wait(0.10)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                            -- --------------------------------------------------\
                -- STEP 6: IMMEDIATELY TPWALK TO LOCKED TARGET\
                -- NO wait for ragdoll, stand-up, CarryChanged or Handoff.\
                -- Speed 75 hardcoded — independent from Cfg.walkSpeed slider.\
                -- Character is mid-knockback; we fly through to main target fast.\
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                UpdateStatus(string.format(\
                    \"FAST: HIT -> TPWalk NOW → %s [%s]\",\
                    tostring(target.category or \"Egg\"),\
                    tostring(target.rarity or \"?\")\
                ))\
    \
                local reachedTarget = fastTPWalk(target.pos, 3, gen, 75)\
                if not reachedTarget or not alive(gen) then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"FAST: target TPWalk interrupted\")\
                    State.busyUid = nil\
                    State.latestTarget = nil\
                    ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
                    task.wait(0.05)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                target = refreshTarget(target)\
                if not target then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"FAST: target vanished on arrival → rescan\")\
                    State.busyUid = nil\
                    State.latestTarget = nil\
                    ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
                    task.wait(0.05)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                -- STEP 7: EXACT CARRY MAIN TARGET\
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                UpdateStatus(string.format(\
                    \"FAST: arrived -> exact carry %s [%s]\",\
                    tostring(target.category or \"Egg\"),\
                    tostring(target.rarity or \"?\")\
                ))\
    \
                local targetCarried = requestCarryExactTarget(target, gen, 4.0)\
                if not targetCarried then\
                    UpdateStatus(\"FAST: exact target carry failed → rescan\")\
                    State.busyUid = nil\
                    State.latestTarget = nil\
                    ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
                    task.wait(0.08)\
                    continue\
                end\
    \
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                -- STEP 8: Fly + TPWalk ke safezone → deposit\
                -- --------------------------------------------------\
                Runtime.steals = Runtime.steals + 1\
    \
                UpdateStatus(string.format(\
                    \"FAST: secured [%d] %s → Safezone\",\
                    Runtime.steals,\
                    tostring(target.category or \"Egg\")\
                ))\
    \
                local delivered = flyToSafezone(gen)\
    \
                if delivered then\
                    UpdateStatus(string.format(\
                        \"FAST: deposited [%d] %s\",\
                        Runtime.steals,\
                        tostring(target.category or \"Egg\")\
                    ))\
                    State.ignoreUidUntilGone = tostring(target.uid)\
                    ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
                    ScanCache.eggs     = {}\
                    if ShopCfg.autoSellAll then pcall(doSellCycle) end\
                else\
                    UpdateStatus(\"FAST: Safezone interrupted\")\
                    forceUnequipTools()\
                end\
    \
                State.busyUid      = nil\
                State.latestTarget = nil\
                task.wait(0.05)\
            end\
    \
            -- Cleanup\
            State.busyUid          = nil\
            State.latestTarget     = nil\
            State.workerRunning    = false\
            if gen == State.generation then State.active = false end\
            Runtime.isAutoWalking  = false\
            Runtime.farming        = false\
            UpdateStatus(\"Idle\")\
        end\
    \
        -- ============================================================\
        -- PUBLIC API\
        -- ============================================================\
        local function start()\
            if State.active or Runtime.farming then return end\
            State.active     = true\
            State.generation = State.generation + 1\
            local gen = State.generation\
    \
            State.latestTarget         = nil\
            State.busyUid              = nil\
            State.ignoreUidUntilGone   = nil\
            State.bypassedChar         = nil\
    \
            task.spawn(function() scanner(gen) end)\
            task.spawn(function() worker(gen)  end)\
        end\
    \
        local function stop()\
            State.active          = false\
            State.generation      = State.generation + 1\
            State.latestTarget    = nil\
            State.busyUid         = nil\
            Runtime.isAutoWalking = false\
        end\
    \
        return { Start = start, Stop = stop, State = State }\
    end)()\
    \
    -- ============================================================\
    -- FAST METHOD ENTRY POINTS ONLY.\
    -- Normal never passes through a shared router.\
    -- ============================================================\
    Runtime.FastFarmStart = function()\
        if Cfg.autoFarm\
            and Cfg.farmMethod == \"Fast\"\
            and not Runtime.farming\
        then\
            FastFarm.Start()\
        end\
    end\
    \
    Runtime.FastFarmStop = function()\
        FastFarm.Stop()\
    end\
    \
    -- [14] NOTIFY\
    Notify = function(text, dur)\
        pcall(function()\
            game:GetService(\"StarterGui\"):SetCore(\"SendNotification\", {\
                Title=\"D-Hub V2.3\", Text=tostring(text), Duration=dur or 3\
            })\
        end)\
    end\
    \
    -- [15] HEARTBEAT LOOP (Anti-stun)\
    local antiStunConn = S.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()\
        local char = LP.Character\
        if char then\
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"Humanoid\")\
            if hum then\
                pcall(function()\
                    if hum.PlatformStand then hum.PlatformStand = false end\
                end)\
                local st = hum:GetState()\
                if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll then\
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    end)\
    table.insert(ScriptConnections, antiStunConn)\
    \
    -- [16] UI CONSTANTS\
    local C = {\
        BASE     = Color3.fromRGB(13,8,32),\
        SURFACE  = Color3.fromRGB(22,14,46),\
        ELEVATED = Color3.fromRGB(33,22,66),\
        BORDER   = Color3.fromRGB(74,45,128),\
        DIVIDER  = Color3.fromRGB(30,18,58),\
        TEXT_1   = Color3.fromRGB(237,232,255),\
        TEXT_2   = Color3.fromRGB(184,159,232),\
        TEXT_3   = Color3.fromRGB(112,85,168),\
        ACCENT   = Color3.fromRGB(139,93,209),\
        ACCENT_D = Color3.fromRGB(106,58,170),\
        GREEN    = Color3.fromRGB(60,180,120),\
        DANGER   = Color3.fromRGB(196,94,138),\
    }\
    local UI = {activeDD = nil}\
    local TS = S.TweenService\
    \
    -- [17] UI HELPERS\
    local function corner(p, r)\
        local c = Instance.new(\"UICorner\", p); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 6); return c\
    end\
    local function stroke(p, col, th)\
        local s = Instance.new(\"UIStroke\", p); s.Color = col or C.BORDER; s.Thickness = th or 1; return s\
    end\
    local function padding(p, top, bot, left, right)\
        local pad = Instance.new(\"UIPadding\", p)\
        pad.PaddingTop    = UDim.new(0, top   or 0)\
        pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, bot   or 0)\
        pad.PaddingLeft   = UDim.new(0, left  or 0)\
        pad.PaddingRight  = UDim.new(0, right or 0)\
    end\
    local function listLayout(p, spacing, align)\
        local l = Instance.new(\"UIListLayout\", p)\
        l.Padding     = UDim.new(0, spacing or 6)\
        l.SortOrder   = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder\
        if align then l.HorizontalAlignment = align end\
        return l\
    end\
    local function makeDraggable(root, handle)\
        local drag, ds, sp = false, nil, nil\
        handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)\
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then\
                drag = true; ds = i.Position; sp = root.Position\
                i.Changed:Connect(function()\
                    if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then drag = false end\
                end)\
            end\
        end)\
        S.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)\
            if drag and (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then\
                local d = i.Position - ds\
                root.Position = UDim2.new(sp.X.Scale, sp.X.Offset+d.X, sp.Y.Scale, sp.Y.Offset+d.Y)\
            end\
        end)\
    end\
    local function makeFrame(parent, size, pos, bg)\
        local f = Instance.new(\"Frame\", parent)\
        f.Size = size; f.BackgroundColor3 = bg or C.SURFACE; f.BorderSizePixel = 0\
        if pos then f.Position = pos end\
        return f\
    end\
    local function makeLabel(parent, text, size, pos, font, textSize, color, xAlign)\
        local l = Instance.new(\"TextLabel\", parent)\
        l.Size = size or UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)\
        if pos then l.Position = pos end\
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1; l.Text = text\
        l.TextColor3 = color or C.TEXT_1; l.Font = font or Enum.Font.GothamMedium\
        l.TextSize   = textSize or 12\
        l.TextXAlignment = xAlign or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left\
        return l\
    end\
    local function makeButton(parent, text, size, pos, bg)\
        local b = Instance.new(\"TextButton\", parent)\
        b.Size = size or UDim2.new(1,0,0,36)\
        if pos then b.Position = pos end\
        b.BackgroundColor3 = bg or C.SURFACE; b.Text = text or \"\"\
        b.TextColor3 = C.TEXT_1; b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; b.TextSize = 12\
        b.AutoButtonColor = false; b.BorderSizePixel = 0\
        return b\
    end\
    \
    local function BuatToggle(parent, label, default, callback)\
        local Row = makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1,0,0,42), nil, default and C.ELEVATED or C.SURFACE)\
        corner(Row)\
        local rs  = stroke(Row, default and C.ACCENT_D or C.BORDER)\
        local acc = makeFrame(Row, UDim2.new(0,2,0,18), UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,-9), default and C.ACCENT or C.BORDER)\
        corner(acc, 1)\
        local lbl  = makeLabel(Row, label, UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0), UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 12, default and C.TEXT_1 or C.TEXT_2)\
        local pill = makeFrame(Row, UDim2.new(0,38,0,20), UDim2.new(1,-50,0.5,-10), default and C.ACCENT_D or C.DIVIDER)\
        corner(pill, 10)\
        local dot = makeFrame(pill, UDim2.new(0,14,0,14), default and UDim2.new(1,-17,0.5,-7) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-7), default and C.ACCENT or C.TEXT_3)\
        corner(dot, 7)\
        local btn = makeButton(Row, \"\", UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), UDim2.new(0,0,0,0), nil)\
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1\
        local state = default\
        local function setVisual(on)\
            TS:Create(Row, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = on and C.ELEVATED or C.SURFACE}):Play()\
            rs.Color = on and C.ACCENT_D or C.BORDER\
            lbl.TextColor3 = on and C.TEXT_1 or C.TEXT_2\
            acc.BackgroundColor3 = on and C.ACCENT or C.BORDER\
            TS:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = on and C.ACCENT_D or C.DIVIDER}):Play()\
            TS:Create(dot, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {\
                Position         = on and UDim2.new(1,-17,0.5,-7) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-7),\
                BackgroundColor3 = on and C.ACCENT or C.TEXT_3,\
            }):Play()\
        end\
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()\
            state = not state; setVisual(state); if callback then callback(state) end\
        end)\
    \
        if default == true and callback then\
            task.spawn(function()\
                task.wait(0.1)\
                pcall(callback, true)\
            end)\
        end\
    \
        return btn, setVisual, function() return state end\
    end\
    \
    local function BuatSlider(parent, label, min, max, default, callback)\
        local Row = makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1,0,0,52), nil, C.SURFACE)\
        corner(Row); stroke(Row, C.BORDER)\
        local lbl   = makeLabel(Row, label..\": \"..default, UDim2.new(1,-14,1,0), UDim2.new(0,14,0,6), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 11, C.TEXT_1)\
        local track = makeFrame(Row, UDim2.new(1,-28,0,4), UDim2.new(0,14,1,-10), C.DIVIDER)\
        corner(track, 2)\
        local prog  = makeFrame(track, UDim2.new(0,0,1,0), nil, C.ACCENT); corner(prog, 2)\
        local thumb = makeFrame(track, UDim2.new(0,12,0,12), UDim2.new(0,-6,0.5,-6), C.TEXT_1); corner(thumb, 6)\
        local dragger = makeButton(track, \"\", UDim2.new(1,0,3,0), UDim2.new(0,0,-1,0))\
        dragger.BackgroundTransparency = 1\
        local function update(v)\
            local pct = math.clamp((v-min)/(max-min), 0, 1)\
            prog.Size  = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)\
            thumb.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -6, 0.5, -6)\
            lbl.Text   = label..\": \"..math.floor(v)\
            if callback then callback(math.floor(v)) end\
        end\
        update(default)\
        local dragging = false\
        dragger.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)\
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = true end\
        end)\
        S.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)\
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end\
        end)\
        S.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)\
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then\
                local rel = i.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X\
                update(min + (max-min) * math.clamp(rel/track.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1))\
            end\
        end)\
    end\
    \
    local function BuatInput(parent, label, placeholder, default, callback)\
        local Row = makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1,0,0,42), nil, C.SURFACE)\
        corner(Row); stroke(Row, C.BORDER)\
        makeLabel(Row, label, UDim2.new(0.55,-10,1,0), UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 11, C.TEXT_1)\
        local box = Instance.new(\"TextBox\", Row)\
        box.Size = UDim2.new(0.4,0,0,26); box.Position = UDim2.new(0.58,0,0.5,-13)\
        box.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEVATED; corner(box, 4); stroke(box, C.DIVIDER)\
        box.Text = tostring(default or \"\"); box.PlaceholderText = placeholder or \"\"\
        box.TextColor3 = C.TEXT_1; box.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; box.TextSize = 11\
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function() if callback then callback(box.Text) end end)\
    end\
    \
    local function BuatDropdown(parent, label, options, default, callback)\
        local selected = default or options[1]\
        local Row = makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1,0,0,42), nil, C.SURFACE)\
        corner(Row); stroke(Row, C.BORDER)\
        makeLabel(Row, label, UDim2.new(0.42,0,1,0), UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 11, C.TEXT_2)\
        local btn = makeButton(Row, selected, UDim2.new(0.55,0,0,28), UDim2.new(0.44,0,0.5,-14), C.ELEVATED)\
        corner(btn, 4); stroke(btn, C.DIVIDER)\
        btn.TextColor3 = C.TEXT_1; btn.TextSize = 11; btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium\
    \
        local Panel = Instance.new(\"Frame\", UI.MainSG)\
        Panel.Size = UDim2.new(0,200,0,math.min(#options*28+10, 160))\
        Panel.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEVATED\
        corner(Panel, 6); stroke(Panel, C.BORDER); Panel.Visible = false; Panel.ZIndex = 20\
    \
        local scroll = Instance.new(\"ScrollingFrame\", Panel)\
        scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,1,-4); scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0,2,0,2)\
        scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1; scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0; scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3; scroll.ZIndex = 20\
        local layout = listLayout(scroll, 2)\
        layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal(\"AbsoluteContentSize\"):Connect(function()\
            scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y+4)\
        end)\
    \
        for _, opt in ipairs(options) do\
            local ob = makeButton(scroll, opt, UDim2.new(1,0,0,26), nil, selected==opt and C.ACCENT_D or C.ELEVATED)\
            corner(ob, 4); ob.ZIndex = 21\
            ob.TextColor3 = selected==opt and C.TEXT_1 or C.TEXT_2; ob.TextSize = 11; ob.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium\
            ob.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()\
                selected  = opt\
                btn.Text  = opt\
                if UI.closeActiveDropdown then UI.closeActiveDropdown() else Panel.Visible = false; UI.activeDD = nil end\
                for _, c in ipairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do\
                    if c:IsA(\"TextButton\") then\
                        c.BackgroundColor3 = c.Text==opt and C.ACCENT_D or C.ELEVATED\
                        c.TextColor3       = c.Text==opt and C.TEXT_1  or C.TEXT_2\
                    end\
                end\
                if callback then callback(opt) end\
            end)\
        end\
    \
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()\
            if Panel.Visible then\
                if UI.closeActiveDropdown then UI.closeActiveDropdown() else Panel.Visible = false; UI.activeDD = nil end\
                return\
            end\
            if UI.closeActiveDropdown then UI.closeActiveDropdown() elseif UI.activeDD and UI.activeDD ~= Panel then UI.activeDD.Visible = false end\
            local ap = btn.AbsolutePosition; local as = btn.AbsoluteSize\
            Panel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(ap.X, ap.Y+as.Y+4)\
            Panel.Visible  = true\
            UI.activeDD = Panel\
        end)\
    \
        return Row\
    end\
    \
    local function BuatMultiSelect(parent, label, options, selectedTable, callback, defaultNone)\
        local ROW = makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1,0,0,42), nil, C.SURFACE)\
        corner(ROW); stroke(ROW, C.BORDER)\
    \
        local function summaryText()\
            if defaultNone then\
                if not next(selectedTable) then return \"None\" end\
            else\
                if selectedTable[\"All\"] or not next(selectedTable) then return \"All\" end\
            end\
            if selectedTable[\"All\"] then return \"All\" end\
            local list = {}\
            for _, opt in ipairs(options) do\
                if opt ~= \"All\" and selectedTable[opt] then table.insert(list, opt) end\
            end\
            if #list == 0 then return defaultNone and \"None\" or \"All\" end\
            if #list == 1 then return list[1] end\
            return #list .. \" Selected\"\
        end\
    \
        makeLabel(ROW, label, UDim2.new(0.42,0,1,0), UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 11, C.TEXT_2)\
        local btn = makeButton(ROW, summaryText(), UDim2.new(0.55,0,0,28), UDim2.new(0.44,0,0.5,-14), C.ELEVATED)\
        corner(btn, 4); stroke(btn, C.DIVIDER)\
        btn.TextColor3 = C.TEXT_1; btn.TextSize = 11; btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium\
    \
        local Panel = Instance.new(\"Frame\", UI.MainSG)\
        Panel.Size = UDim2.new(0,220,0,200); Panel.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEVATED\
        corner(Panel, 6); stroke(Panel, C.BORDER); Panel.Visible = false; Panel.ZIndex = 20\
    \
        local scroll = Instance.new(\"ScrollingFrame\", Panel)\
        scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,1,-4); scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0,2,0,2)\
        scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1; scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0; scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4; scroll.ZIndex = 20\
        local layout = listLayout(scroll, 2)\
        layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal(\"AbsoluteContentSize\"):Connect(function()\
            scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y+8)\
        end)\
    \
        local function refreshList()\
            for _, c in ipairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA(\"TextButton\") then c:Destroy() end end\
    \
            if defaultNone then\
                local noneSelected = not next(selectedTable)\
                local ob = makeButton(scroll, (noneSelected and \"[✓] \" or \"[ ] \") .. \"None\", UDim2.new(1,0,0,26), nil, noneSelected and C.DANGER or C.ELEVATED)\
                corner(ob, 4); ob.ZIndex = 21\
                ob.TextColor3 = noneSelected and C.TEXT_1 or C.TEXT_2; ob.TextSize = 11; ob.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium\
                ob.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()\
                    table.clear(selectedTable)\
                    btn.Text = summaryText()\
                    refreshList()\
                    if callback then callback(selectedTable) end\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            for _, opt in ipairs(options) do\
                local isAll = (opt == \"All\")\
                if defaultNone and isAll then continue end\
    \
                local sel = selectedTable[opt]==true or (isAll and selectedTable[\"All\"])\
                local ob  = makeButton(scroll, (sel and \"[✓] \" or \"[ ] \")..opt, UDim2.new(1,0,0,26), nil, sel and C.ACCENT_D or C.ELEVATED)\
                corner(ob, 4); ob.ZIndex = 21\
                ob.TextColor3 = sel and C.TEXT_1 or C.TEXT_2; ob.TextSize = 11; ob.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium\
                ob.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()\
                    if isAll then\
                        table.clear(selectedTable); selectedTable[\"All\"] = true\
                    else\
                        selectedTable[\"All\"] = nil\
                        if selectedTable[opt] then selectedTable[opt] = nil else selectedTable[opt] = true end\
                        if not defaultNone then\
                            local any = false\
                            for _, v in pairs(selectedTable) do if v then any = true; break end end\
                            if not any then selectedTable[\"All\"] = true end\
                        end\
                    end\
                    btn.Text = summaryText()\
                    refreshList()\
                    if callback then callback(selectedTable) end\
                end)\
            end\
        end\
    \
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()\
            if Panel.Visible then\
                if UI.closeActiveDropdown then UI.closeActiveDropdown() else Panel.Visible = false; UI.activeDD = nil end\
                return\
            end\
            if UI.closeActiveDropdown then UI.closeActiveDropdown() elseif UI.activeDD and UI.activeDD ~= Panel then UI.activeDD.Visible = false end\
            refreshList()\
            local ap = btn.AbsolutePosition; local as = btn.AbsoluteSize\
            Panel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(ap.X, ap.Y+as.Y+4)\
            Panel.Visible  = true\
            UI.activeDD = Panel\
        end)\
    \
        return ROW\
    end\
    \
    local function BuatSection(parent, text)\
        local lbl = makeLabel(parent, text:upper(), UDim2.new(1,0,0,20), nil, Enum.Font.GothamBold, 10, C.TEXT_3)\
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1\
    end\
    \
    local function BuatActionBtn(parent, label, color, callback)\
        local Row = makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1,0,0,36), nil, color or C.SURFACE)\
        corner(Row); stroke(Row, C.BORDER)\
        local acc = makeFrame(Row, UDim2.new(0,2,0,16), UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,-8), C.BORDER); corner(acc, 1)\
        makeLabel(Row, label, UDim2.new(1,-18,1,0), UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 12, C.TEXT_1)\
        local btn = makeButton(Row, \"\", UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)); btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1\
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if callback then callback() end end)\
        return Row, btn\
    end\
    \
    -- [18] BUILD UI\
    local function buildUI()\
        local MainSG = Instance.new(\"ScreenGui\", GuiRoot)\
        MainSG.Name = \"DHub_SAE_V2.3\"; MainSG.ResetOnSpawn = false\
        UI.MainSG = MainSG\
    \
        local function closeActiveDropdown()\
            if UI.activeDD then\
                UI.activeDD.Visible = false\
                UI.activeDD = nil\
            end\
        end\
        UI.closeActiveDropdown = closeActiveDropdown\
    \
        S.UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gpe)\
            if gpe then return end\
            if not UI.activeDD or not UI.activeDD.Visible then return end\
            if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end\
            local p  = inp.Position\
            local ap = UI.activeDD.AbsolutePosition\
            local as = UI.activeDD.AbsoluteSize\
            if not (p.X >= ap.X and p.X <= ap.X + as.X and p.Y >= ap.Y and p.Y <= ap.Y + as.Y) then\
                closeActiveDropdown()\
            end\
        end)\
    \
        local Root = makeFrame(MainSG, UDim2.new(0,460,0,400))\
        Root.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,-230,0.5,-200); Root.BackgroundTransparency = 1\
    \
        local ReleaseFloat = makeFrame(\
            MainSG,\
            UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 42),\
            UDim2.new(1, -190, 0.5, -21),\
            C.SURFACE\
        )\
        ReleaseFloat.Name = \"DHub_BypassReleaseFloat\"\
        ReleaseFloat.ZIndex = 200\
        corner(ReleaseFloat, 7)\
        stroke(ReleaseFloat, C.BORDER, 1)\
    \
        local ReleaseGrip = makeFrame(ReleaseFloat, UDim2.new(0, 24, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), C.ELEVATED)\
        ReleaseGrip.ZIndex = 201; corner(ReleaseGrip, 7)\
        local GripLbl = makeLabel(ReleaseGrip, \"≡\", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 14, C.TEXT_2, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)\
        GripLbl.ZIndex = 202\
        makeDraggable(ReleaseFloat, ReleaseGrip)\
        makeDraggable(ReleaseFloat, GripLbl)\
    \
        local ReleaseBtn = makeButton(ReleaseFloat, \"Bypass Release\", UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, -4), UDim2.new(0, 25, 0, 2), C.SURFACE)\
        ReleaseBtn.Name = \"BypassReleaseButton\"\
        ReleaseBtn.ZIndex = 201; ReleaseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; ReleaseBtn.TextSize = 11; ReleaseBtn.TextColor3 = C.TEXT_1\
        corner(ReleaseBtn, 6)\
    \
        ReleaseBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TS:Create(ReleaseBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEVATED}):Play() end)\
        ReleaseBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TS:Create(ReleaseBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundColor3 = C.SURFACE}):Play() end)\
        ReleaseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()\
            releaseTreadmill()\
            Notify(\"Bypass Release: treadmill released\", 2)\
        end)\
    \
        local MinIcon = Instance.new(\"ImageButton\", Root)\
        MinIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0,48,0,48); MinIcon.BackgroundColor3 = C.SURFACE\
        MinIcon.Image = \"rbxassetid://122511465288707\"; MinIcon.Visible = false\
        MinIcon.AutoButtonColor = false; corner(MinIcon, 8); stroke(MinIcon, C.BORDER)\
        makeDraggable(Root, MinIcon)\
    \
        local Win = makeFrame(Root, UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), UDim2.new(0,0,0,0), C.BASE)\
        Win.ClipsDescendants = true; corner(Win, 8); stroke(Win, C.BORDER)\
    \
        local Header = makeFrame(Win, UDim2.new(1,0,0,44), nil, C.SURFACE); corner(Header, 8)\
        makeFrame(Header, UDim2.new(1,0,0,8), UDim2.new(0,0,1,-8), C.SURFACE)\
        makeFrame(Header, UDim2.new(0,36,0,3), UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), C.ACCENT)\
        local ico = Instance.new(\"ImageLabel\", Header)\
        ico.Size = UDim2.new(0,28,0,28); ico.Position = UDim2.new(0,8,0.5,-14)\
        ico.BackgroundTransparency = 1; ico.Image = \"rbxassetid://122511465288707\"; corner(ico, 8)\
        local titleLbl = makeLabel(Header, \"\", UDim2.new(1,-100,1,0), UDim2.new(0,44,0,0), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 13, C.TEXT_1)\
        titleLbl.RichText = true\
        titleLbl.Text     = \"D-HUB: <font color='#8B5DD1'>STEAL AN EGG</font> <font color='#666'>V2.3</font>\"\
        makeDraggable(Root, Header)\
    \
        local origSize = Root.Size\
        local MinBtn   = makeButton(Header, \"—\", UDim2.new(0,22,0,22), UDim2.new(1,-30,0.5,-11), C.SURFACE)\
        corner(MinBtn, 4); MinBtn.TextSize = 13; MinBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; MinBtn.TextColor3 = C.TEXT_2\
        MinBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()\
            Win.Visible = false; MinIcon.Visible = true\
            TS:Create(Root, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Size=UDim2.new(0,48,0,48)}):Play()\
        end)\
        MinIcon.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()\
            Win.Visible = true; MinIcon.Visible = false\
            TS:Create(Root, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Size=origSize}):Play()\
        end)\
    \
        local Body = makeFrame(Win, UDim2.new(1,0,1,-44), UDim2.new(0,0,0,44))\
        Body.BackgroundTransparency = 1\
    \
        local Sidebar = makeFrame(Body, UDim2.new(0,130,1,0), nil, C.SURFACE)\
        Sidebar.BorderSizePixel = 0\
        makeFrame(Sidebar, UDim2.new(0,1,1,0), UDim2.new(1,0,0,0), C.DIVIDER)\
    \
        local TabScroll = Instance.new(\"ScrollingFrame\", Sidebar)\
        TabScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,-50); TabScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1\
        TabScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0; TabScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 2\
        local tabLayout = listLayout(TabScroll, 4, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center)\
        padding(TabScroll, 10, 10, 8, 8)\
        tabLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal(\"AbsoluteContentSize\"):Connect(function()\
            TabScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,tabLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y+20)\
        end)\
    \
        local ProfileBar = makeFrame(Sidebar, UDim2.new(1,0,0,50), UDim2.new(0,0,1,-50), C.BASE)\
        local avi = Instance.new(\"ImageLabel\", ProfileBar)\
        avi.Size = UDim2.new(0,32,0,32); avi.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0.5,-16)\
        avi.BackgroundColor3 = C.SURFACE; avi.BorderSizePixel = 0; corner(avi, 16)\
        task.spawn(function()\
            pcall(function()\
                avi.Image = S.Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(LP.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420)\
            end)\
        end)\
        makeLabel(ProfileBar, LP.DisplayName, UDim2.new(1,-54,0,16), UDim2.new(0,50,0.5,-16), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 10, C.TEXT_1)\
        makeLabel(ProfileBar, \"D-Hub V2.3\",     UDim2.new(1,-54,0,12), UDim2.new(0,50,0.5,2),   Enum.Font.Gotham,     9,  C.ACCENT)\
    \
        local ContentArea = makeFrame(Body, UDim2.new(1,-145,1,0), UDim2.new(0,145,0,0))\
        ContentArea.BackgroundTransparency = 1\
    \
        local pages   = {}\
        local navBtns = {}\
    \
        local function makePage(name)\
            local page = Instance.new(\"ScrollingFrame\", ContentArea)\
            page.Name = name; page.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)\
            page.BackgroundTransparency = 1; page.BorderSizePixel = 0\
            page.ScrollBarThickness = 3; page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.DIVIDER\
            page.Visible = (name == \"Auto\")\
            page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,1200)\
            local layout = listLayout(page, 8)\
            padding(page, 14, 14, 14, 14)\
            layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal(\"AbsoluteContentSize\"):Connect(function()\
                page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,math.max(layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y+35, 600))\
            end)\
            pages[name] = page\
            return page\
        end\
    \
        local function switchTab(name)\
            if UI.closeActiveDropdown then UI.closeActiveDropdown() end\
            for n, p in pairs(pages) do\
                local on = (n == name)\
                p.Visible = on\
                if on then\
                    local layout = p:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"UIListLayout\")\
                    if layout then\
                        local absY = layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y\
                        p.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(absY + 35, 600))\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
            for n, d in pairs(navBtns) do\
                local on = (n == name)\
                TS:Create(d.btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {BackgroundColor3 = on and C.ELEVATED or C.SURFACE}):Play()\
                TS:Create(d.ind, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1}):Play()\
                d.lbl.TextColor3 = on and C.TEXT_1 or C.TEXT_2\
            end\
        end\
    \
        local function makeNavBtn(name)\
            local btn = makeButton(TabScroll, \"\", UDim2.new(1,-2,0,34), nil, C.SURFACE); corner(btn, 6)\
            local ind = makeFrame(btn, UDim2.new(0,3,0,16), UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,-8), C.ACCENT); corner(ind, 2)\
            ind.BackgroundTransparency = (name == \"Auto\") and 0 or 1\
            local lbl = makeLabel(btn, name, UDim2.new(1,-16,1,0), UDim2.new(0,12,0,0), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 11, (name == \"Auto\") and C.TEXT_1 or C.TEXT_2)\
            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() switchTab(name) end)\
            navBtns[name] = {btn=btn, ind=ind, lbl=lbl}\
        end\
    \
        local TAB_LIST = {\"Auto\", \"Plot\", \"Shop\", \"Event\", \"Misc\", \"Info\"}\
        for _, t in ipairs(TAB_LIST) do makeNavBtn(t); makePage(t) end\
    \
        -- ============ TAB: AUTO ============\
        local P = pages[\"Auto\"]\
    \
        -- ========================================================\
        -- FARM METHOD\
        -- Selector only. Fast engine will be added later.\
        -- ========================================================\
        BuatSection(P, \"Farm Method\")\
    \
        BuatDropdown(\
            P,\
            \"Select Method\",\
            {\"Normal\", \"Fast\"},\
            Cfg.farmMethod,\
            function(v)\
                local nextMethod =\
                    (v == \"Fast\") and \"Fast\" or \"Normal\"\
    \
                local changed =\
                    nextMethod ~= Cfg.farmMethod\
    \
                Cfg.farmMethod = nextMethod\
                saveConfig()\
    \
                if changed and Cfg.autoFarm then\
                    Notify(\
                        \"Farm Method set to \"\
                        .. nextMethod\
                        .. \". OFF -> ON Auto Farm to switch engine.\",\
                        4\
                    )\
                end\
            end\
        )\
    \
        BuatSection(P, \"Egg Farm\")\
    \
        local statusRow = makeFrame(P, UDim2.new(1,0,0,32), nil, C.SURFACE); corner(statusRow); stroke(statusRow, C.BORDER)\
        local statusLbl = makeLabel(statusRow, \"Status: Idle  |  Steals: 0\", UDim2.new(1,-14,1,0), UDim2.new(0,10,0,0), Enum.Font.Gotham, 11, C.ACCENT)\
        Runtime.StatusLbl = statusLbl\
    \
        BuatMultiSelect(P, \"Select Area\", ZONE_NAMES, Cfg.selectedAreas, function(tbl)\
            Cfg.selectedAreas = tbl; saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatMultiSelect(P, \"Select Rarity\", RARITIES, Cfg.selectedRarities, function(tbl)\
            Cfg.selectedRarities = tbl; saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatInput(P, \"Min Weight\", \"0 = All\", tostring(Cfg.minWeight), function(v)\
            Cfg.minWeight = math.max(0, tonumber(v) or 0); saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatDropdown(P, \"Farm Priority\", {\"Nearest\", \"Hugest\", \"Rarest\"}, Cfg.farmPriority, function(v)\
            Cfg.farmPriority = v or \"Nearest\"; saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatToggle(P, \"Instant Steal (0s Prompt)\", Cfg.instantSteal, function(v)\
            Cfg.instantSteal = v\
            if v then applyInstantToAll() end\
            saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatToggle(P, \"Auto Farm Eggs\", Cfg.autoFarm, function(v)\
            Cfg.autoFarm = v\
            PlotRuntime.placeGeneration = PlotRuntime.placeGeneration + 1\
            PlotRuntime.placeOwner = nil\
    \
            if v and not Runtime.farming then\
                if Cfg.farmMethod == \"Fast\"\
                    and Runtime.FastFarmStart\
                then\
                    Runtime.FastFarmStart()\
                else\
                    task.spawn(doFarmLoop)\
                end\
            elseif not v then\
                if Runtime.FastFarmStop then\
                    Runtime.FastFarmStop()\
                end\
    \
                if PlotCfg.autoPlace and not PlotRuntime.placing then\
                    task.spawn(doPlotPlaceLoop)\
                end\
            end\
            saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatToggle(P, \"Anti Boss\", Cfg.antiBoss, function(v)\
            Cfg.antiBoss = v\
            if not v then stopAntiBossSpamTeleport() end\
            saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
