        BuatToggle(P, \"Auto Treadmill (When Waiting)\", Cfg.autoTreadmill, function(v)\
            Cfg.autoTreadmill = v\
            if not v and isPlayerOnTreadmill() then releaseTreadmill() end\
            saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatSection(P, \"Speed Settings (Walk Engine)\")\
    \
        BuatSlider(P, \"Walk Speed\", 1, 320, Cfg.walkSpeed, function(v)\
            Cfg.walkSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or Cfg.walkSpeed, 1, 320)\
    \
            -- Apply slider changes instantly to the currently active farm movement.\
            applyConfiguredWalkSpeedNow()\
    \
            saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatActionBtn(P, \"Reset Steal Counter\", C.SURFACE, function()\
            Runtime.steals = 0; UpdateStatus(Runtime.statusText)\
            Notify(\"Counter reset\", 2)\
        end)\
    \
        BuatActionBtn(P, \"Force Re-Scan\", C.SURFACE, function()\
            ScanCache.lastScan = 0\
            Notify(\"Cache cleared, next cycle re-scans\", 2)\
        end)\
    \
        -- ============ TAB: PLOT ============\
        local PP = pages[\"Plot\"]\
        BuatSection(PP, \"Plot Manager\")\
    \
        local plotStatusRow = makeFrame(PP, UDim2.new(1,0,0,32), nil, C.SURFACE); corner(plotStatusRow); stroke(plotStatusRow, C.BORDER)\
        local plotStatusLbl = makeLabel(plotStatusRow, \"Plot: Idle  |  Placed: 0\", UDim2.new(1,-14,1,0), UDim2.new(0,10,0,0), Enum.Font.Gotham, 11, C.ACCENT)\
        PlotRuntime.StatusLbl2 = plotStatusLbl\
    \
        BuatSection(PP, \"Place Settings\")\
        BuatMultiSelect(PP, \"Rarity To Place\", RARITIES, PlotCfg.selectedRarities, function(tbl)\
            PlotCfg.selectedRarities = tbl; saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatInput(PP, \"Min Weight To Place\", \"0 = place all\", tostring(PlotCfg.minWeight), function(v)\
            PlotCfg.minWeight = math.max(0, tonumber(v) or 0); saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatSection(PP, \"Automation\")\
        BuatToggle(PP, \"Auto Place Eggs\", PlotCfg.autoPlace, function(v)\
            PlotCfg.autoPlace = v\
            PlotRuntime.placeGeneration = PlotRuntime.placeGeneration + 1\
            PlotRuntime.placeOwner = nil\
            if v and not PlotRuntime.placing and not Cfg.autoFarm then\
                task.spawn(doPlotPlaceLoop)\
            elseif not v then\
                UpdatePlotStatus(\"Idle\")\
            end\
            saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatToggle(PP, \"Auto Hatch Eggs\", PlotCfg.autoHatch, function(v)\
            PlotCfg.autoHatch = v\
            if v and not PlotRuntime.hatching then task.spawn(doHatchLoop) end\
            saveConfig()\
        end)\
    \
        BuatSection(PP, \"Actions\")\
        BuatActionBtn(PP, \"Place Eggs Now\", C.ACCENT_D, function()\
            if Cfg.autoFarm then Notify(\"Stop Auto Farm before manual Plot placement.\", 3); return end\
            PlotRuntime.placeGeneration = PlotRuntime.placeGeneration + 1\
            local ok = doPlotPlaceOnce(\"manual\", PlotRuntime.placeGeneration)\
            Notify(ok and \"Placed eggs successfully!\" or \"Could not place (full / none)\", 3)\
        end)\
    \
        BuatActionBtn(PP, \"Reset Placed Counter\", C.SURFACE, function()\
            PlotRuntime.placedCount = 0; UpdatePlotStatus(PlotRuntime.statusText)\
            Notify(\"Plot counter reset\", 2)\
        end)\
    \
        -- ============ TAB: SHOP ============\
        local PS = pages[\"Shop\"]\
        BuatSection(PS, \"Auto Sell Eggs\")\
        BuatMultiSelect(PS, \"Egg Rarity to Sell\", SELL_RARITIES, ShopCfg.sellEggRarities, function(tbl)\
            ShopCfg.sellEggRarities = tbl; saveConfig()\
        end, true)\
    \

    -- [19] MAIN LAUNCH\
    local function launch()\
        buildUI()\
        startStandaloneAntiBossWatcher()\
    \
        task.spawn(function()\
            task.wait(0.3)\
            ensureCharacter(5)\
    \
            if Cfg.instantSteal then applyInstantToAll() end\
    \
            if Cfg.autoFarm and not Runtime.farming then\
                if Cfg.farmMethod == \"Fast\"\
                    and Runtime.FastFarmStart\
                then\
                    Runtime.FastFarmStart()\
                else\
                    task.spawn(doFarmLoop)\
                end\
            elseif PlotCfg.autoPlace and not PlotRuntime.placing and not Cfg.autoFarm then\
                task.spawn(doPlotPlaceLoop)\
            end\
    \
            if EventCfg.autoFuseEvent then\
                -- Config-loaded ON behaves exactly like a manual rising edge:\
                -- one Safezone opening pass, then scanner.\
                beginFuseEventSession()\
            elseif EventCfg.autoBoss and not EventRuntime.running then\
                task.spawn(doEventMasterLoop)\
            end\
    \
            if PlotCfg.autoHatch and not PlotRuntime.hatching then\
                task.spawn(doHatchLoop)\
            end\
    \
            if MiscCfg.fpsBoost then applyFpsBoost(true) end\
            if MiscCfg.ultraFps then applyUltraFps(true) end\
            if ShopCfg.autoSellAll then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.5); doSellCycle() end) end\
            if ShopCfg.autoFuse then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.5); doFuseOnce() end) end\
        end)\
    \
        Notify(\"D-Hub SAE V2.3.1 Loaded! + Rift Carry/Claim Loop Fix\", 3)\
    end\
    \
    local key = getEnvKey()\
    if not key or key==\"\" then\
        pcall(function() if isfile and isfile(KEY_PATH) then key = readfile(KEY_PATH) end end)\
    end\
    if isDevMode() then\
        launch()\
    else\
        if checkKey(key) then\
            pcall(function() writefile(KEY_PATH, key) end)\
            launch()\
        else\
            pcall(function() LP:Kick(\"D-Hub | Invalid Key. Get key: \" .. GET_KEY_URL) end)\
        end\
    end\
    \
    -- =====================================================================\
    -- [D-HUB ADDON] DISCORD EGG WEBHOOK NOTIFIER\
    -- =====================================================================\
    -- IMPORTANT:\
    -- Addon ini STANDALONE / OBSERVER ONLY.\
    -- Tidak mengubah:\
    --   * WalkToTarget\
    --   * doFarmLoop\
    --   * instantCarryEscape\
    --   * Anti Boss\
    --   * Auto Sell\
    --   * Plot\
    --   * UI\
    --   * launch()\
    --\
    -- Trigger:\
    --   Game GUI menampilkan: \"You stole an EGG!\"\
    --\
    -- Data:\
    --   * Egg baru dari Save.EggInventory\
    --   * Pet / rarity dari Data.Assets\
    --   * Area dicocokkan dari snapshot field egg sebelum egg hilang\
    --   * Thumbnail dari robloxstealanegg.wiki\
    -- =====================================================================\
    do\
        -- GANTI DENGAN WEBHOOK DISCORD KAMU.\
        -- Disarankan gunakan webhook BARU jika URL lama pernah diposting publik.\
        local WEBHOOK_URL = \"https://discordapp.com/api/webhooks/1545715146612154402/UMrErzDZDFsUGAR8v3lQRNVHAgka0PHwmWV2K-81iFsNmN8sbipLqOzg6f7A9dOClezl\"\
    \
        -- true = semua rarity dikirim untuk TEST.\
        -- false = hanya Secret -> BrainrotGod.\
        local WEBHOOK_TEST_ALL_RARITIES = true\
    \
        local WEBHOOK_RARITY_ALLOWED = {\
            Secret       = true,\
            Exotic       = true,\
            Exclusive    = true,\
            Limited      = true,\
            Superior     = true,\
            Eternal      = true,\
            Divine       = true,\
            Titan        = true,\
            BrainrotGod  = true,\
            Transcendent = true,\
        }\
    \
        local WIKI_PETS_PAGE = \"https://robloxstealanegg.wiki/pets/\"\
        local WIKI_IMAGE_BASE = \"https://robloxstealanegg.wiki/images/pets/\"\
    \
        local Notifier = {\
            knownInventory = {},\
            recentNewEggs = {},\
            fieldCache = {},\
            previousField = {},\
            vanishedField = {},\
            sentUids = {},\
            initializedInventory = false,\
            lastGuiTrigger = 0,\
    \
            -- Rift reward observer state.\
            -- Observer only: does not call or modify Rift remotes/farm logic.\
            riftClaimArmedUntil = 0,\
            riftRewardUid = nil,\
        }\
    \
        local function now()\
            return tick()\
        end\
    \
        local function copyShallow(t)\
            if type(t) ~= \"table\" then return {} end\
            local c = {}\
            for k, v in pairs(t) do\
                c[k] = v\
            end\
            return c\
        end\
    \
        local function maskUsername(name)\
            name = tostring(name or \"???\")\
            return name:sub(1, 3) .. \"*****\"\
        end\
    \
        local function getRequest()\
            local fn = nil\
    \
            pcall(function()\
                if syn and type(syn.request) == \"function\" then\
                    fn = syn.request\
                elseif http and type(http.request) == \"function\" then\
                    fn = http.request\
                elseif type(http_request) == \"function\" then\
                    fn = http_request\
                elseif type(request) == \"function\" then\
                    fn = request\
                elseif fluxus and type(fluxus.request) == \"function\" then\
                    fn = fluxus.request\
                elseif type(krnl_request) == \"function\" then\
                    fn = krnl_request\
                end\
            end)\
    \
            return fn\
        end\
    \
        local function webhookConfigured()\
            local url = tostring(WEBHOOK_URL or \"\")\
            return url ~= \"\"\
                and url ~= \"PASTE_DISCORD_WEBHOOK_HERE\"\
                and url:find(\"/api/webhooks/\", 1, true) ~= nil\
        end\
    \
        local function getSave()\
            local save = nil\
            pcall(function()\
                save = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save).Get()\
            end)\
            return save\
        end\
    \
        local function getAssetsDirectory()\
            local dir = nil\
            pcall(function()\
                local assets = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Data.Assets)\
                dir = assets and (assets.Directory or assets)\
            end)\
            return type(dir) == \"table\" and dir or nil\
        end\
    \
        local function findAssetData(category)\
            category = tostring(category or \"\")\
            local dir = getAssetsDirectory()\
            if not dir then return nil, category end\
    \
            if type(dir[category]) == \"table\" then\
                return dir[category], category\
            end\
    \
            local wanted = category:lower()\
    \
            for key, data in pairs(dir) do\
                if type(data) == \"table\" then\
                    local keyText = tostring(key)\
                    local display = tostring(\
                        data.DisplayName\
                        or data.Name\
                        or data.Title\
                        or \"\"\
                    )\
    \
                    if keyText:lower() == wanted\
                        or (display ~= \"\" and display:lower() == wanted)\
                    then\
                        return data, keyText\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return nil, category\
        end\
    \
        local function resolvePetInfo(category)\
            category = tostring(category or \"Unknown\")\
            local data, key = findAssetData(category)\
    \
            local displayName = category\
            local rarity = \"Unknown\"\
    \
            if type(data) == \"table\" then\
                displayName = tostring(\
                    data.DisplayName\
                    or data.Name\
                    or data.Title\
                    or key\
                    or category\
                )\
    \
                local r = data.Rarity\
                if type(r) == \"table\" then\
                    rarity = tostring(\
                        r._id\
                        or r.id\
                        or r.Name\
                        or r.DisplayName\
                        or r.Title\
                        or \"Unknown\"\
                    )\
                elseif type(r) == \"string\" then\
                    rarity = r\
                elseif data.RarityName then\
                    rarity = tostring(data.RarityName)\
                end\
            end\
    \
            -- Pakai cache rarity existing script sebagai fallback.\
            if rarity == \"Unknown\" then\
                pcall(buildAssetRarityCache)\
                rarity = tostring(\
                    AssetRarityCache[category:lower()]\
                    or AssetRarityCache[tostring(key):lower()]\
                    or \"Unknown\"\
                )\
            end\
    \
            return displayName, rarity\
        end\
    \
        -- -------------------------------------------------------------\
        -- PET INCOME / SECOND\
        -- Mengikuti logic yang sudah dipakai script pada Backpack Speed:\
        -- Data.Assets.EarningRate * mutation multiplier.\
        -- -------------------------------------------------------------\
        local function collectMutationNames(record)\
            local names = {}\
            local seen = {}\
    \
            local function addMutation(v)\
                if type(v) ~= \"string\" then return end\
    \
                v = tostring(v)\
                if v == \"\" or v == \"None\" or seen[v] then return end\
    \
                seen[v] = true\
                table.insert(names, v)\
            end\
    \
            if type(record) == \"table\" then\
                addMutation(record.BaseMutation)\
    \
                local muts = record.Mutations\
                if type(muts) == \"string\" then\
                    addMutation(muts)\
    \
                elseif type(muts) == \"table\" then\
                    -- Array style: {\"Golden\", \"Shiny\"}\
                    for k, v in pairs(muts) do\
                        if type(v) == \"string\" then\
                            addMutation(v)\
    \
                        -- Dictionary style: {Golden=true}\
                        elseif v == true and type(k) == \"string\" then\
                            addMutation(k)\
    \
                        -- Object style / nested mutation records\
                        elseif type(v) == \"table\" then\
                            addMutation(v._id)\
                            addMutation(v.id)\
                            addMutation(v.Name)\
                            addMutation(v.Mutation)\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return names\
        end\
    \
        local function resolvePetIncome(category, record)\
            local assetData = select(1, findAssetData(category))\
            local baseRate = 0\
    \
            if type(assetData) == \"table\" then\
                baseRate = tonumber(assetData.EarningRate) or 0\
            end\
    \
            local multiplier = 1\
            local mutationNames = collectMutationNames(record)\
    \
            if #mutationNames > 0 then\
                pcall(function()\
                    local Mutations = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Modules.Mutations)\
    \
                    if Mutations and type(Mutations.EarningsFor) == \"function\" then\
                        local result = Mutations.EarningsFor(mutationNames)\
    \
                        if type(result) == \"number\" and result > 0 then\
                            multiplier = result\
                        end\
                    end\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            return baseRate * multiplier, baseRate, multiplier\
        end\
    \
        local function formatIncome(value)\
            value = tonumber(value) or 0\
            local absValue = math.abs(value)\
    \
            if absValue >= 1e12 then\
                return string.format(\"%.2fT\", value / 1e12):gsub(\"%.00\", \"\")\
            elseif absValue >= 1e9 then\
                return string.format(\"%.2fB\", value / 1e9):gsub(\"%.00\", \"\")\
            elseif absValue >= 1e6 then\
                return string.format(\"%.2fM\", value / 1e6):gsub(\"%.00\", \"\")\
            elseif absValue >= 1e3 then\
                return string.format(\"%.2fK\", value / 1e3):gsub(\"%.00\", \"\")\
            end\
    \
            if value == math.floor(value) then\
                return tostring(math.floor(value))\
            end\
    \
            return string.format(\"%.2f\", value):gsub(\"0+$\", \"\"):gsub(\"%.$\", \"\")\
        end\
    \
        -- -------------------------------------------------------------\
        -- DISCORD EMBED COLOR BY RARITY\
        -- Field embed.color = garis vertikal kiri Discord.\
        -- -------------------------------------------------------------\
        local RARITY_EMBED_COLORS = {\
            Common       = 0xA3A3A3,\
            Basic        = 0xA3A3A3,\
    \
            Uncommon     = 0x4ADE80,\
            Celestial    = 0x4ADE80,\
    \
            Rare         = 0x60A5FA,\
            SuperRare    = 0x2DD4BF,\
            Epic         = 0xC084FC,\
            Legendary    = 0xFACC15,\
    \
            Mythic       = 0xE879F9,\
            Mythical     = 0xE879F9,\
            Rainbow      = 0xFB7185,\
            Prismatic    = 0xFB7185,\
    \
            -- Warna dari rarity display yang kamu kirim.\
            Cosmic       = 0x22D3EE,\
            Secret       = 0xF97316,\
            Eternal      = 0xA5B4FC,\
            Divine       = 0xF0ABFC,\
    \
            Exotic       = 0xFB7185,\
            Exclusive    = 0xF472B6,\
            Admin        = 0xF472B6,\
            Limited      = 0xF43F5E,\
            Superior     = 0xA3E635,\
            Transcendent = 0xD8B4FE,\
            Titan        = 0xEF4444,\
            BrainrotGod  = 0xFDE047,\
        }\
    \
        local function getRarityEmbedColor(rarity)\
            rarity = tostring(rarity or \"\")\
    \
            if RARITY_EMBED_COLORS[rarity] then\
                return RARITY_EMBED_COLORS[rarity]\
            end\
    \
            local wanted = rarity:lower()\
    \
            for rarityName, color in pairs(RARITY_EMBED_COLORS) do\
                if tostring(rarityName):lower() == wanted then\
                    return color\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return 0x8B5DD1\
        end\
    \
        local function slugifyPetName(name)\
            name = tostring(name or \"\"):lower()\
            name = name:gsub(\"&\", \" and \")\
            name = name:gsub(\"['’`]\", \"\")\
            name = name:gsub(\"[%(%)%[%]{}]\", \" \")\
            name = name:gsub(\"[_/]\", \" \")\
            name = name:gsub(\"[^%w%s%-]\", \" \")\
            name = name:gsub(\"%s+\", \" \")\
            name = name:gsub(\"^%s+\", \"\")\
            name = name:gsub(\"%s+$\", \"\")\
            name = name:gsub(\"%s\", \"-\")\
            name = name:gsub(\"%-+\", \"-\")\
            return name\
        end\
    \
        local function wikiPetImage(petName)\
            local slug = slugifyPetName(petName)\
            if slug == \"\" or slug == \"unknown\" then\
                return nil\
            end\
            return WIKI_IMAGE_BASE .. slug .. \".webp\"\
        end\
    \
        local function getFieldRecords()\
            local records = nil\
    \
            pcall(function()\
                local EggState = require(S.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)\
                local snap = EggState.ReadFieldEggs()\
                records = snap and snap.Records\
            end)\
    \
            if type(records) ~= \"table\" then\
                pcall(function()\
                    local packages = S.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(\"Packages\")\
                    local networking = packages and packages:FindFirstChild(\"Networking\")\
                    local rf = networking and networking:FindFirstChild(\"RF/EggWorld/AskFieldEggSnapshot\")\
    \
                    if rf then\
                        local snap = rf:InvokeServer()\
                        records = snap and snap.Records\
                    end\
                end)\
            end\
    \
            return type(records) == \"table\" and records or {}\
        end\
    \
        local function pollFieldEggs()\
            local current = {}\
            local t = now()\
    \
            for _, rec in ipairs(getFieldRecords()) do\
                if type(rec) == \"table\" and rec.Uid then\
                    local uid = tostring(rec.Uid)\
                    local info = {\
                        uid = uid,\
                        category = tostring(rec.AssetCategory or \"\"),\
                        area = tostring(rec.AreaId or rec.Area or \"Unknown\"),\
                        eggName = tostring(\
                            rec.EggName\
                            or rec.EggType\
                            or rec.DisplayName\
                            or \"Field Egg\"\
                        ),\
                        at = t,\
                    }\
    \
                    current[uid] = info\
                    Notifier.fieldCache[uid] = info\
                end\
            end\
    \
            -- Record egg yang baru hilang dari field.\
            -- Berguna apabila UID inventory berubah atau inventory update agak terlambat.\
            for uid, oldInfo in pairs(Notifier.previousField) do\
                if not current[uid] then\
                    local vanished = copyShallow(oldInfo)\
                    vanished.at = t\
                    Notifier.vanishedField[uid] = vanished\
                end\
            end\
    \
            Notifier.previousField = current\
    \
            for uid, info in pairs(Notifier.vanishedField) do\
                if t - (info.at or 0) > 20 then\
                    Notifier.vanishedField[uid] = nil\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        local function pollRiftClaimState()\
            local t = now()\
            local armed = false\
    \
            -- Normal sacrifice flow keeps pendingRewardSince populated through\
            -- the reveal/claim lifecycle.\
            if EventRuntime\
                and EventRuntime.pendingRewardSince ~= nil\
            then\
                armed = true\
            end\
    \
            -- Recovery-after-reload path can have PendingReward=true while\
            -- pendingRewardSince is nil. askRiftState() already stores the latest\
            -- authoritative state in EventRuntime.lastRiftState.\
            local state = EventRuntime and EventRuntime.lastRiftState\
            if type(state) == \"table\" and state.PendingReward == true then\
                armed = true\
            end\
    \
            if armed then\
                -- One Rift claim yields one newly-owned egg. Keep a grace window so\
                -- Save.EggInventory replication can arrive just after PendingReward clears.\
                Notifier.riftClaimArmedUntil = math.max(\
                    Notifier.riftClaimArmedUntil or 0,\
                    t + 15\
                )\
            end\
        end\
    \
        local function pollInventory()\
            local save = getSave()\
            local inventory = save and save.EggInventory\
    \
            if type(inventory) ~= \"table\" then\
                return\
            end\
    \
            local t = now()\
    \
            if not Notifier.initializedInventory then\
                for uid, _ in pairs(inventory) do\
                    Notifier.knownInventory[tostring(uid)] = true\
                end\
    \
                Notifier.initializedInventory = true\
                return\
            end\
    \
            for uid, eggData in pairs(inventory) do\
                local uidStr = tostring(uid)\
    \
                if not Notifier.knownInventory[uidStr] then\
                    Notifier.knownInventory[uidStr] = true\
    \
                    local record = copyShallow(eggData)\
                    local fieldInfo =\
                        Notifier.fieldCache[uidStr]\
                        or Notifier.vanishedField[uidStr]\
    \
                    local isRiftReward =\
                        not fieldInfo\
                        and t <= (Notifier.riftClaimArmedUntil or 0)\
                        and Notifier.riftRewardUid == nil\
    \
                    Notifier.recentNewEggs[uidStr] = {\
                        uid = uidStr,\
                        record = record,\
                        field = fieldInfo and copyShallow(fieldInfo) or nil,\
                        at = t,\
                        isRiftReward = isRiftReward == true,\
                    }\
    \
                    if isRiftReward then\
                        -- Consume this claim window after exactly one new reward UID.\
                        Notifier.riftRewardUid = uidStr\
                        Notifier.riftClaimArmedUntil = 0\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            for uid, info in pairs(Notifier.recentNewEggs) do\
                if t - (info.at or 0) > 30 then\
                    Notifier.recentNewEggs[uid] = nil\
                end\
            end\
        end\
    \
        local function getBestVanishedField(category, maxAge)\
            local best = nil\
            local wanted = tostring(category or \"\"):lower()\
            local t = now()\
    \
            for _, info in pairs(Notifier.vanishedField) do\
                local age = t - (info.at or 0)\
                if age <= (maxAge or 10) then\
                    local sameCategory =\
                        wanted ~= \"\"\
                        and tostring(info.category or \"\"):lower() == wanted\
    \
                    if not best\
                        or sameCategory\
                        or (info.at or 0) > (best.at or 0)\
                    then\
                        best = info\
    \
                        if sameCategory then\
                            break\
                        end\
                    end\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return best\
        end\
    \
        local function newestInventoryEgg(maxAge)\
            pollInventory()\
    \
            local t = now()\
            local best = nil\
    \
            for uid, info in pairs(Notifier.recentNewEggs) do\
                if not Notifier.sentUids[uid]\
                    and t - (info.at or 0) <= (maxAge or 10)\
                    and (not best or (info.at or 0) > (best.at or 0))\
                then\
                    best = info\
                end\
            end\
    \
            return best\
        end\
    \
        local function buildNotifierTarget(info)\
            if type(info) ~= \"table\" then return nil end\
    \
            local record = type(info.record) == \"table\" and info.record or {}\
            local field = type(info.field) == \"table\" and info.field or nil\
    \
            local category = tostring(\
                record.AssetCategory\
                or record.Category\
                or record.PetName\
                or (field and field.category)\
                or \"\"\
            )\
    \
            if category == \"\" then\
                category = \"Unknown\"\
            end\
    \
            -- Jika exact UID field tidak ketemu, cocokkan egg field yang baru hilang.\
            if not field then\
                field = getBestVanishedField(category, 12)\
            end\
    \
            local petName, rarity = resolvePetInfo(category)\
    \
            -- Income/s pet yang baru didapat.\
            -- record = data Save.EggInventory untuk egg ini.\
            local incomePerSecond, baseIncome, incomeMultiplier =\
                resolvePetIncome(category, record)\
    \
            local eggName = tostring(\
                record.EggName\
                or record.EggType\
                or record.DisplayName\
                or record.Name\
                or (field and field.eggName)\
                or \"Field Egg\"\
            )\
    \
            local area = tostring(\
                record.AreaId\
                or record.Area\
                or record.AreaName\
                or record.SourceArea\
                or record.OriginArea\
                or (field and field.area)\
                or \"Unknown\"\
            )\
    \
            return {\
                uid = tostring(info.uid or \"\"),\
                eggName = eggName,\
                petName = petName,\
                category = category,\
                rarity = rarity,\
                area = area,\
    \
                -- Webhook-only metadata.\
                incomePerSecond = incomePerSecond,\
                baseIncome = baseIncome,\
                incomeMultiplier = incomeMultiplier,\
            }\
        end\
    \
        local function sendWebhook(target)\
            if type(target) ~= \"table\" then\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            if not WEBHOOK_TEST_ALL_RARITIES\
                and not WEBHOOK_RARITY_ALLOWED[tostring(target.rarity)]\
            then\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            if not webhookConfigured() then\
                warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Isi WEBHOOK_URL terlebih dahulu.\")\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            local req = getRequest()\
            if type(req) ~= \"function\" then\
                warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Executor tidak memiliki request/http_request.\")\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            local uid = tostring(target.uid or \"\")\
            if uid ~= \"\" and Notifier.sentUids[uid] then\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            local petImage = wikiPetImage(target.petName)\
            local masked = maskUsername(LP.Name)\
    \
            local embed = {\
                title = \"🥚 You Got An Egg!\",\
                url = WIKI_PETS_PAGE,\
                description = \"**\" .. masked .. \"**\",\
                color = getRarityEmbedColor(target.rarity),\
    \
                fields = {\
                    {\
                        name = \"✨ Informasi Egg\",\
                        value = table.concat({\
                            \"**Egg** • \" .. tostring(target.eggName or \"Field Egg\"),\
                            \"**Pet** • \" .. tostring(target.petName or \"Unknown\"),\
                            \"**Rarity** • \" .. tostring(target.rarity or \"Unknown\"),\
                            \"**Income/s** • +\" .. formatIncome(target.incomePerSecond or 0) .. \"/s\",\
                            \"**Area** • \" .. tostring(target.area or \"Unknown\"),\
                        }, \"\\n\"),\
                        inline = false,\
                    },\
                },\
    \
                footer = {\
                    text = \"D-Hub Flex • \" .. tostring(target.area or \"Unknown\"),\
                },\
    \
                timestamp = os.date(\"!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\"),\
            }\
    \
            if petImage then\
                embed.thumbnail = {\
                    url = petImage,\
                }\
            end\
    \
            local payload = {\
                username = \"D-Hub Flex\",\
                embeds = {embed},\
            }\
    \
            local ok, response = pcall(function()\
                return req({\
                    Url = WEBHOOK_URL,\
                    Method = \"POST\",\
                    Headers = {\
                        [\"Content-Type\"] = \"application/json\",\
                    },\
                    Body = S.HttpService:JSONEncode(payload),\
                })\
            end)\
    \
            if not ok then\
                warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Webhook request error:\", tostring(response))\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            local status = nil\
            if type(response) == \"table\" then\
                status = tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.Status)\
            end\
    \
            if status and (status < 200 or status >= 300) then\
                warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Discord HTTP:\", status)\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            if uid ~= \"\" then\
                Notifier.sentUids[uid] = true\
                Notifier.recentNewEggs[uid] = nil\
            end\
    \
            print(string.format(\
                \"[D-Hub Notifier] SENT | %s | %s | %s | %s\",\
                tostring(target.eggName),\
                tostring(target.petName),\
                tostring(target.rarity),\
                tostring(target.area)\
            ))\
    \
            return true\
        end\
    \
        local function sendRiftRewardWebhook(target)\
            if type(target) ~= \"table\" then\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            if not webhookConfigured() then\
                warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Isi WEBHOOK_URL terlebih dahulu.\")\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            local req = getRequest()\
            if type(req) ~= \"function\" then\
                warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Executor tidak memiliki request/http_request.\")\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            local uid = tostring(target.uid or \"\")\
            if uid ~= \"\" and Notifier.sentUids[uid] then\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            local petImage = wikiPetImage(target.petName)\
            local masked = maskUsername(LP.Name)\
    \
            local embed = {\
                title = \"🌌 The Rift Reward Claimed!\",\
                url = WIKI_PETS_PAGE,\
                description = \"**\" .. masked .. \"**\",\
                color = getRarityEmbedColor(target.rarity),\
    \
                fields = {\
                    {\
                        name = \"🥚 Rift Egg Reward\",\
                        value = table.concat({\
                            \"**Egg** • \" .. tostring(target.eggName or \"Rift Egg\"),\
                            \"**Pet Inside** • \" .. tostring(target.petName or \"Unknown\"),\
                            \"**Rarity** • \" .. tostring(target.rarity or \"Unknown\"),\
                            \"**Income/s** • +\" .. formatIncome(target.incomePerSecond or 0) .. \"/s\",\
                            \"**Source** • The Rift\",\
                        }, \"\\n\"),\
                        inline = false,\
                    },\
                },\
    \
                footer = {\
                    text = \"D-Hub Flex • The Rift\",\
                },\
    \
                timestamp = os.date(\"!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\"),\
            }\
    \
            if petImage then\
                embed.thumbnail = {\
                    url = petImage,\
                }\
            end\
    \
            local payload = {\
                username = \"D-Hub Flex\",\
                embeds = {embed},\
            }\
    \
            local ok, response = pcall(function()\
                return req({\
                    Url = WEBHOOK_URL,\
                    Method = \"POST\",\
                    Headers = {\
                        [\"Content-Type\"] = \"application/json\",\
                    },\
                    Body = S.HttpService:JSONEncode(payload),\
                })\
            end)\
    \
            if not ok then\
                warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Rift webhook request error:\", tostring(response))\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            local status = nil\
            if type(response) == \"table\" then\
                status = tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.Status)\
            end\
    \
            if status and (status < 200 or status >= 300) then\
                warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Rift Discord HTTP:\", status)\
                return false\
            end\
    \
            if uid ~= \"\" then\
                Notifier.sentUids[uid] = true\
                Notifier.recentNewEggs[uid] = nil\
            end\
    \
            Notifier.riftRewardUid = nil\
    \
            print(string.format(\
                \"[D-Hub Notifier] RIFT SENT | Egg=%s | Pet=%s | Rarity=%s | UID=%s\",\
                tostring(target.eggName),\
                tostring(target.petName),\
                tostring(target.rarity),\
                tostring(uid)\
            ))\
    \
            return true\
        end\
    \
        local function processPendingRiftReward()\
            local uid = Notifier.riftRewardUid\
            if not uid or Notifier.sentUids[uid] then\
                return\
            end\
    \
            local info = Notifier.recentNewEggs[uid]\
            if not info or info.isRiftReward ~= true then\
                return\
            end\
    \
            local target = buildNotifierTarget(info)\
            if not target then\
                return\
            end\
    \
            -- Rift rewards do not originate from a field area. Force the source\
            -- label so Discord never shows a stale matched field location.\
            target.area = \"The Rift\"\
    \
            sendRiftRewardWebhook(target)\
        end\
    \
        local function cleanGuiText(text)\
            text = tostring(text or \"\")\
            text = text:gsub(\"<[^>]->\", \"\")\
            text = text:gsub(\"%s+\", \" \")\
            return text:lower()\
        end\
    \
        local function isEggStolenText(text)\
            return cleanGuiText(text):find(\"you stole an egg\", 1, true) ~= nil\
        end\
    \
        local function handleEggNotification()\
            local t = now()\
    \
            -- Satu notif game dapat muncul pada beberapa TextLabel.\
            if t - Notifier.lastGuiTrigger < 1.25 then\
                return\
            end\
    \
            Notifier.lastGuiTrigger = t\
    \
            task.spawn(function()\
                print(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Detected: You stole an EGG!\")\
    \
                local found = nil\
                local started = now()\
    \
                -- Inventory biasanya update sangat dekat dengan GUI notification.\
                while now() - started < 4 do\
                    pollFieldEggs()\
                    pollInventory()\
    \
                    local info = newestInventoryEgg(12)\
                    if info then\
                        found = buildNotifierTarget(info)\
                        break\
                    end\
    \
                    task.wait(0.10)\
                end\
    \
                if found then\
                    sendWebhook(found)\
                else\
                    warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Notif terdeteksi, tapi egg inventory baru belum ditemukan.\")\
                end\
            end)\
        end\
    \
        local function watchGuiNotifications()\
            local playerGui =\
                LP:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"PlayerGui\")\
                or LP:WaitForChild(\"PlayerGui\", 10)\
    \
            if not playerGui then\
                warn(\"[D-Hub Notifier] PlayerGui tidak ditemukan.\")\
                return\
            end\
    \
            local watched = setmetatable({}, {__mode = \"k\"})\
    \
            local function watch(obj)\
                if not obj\
                    or watched[obj]\
                    or not (\
                        obj:IsA(\"TextLabel\")\
                        or obj:IsA(\"TextButton\")\
                        or obj:IsA(\"TextBox\")\
                    )\
                then\
                    return\
                end\
    \
                watched[obj] = true\
    \
                local function inspect()\
                    local ok, value = pcall(function()\
                        return obj.Text\
                    end)\
    \
                    if ok and isEggStolenText(value) then\
                        handleEggNotification()\
                    end\
                end\
    \
                inspect()\
    \
                local conn = obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal(\"Text\"):Connect(inspect)\
                table.insert(ScriptConnections, conn)\
            end\
    \
            for _, obj in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do\
                watch(obj)\
            end\
    \
            local conn = playerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(watch)\
            table.insert(ScriptConnections, conn)\
        end\
    \
        -- -------------------------------------------------------------\
        -- START OBSERVER\
        -- -------------------------------------------------------------\
        task.spawn(function()\
            -- Snapshot awal agar egg yang sudah dimiliki sebelum script jalan\
            -- tidak dianggap hasil steal baru.\
            pollInventory()\
            pollFieldEggs()\
    \
            -- Observer metadata saja; tidak menyentuh movement/farm state.\
            task.spawn(function()\
                while true do\
                    task.wait(0.12)\
    \
                    -- Passive state observation only.\
                    pcall(pollRiftClaimState)\
                    pcall(pollFieldEggs)\
                    pcall(pollInventory)\
                    pcall(processPendingRiftReward)\
                end\
            end)\
    \
            watchGuiNotifications()\
    \
            print(\"[D-Hub Notifier] Standalone webhook observer loaded.\")\
        end)\
    end\
    -- =====================================================================\
    -- END D-HUB WEBHOOK NOTIFIER ADDON\
    -- =====================================================================")()
end
end)
local ok_11, err_12 = pcall(function()
local isLoaded = game:IsLoaded()
local ok_5, err_6 = pcall(function()
local ok_3, err_4 = pcall(function()
local HiddenUI = gethui()
end)
for _, child in ipairs(HiddenUI:GetChildren()) do
end
end)

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local PathfindingService = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
local LocalPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local ok_7, err_8 = pcall(function()
local folder = isfolder("D-HUB")
makefolder("D-HUB")
end)
local ok_9, err_10 = pcall(function()
isfile("D-HUB/StealAnEggV2.3.json")
end)
end)
if not _ok then
    error("D-Hub v5: [string \"loadstring\"]:254: attempt to index nil with 'Connect'")
end
